### [1] [Visual Text Compression as Measure Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06708)
*Lv Tang,Tianyi Zheng,Yang Liu,Bo Li,Xingyu Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对视觉文本压缩（VTC）中token节省无法可靠转化为下游任务收益的问题，提出基于测度传输理论的分析框架，通过推前映射将信息损失分解为精度代价和覆盖代价，并据此设计无需下游标签的路由准则和注视机制，在24个NLP数据集上以70.8%的准确率匹配每数据集最优路径。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的视觉文本压缩（VTC）虽然能大幅减少解码器token数量（3-20倍），但token节省与下游任务性能之间缺乏可预测的对应关系：在某些任务上视觉路径优于文本路径，在某些任务上则完全失效。压缩比本身无法预测这种差异，因此需要一种衡量视觉编码所导致的任务相关信息损失的原则性度量，而不是另一个效率指标。

Method: 将视觉文本压缩（VTC）形式化为测度传输（measure transport）问题：将文本token和视觉token视为经验概率测度，证明ViT补丁编码器诱导一个推前映射（push-forward map），其传输代价分解为两部分——补丁内聚合引起的精度代价（precision cost）和跨补丁碎片化引起的覆盖代价（coverage cost）。两项代价均可通过无需下游标签的探针进行估计。基于此设计两个应用：（1）无需下游标签的路由准则，判断给定输入使用视觉路径还是文本路径；（2）传输信息驱动的注视机制（transport-informed foveation），对高代价区域进行更高分辨率的重新编码。

Result: 在Qwen3-4B模型上对24个NLP数据集进行实验：（1）无需标签的路由准则在17/24个数据集上（70.8%）匹配了每数据集的oracle最优选择；（2）相比纯LLM基线，平均任务得分提升+3.3%，同时平均token数减少-10.3%。

Conclusion: 基于测度传输理论的分析框架能够有效量化视觉文本压缩中的信息损失，并据此设计出无需下游标签即可工作的路由策略和注视机制，在提升任务性能的同时减少token消耗，为VTC的实际部署提供了理论指导和实用工具。

Original Abstract: Visual text compression (VTC) promises efficient long-context processing by rendering text into an image and re-encoding it with a vision-language model, often producing $3$--$20\times$ fewer decoder tokens than subword tokenization. Yet token savings do not translate predictably into downstream utility: on some tasks the visual path matches or exceeds the text path, on others it collapses, and the compression ratio itself does not predict which regime will occur. The missing quantity is therefore not another summary of efficiency, but a principled measure of task-relevant information loss induced by visual encoding. We address this problem by formulating VTC in the language of measure transport. Treating text and visual tokens as empirical probability measures, we show that the ViT patch encoder induces a push-forward map whose transport cost decomposes into a precision cost from within-patch aggregation and a coverage cost from cross-patch fragmentation. Both terms are estimable from downstream-label-free probes. This formulation yields two operational consequences: a downstream-label-free routing criterion that selects whether to use the visual path for a given input or benchmark instance, and a transport-informed foveation mechanism that re-encodes high-cost regions at higher resolution. Across $24$ NLP datasets at Qwen3-4B, our label-free rule matches the per-dataset oracle on $17/24$ datasets ($70.8\%$), and improves the average task score by $+3.3\%$ with $-10.3\%$ average tokens relative to a pure-LLM.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉文本压缩（VTC）通过将文本渲染为图像并用视觉-语言模型重新编码，有望实现高效的长上下文处理，通常产生的解码器token数比子词分词少3-20倍。然而，token节省并不能可预测地转化为下游实用性：在某些任务上，视觉路径与文本路径性能相当甚至更优，而在另一些任务上则完全失效，且压缩比本身无法预测出现哪种情况。因此，缺失的并非另一个效率指标，而是一种衡量视觉编码所导致的任务相关信息损失的原则性度量。我们通过将VTC形式化为测度传输的语言来解决这一问题。将文本和视觉token视为经验概率测度，我们证明ViT补丁编码器诱导一个推前映射，其传输代价分解为补丁内聚合引起的精度代价和跨补丁碎片化引起的覆盖代价。两项代价均可通过无需下游标签的探针进行估计。这一形式化产生两个操作性后果：一个无需下游标签的路由准则，用于选择对给定输入或基准实例使用视觉路径还是文本路径；以及一个传输信息驱动的注视机制，对高代价区域以更高分辨率重新编码。在Qwen3-4B模型上的24个NLP数据集中，我们的无标签规则在17/24个数据集（70.8%）上匹配了每数据集的oracle最优选择，并且相比纯LLM基线，平均任务得分提升+3.3%，同时平均token数减少-10.3%。

</details>


### [2] [HumanNet: Scaling Human-centric Video Learning to One Million Hours](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06747)
*Yufan Deng,Daquan Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HumanNet是一个百万小时级的人类中心视频数据集，通过系统化的数据策展范式将非结构化互联网视频转化为可扩展的具身学习基础，实验表明第一人称人类视频可有效替代机器人数据进行视觉-语言-动作模型训练。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 具身智能的发展受限于缺乏大规模、多样化且富含标注的人类活动数据，现有视觉和语言领域已通过互联网语料实现规模化，但物理交互学习仍缺乏类似的数据基础设施。

Method: 构建了包含100万小时人类中心视频的HumanNet数据集，覆盖第一人称和第三人称视角，包含细粒度活动、人-物交互、工具使用和长周期行为；提供交互为中心的标注（字幕、运动描述、手部和身体信号）；引入系统化数据策展范式，将人类中心过滤、时间结构、视角多样性和标注丰富性作为一等设计原则。

Result: 在固定验证集下，使用HumanNet中的1000小时第一人称视频对Qwen VLM进行持续训练，效果优于使用Magic Cobot中100小时真实机器人数据训练的模型，表明第一人称人类视频可成为机器人数据的可扩展且经济有效的替代方案。

Conclusion: 人类中心视频可以作为训练具身基础模型的可扩展数据来源，而非仅依赖机器人专用数据，为具身智能的规模化发展提供了新路径。

Original Abstract: Progress in embodied intelligence increasingly depends on scalable data infrastructure. While vision and language have scaled with internet corpora, learning physical interaction remains constrained by the lack of large, diverse, and richly annotated human activity data. We present HumanNet, a one-million-hour human-centric video corpus that captures how humans interact with the physical world at scale. HumanNet spans both first-person and third-person perspectives and covers fine-grained activities, human-object interactions, tool use, and long-horizon behaviors across diverse real-world environments. Beyond raw video, the dataset provides interaction-centric annotations, including captions, motion descriptions, and hand and body-related signals, enabling motion-aware and interaction-aware learning. Beyond scale, HumanNet introduces a systematic data curation paradigm for embodied learning, where human-centric filtering, temporal structuring, viewpoint diversity, and annotation enrichment are treated as first-class design principles. This design transforms unstructured internet video into a scalable substrate for representation learning, activity understanding, motion generation, and human-to-robot transfer. We conduct a first-step validation on the value of this design through controlled vision-language-action ablation: under a fixed set of validation data, continued training from the Qwen VLM model with 1000 hours of egocentric video drawn from HumanNet surpasses the continued training with 100 hours of real-robot data from Magic Cobot, indicating that egocentric human video could be a scalable and cost-effective substitute for robot data. By building this project, we aim to explore the opportunity to scale embodied foundation models using human-centric videos, rather than relying solely on robot-specific data.

中文摘要翻译: 具身智能的进步越来越依赖于可扩展的数据基础设施。虽然视觉和语言领域已通过互联网语料实现了规模化，但物理交互学习仍受限于缺乏大规模、多样化且富含标注的人类活动数据。我们提出HumanNet，一个百万小时级的人类中心视频语料库，大规模捕捉人类如何与物理世界进行交互。HumanNet涵盖第一人称和第三人称视角，包含细粒度活动、人-物交互、工具使用以及在多样真实环境中的长周期行为。除了原始视频外，该数据集还提供以交互为中心的标注，包括字幕、运动描述以及手部和身体相关信号，支持运动感知和交互感知学习。超越规模本身，HumanNet为具身学习引入了一种系统化的数据策展范式，将人类中心过滤、时间结构组织、视角多样性和标注丰富性作为一等设计原则。这一设计将非结构化互联网视频转化为表示学习、活动理解、运动生成以及人-机迁移的可扩展基础。我们通过受控的视觉-语言-动作消融实验对该设计的价值进行了初步验证：在固定验证集下，使用来自HumanNet的1000小时第一人称视频对Qwen视觉语言模型进行持续训练，其效果优于使用Magic Cobot中100小时真实机器人数据进行的持续训练，这表明第一人称人类视频可以成为机器人数据的可扩展且经济有效的替代方案。通过建设该项目，我们旨在探索利用人类中心视频而非仅依赖机器人特定数据来规模化具身基础模型的可能性。

</details>


### [3] [R$^3$L: Reasoning 3D Layouts from Relative Spatial Relations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06758)
*Zhifeng Gu,Yuqi Wang,Bing Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出R³L框架，通过不变空间分解、一致空间想象和支持性空间优化，提升3D布局生成中多跳相对空间推理的可靠性与一致性，有效缓解参考系变换导致的语义和度量漂移。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 多跳相对空间推理需要反复进行参考系变换，导致推断出的空间关系存在累积误差，引发语义和度量漂移，而现有方法仅依赖后处理启发式方法处理这些不可靠的关系。

Method: 1) 不变空间分解（Invariant Spatial Decomposition）：解耦耦合的关系链；2) 一致空间想象（Consistent Spatial Imagination）：通过“想象-修正”循环促进自一致性；3) 支持性空间优化（Supportive Spatial Optimization）：通过全局到局部的坐标重参数化简化位姿优化。

Result: 在多种场景类型和指令下的广泛实验表明，R³L能生成物理上更可行、语义上更一致的布局；分析表明解决参考系引发的不一致性对可靠的多跳相对空间推理至关重要。

Conclusion: 解决参考系变换导致的不一致性是提升多跳相对空间推理可靠性的关键，R³L框架在该方向上迈出了重要一步。

Original Abstract: Relative spatial relations provide a compact representation of spatial structure and are fundamental to relative spatial reasoning in 3D layout generation. Recent works leverage Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) to infer such relations, but the inferred relations are often unreliable and are typically handled with post-hoc heuristics. In this paper, we propose R$^3$L, a general framework that improves the reliability and consistency of relative spatial reasoning for 3D layout generation. Our key motivation is that multi-hop reasoning requires repeated reference-frame transformations, which accumulate errors in inferred relations and lead to semantic and metric drift. To mitigate this, we propose invariant spatial decomposition to break coupled relation chains, and consistent spatial imagination to promote self-consistency through an imagine-and-revise loop. We further introduce supportive spatial optimization to ease pose optimization via global-to-local coordinate re-parameterization. Extensive experiments across diverse scene types and instructions demonstrate that R$^3$L produces more physically feasible and semantically consistent layouts. Notably, our analysis shows that resolving frame-induced inconsistencies is crucial for reliable multi-hop relative spatial reasoning. The code is available at https://github.com/Neal2020GitHub/R3L.

中文摘要翻译: 相对空间关系提供了空间结构的紧凑表示，是3D布局生成中进行相对空间推理的基础。近期工作利用多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）推断此类关系，但推断出的关系往往不可靠，通常只能借助后处理启发式方法处理。本文提出R³L，一个通用框架，旨在提升3D布局生成中相对空间推理的可靠性和一致性。我们的核心动机在于：多跳推理需要反复进行参考系变换，这会在推断的关系中累积误差，导致语义和度量漂移。为缓解这一问题，我们提出不变空间分解以解耦耦合的关系链，以及一致空间想象，通过“想象-修正”循环促进自一致性。此外，我们引入支持性空间优化，通过全局到局部的坐标重参数化简化位姿优化。在多种场景类型和指令下的广泛实验表明，R³L能够生成物理上更可行、语义上更一致的布局。值得关注的是，我们的分析表明，解决参考系引发的不一致性是实现可靠的多跳相对空间推理的关键。代码已开源：https://github.com/Neal2020GitHub/R3L。

</details>


### [4] [LookWhen? Fast Video Recognition by Learning When, Where, and What to Compute](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06809)
*Ali Salamatian,Anthony Fuller,Pritam Sarkar,James R. Green,Leonid Sigal,Evan Shelhamer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LookWhen提出了一种选择器-提取器框架，通过学习何时、何处以及计算什么来减少视频处理中的冗余，仅处理top-K令牌即可近似全视频表示，在多个基准上实现了更好的精度-计算量权衡。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: Transformer处理视频令牌具有昂贵的超线性计算成本，而视频中充满冗余信息，因此需要更高效的方法来避免处理所有令牌。

Method: 提出选择器-提取器框架：浅层选择器在缩小的视频上快速对所有时空令牌评分，深层提取器仅处理top-K选中的令牌来逼近全视频表示。选择预训练通过最近邻距离对令牌独特性排序，提取预训练通过蒸馏视频教师和图像教师（归一化帧级表示以学习视频中的变化）来实现。

Result: 在Kinetics-400、SSv2、Epic-Kitchens、Diving48、Jester、Charades六个数据集上，LookWhen在12个实验设置中的9个上实现了Pareto主导的精度-FLOPs权衡，在精度-吞吐量上比InternVideo2-B快6.7倍。

Conclusion: LookWhen通过选择性计算有效减少了视频处理冗余，学习到通用高效的表征，在精度和计算效率之间取得了更优的平衡。

Original Abstract: Transformers dominate video recognition. They split videos into tokens, and processing them has expensive superlinear computational cost. Yet videos are filled with redundancy, so we can question the need for this expense. We introduce LookWhen, a selector-extractor framework that factorizes video recognition into learning when, where, and what to compute. Our shallow selector gets a scaled-down video and quickly scores all tokens across space-time, while our deep extractor gets the top-K selected tokens to approximate full-video representations without actually processing all the tokens. A key challenge is defining effective supervision for selection and extraction. For selection pre-training, we introduce a score on representations that ranks tokens by uniqueness using a simple nearest-neighbor distance. For extraction pre-training, we distill both a video teacher and an image teacher, for which we normalize its frame-wise representations to learn what changes within videos. Through these strategies, our selector-extractor learns general and efficient representations for feature extraction or fine-tuning to a task. Through experiments on Kinetics-400, SSv2, Epic-Kitchens, Diving48, Jester, and Charades, we show that LookWhen achieves a better accuracy-computation trade-off than efficient models and upgraded baselines of similar size. LookWhen Pareto-dominates in accuracy-FLOPs on 9 of 12 cases (6 tasks x 2 settings) and roughly matches on 3. In accuracy-throughput, measuring time in practice, LookWhen is more efficient still at 6.7x faster than InternVideo2-B at equal accuracy.

中文摘要翻译: Transformer主导着视频识别领域。它们将视频分割成令牌，处理这些令牌需要昂贵的超线性计算成本。然而视频中充满了冗余，因此我们可以质疑这种开销的必要性。我们引入了LookWhen，一个选择器-提取器框架，将视频识别分解为学习何时、何处以及计算什么。我们的浅层选择器获取缩小的视频并快速对所有时空令牌评分，而我们的深层提取器获取top-K选中的令牌来逼近全视频表示，而无需实际处理所有令牌。一个关键挑战是为选择和提取定义有效的监督信号。对于选择预训练，我们引入了一个基于表征的评分，通过简单的最近邻距离按独特性对令牌排序。对于提取预训练，我们蒸馏了视频教师和图像教师，为此我们归一化了其帧级表征以学习视频内部的变化。通过这些策略，我们的选择器-提取器学习到通用高效的表示，可用于特征提取或针对特定任务的微调。通过在Kinetics-400、SSv2、Epic-Kitchens、Diving48、Jester和Charades上的实验，我们表明LookWhen在精度-计算量权衡上优于同等规模的高效模型和改进基线。LookWhen在12个实验案例（6个任务×2个设置）中的9个上实现了Pareto主导的精度-FLOPs权衡，并在3个案例上大致持平。在精度-吞吐量（实际测量时间）方面，LookWhen效率更高，在相同精度下比InternVideo2-B快6.7倍。

</details>


### [5] [Knowledge Transfer Scaling Laws for 3D Medical Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06859)
*Ho Hin Lee,Dongna Du,Chu Wang,Yuankai Huo,Shi Gu,James C. Gee,Yifan Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现不同医学影像模态在预训练时存在非对称的知识迁移（如CT可大幅提升MRI性能，反之则弱），并遵循可预测的幂律缩放规律。基于此，提出将数据分配建模为缩放律优化问题，得到枢纽-岛屿结构的数据分配策略，在3D医学影像任务上优于比例采样高达58%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 当前视觉基础模型向3D医学影像扩展时，需要混合CT、MRI、PET等异质模态进行统一预训练，但现有模态混合策略多基于启发式方法，缺乏理论指导和最优分配原则。

Method: 首先观察并验证不同医学影像模态在预训练中的缩放速率差异及非对称知识迁移现象，发现MAE重建损失和跨域迁移均服从域特定的幂律规律。据此将数据分配建模为缩放律优化问题，求解得到枢纽-岛屿结构的最优分配方案：高迁移性模态作为枢纽获得更多分配，孤立模态作为岛屿需直接投入。

Result: 迁移感知的数据分配策略相比数据比例采样提升高达58%，在未知预算下泛化性能优异（r=0.989）；在疾病分类和器官/病变分割任务上验证了该策略能提供更强的预训练表征。

Conclusion: 通过将数据分配形式化为缩放律优化问题，可以系统性地确定医学影像多模态预训练中的最优数据配比，为构建更高效的3D医学影像基础模型提供了理论驱动的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Vision foundation models are increasingly moving beyond 2D to volumetric domains such as 3D medical imaging, where unified pretraining across different imaging modalities (i.e. CT, MRI, and PET) could provide foundational models for diverse clinical tasks. However, training such models requires mixing heterogeneous imaging domains, and current mixture strategies remain largely heuristic. In this work, we observe that different medical imaging domains scale at variable rates during pretraining, and knowledge transfer between domains is strongly asymmetric: training on one domain can substantially improve another, but the reverse may be much weaker. Interestingly, both MAE reconstruction loss and cross-domain transfer follow predictable power-law trends with domain-specific behaviors. Motivated by these findings, we formulate data allocation as a scaling-law optimization problem. The derived allocations reveal an interpretable hub-and-island structure: highly transferable domains emerge as hubs that benefit many others and deserve strategic allocation, while isolated domains act as islands requiring direct investment. Empirically, transfer-aware allocation outperforms data-proportional sampling by up to 58% and generalizes well to unseen budgets with r=0.989. Downstream validation on disease classification and organ/lesion segmentation further confirms that the derived transfer-aware mixtures provide stronger pretrained representations for clinical 3D medical imaging tasks.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉基础模型正日益从二维领域扩展到三维医学影像等体积域，其中跨不同成像模态（如CT、MRI和PET）的统一预训练可为多样化的临床任务提供基础模型。然而，训练此类模型需要混合异质成像域，当前的混合策略在很大程度上仍基于启发式方法。在本文中，我们观察到不同的医学影像域在预训练期间以不同的速率进行缩放，且域间的知识迁移存在强烈的不对称性：在一个域上训练可以显著提升另一个域的性能，但反向迁移可能弱得多。有趣的是，MAE重建损失和跨域迁移均遵循具有域特定行为的可预测幂律趋势。基于这些发现，我们将数据分配形式化为一个缩放律优化问题。由此得出的分配揭示了一种可解释的枢纽-岛屿结构：高迁移性的域成为惠及许多其他域的枢纽，需要战略性分配；而孤立域则作为岛屿，需要直接投入。实验表明，迁移感知的分配策略在性能上优于数据比例采样高达58%，并且在未知预算下具有良好的泛化能力（r=0.989）。在疾病分类和器官/病变分割任务上的下游验证进一步证实，所推导的迁移感知混合策略能为临床三维医学成像任务提供更强的预训练表征。

</details>


### [6] [AdpSplit: Error-Driven Adaptive Splitting for Faster Geometry Discovery in 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06876)
*Yongjae Lee,Jingxing Li,Abhay Kumar Yadav,Rama Chellappa,Deliang Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AdpSplit是一种基于误差驱动的自适应分裂算子，替代3DGS中固定数量的随机分裂，通过根据L1像素误差区域统计决定子节点数量和初始化参数，减少了密集化迭代次数，在多个数据集上加速训练9.2%-22.3%，并保持渲染质量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 原始3DGS中，二进制分裂算子需要进行多次密集化轮次才能揭示精细细节，成为高效训练流程的瓶颈，导致训练时间过长。

Method: AdpSplit提出误差驱动的自适应分裂算子：根据L1像素误差区域统计信息动态决定分裂子节点的数量，并基于误差区域统计初始化子节点参数，从而减少密集化迭代次数。

Result: 在MipNeRF360、Deep-Blending和Tanks&Temples数据集上，AdpSplit作为即插即用替代方案，将多个加速3DGS流程的训练时间减少9.2%-22.3%。与FastGS结合时，在MipNeRF360上达到全调度PSNR的同时训练时间减少16.4%，相对于原始3DGS实现12.6倍加速。

Conclusion: AdpSplit通过自适应分裂策略有效减少了训练时间，同时保持了全调度训练的渲染质量，为3DGS的高效训练提供了简单有效的改进方案。

Original Abstract: Adaptive density control in 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) repeatedly grows the Gaussian population through fixed-cardinality random splitting to discover useful scene structure. However, in vanilla 3DGS, its binary split operator requires many densification rounds to expose fine details, making it a bottleneck for efficient training schedules with fewer iterations. We introduce AdpSplit, an error-driven adaptive split operator that determines the number of split children and initializes the child parameters from L1-pixel-error region statistics, enabling fewer densification iterations, thus reduced training time, while preserving the rendering quality of full-schedule training. Across the MipNeRF360, Deep-Blending, and Tanks&Temples datasets, AdpSplit reduces the training time of multiple accelerated 3DGS pipelines by 9.2%-22.3% as a simple drop-in replacement for the standard split operator. With FastGS, AdpSplit matches the full-schedule PSNR on MipNeRF360 while reducing training time by 16.4%, corresponding to a 12.6x acceleration over vanilla 3DGS.

中文摘要翻译: 3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）中的自适应密度控制通过固定数量的随机分裂反复增加高斯分布数量，以发现有用的场景结构。然而，在原始3DGS中，其二进制分裂算子需要多次密集化轮次才能揭示精细细节，这成为迭代次数更少的高效训练流程的瓶颈。我们提出AdpSplit，一种基于误差驱动的自适应分裂算子，该算子根据L1像素误差区域统计信息确定分裂子节点的数量并初始化子节点参数，从而减少密集化迭代次数，进而降低训练时间，同时保持全调度训练的渲染质量。在MipNeRF360、Deep-Blending和Tanks&Temples数据集上，AdpSplit作为标准分裂算子的简单即插即用替代方案，将多个加速3DGS流程的训练时间减少了9.2%-22.3%。结合FastGS，AdpSplit在MipNeRF360上达到了全调度的PSNR，同时训练时间减少了16.4%，相当于相比原始3DGS实现了12.6倍加速。

</details>


### [7] [TriDE: Triangle-Consistent Translation Directions for Global Camera Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06889)
*Francisco Chen,Yiran Wang,Yunpeng Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TriDE，利用相机-三角形一致性作为高阶验证信号，通过消息传递机制在不依赖全局非线性优化的前提下联合估计全局相机方向，显著提升了方向估计精度和下游相机定位效果。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法独立处理每对图像的相对方向估计，导致局部合理但全局不一致的问题，且全局非线性优化方法计算成本高且对初始化敏感。

Method: 提出TriDE，利用相机-三角形一致性作为高阶验证信号，通过消息传递机制在方向及其关联的加权三角形之间传播信息，以联合优化全局方向估计。

Result: 在真实图像图上，TriDE显著提升了方向估计精度，并得到更优的下游相机位置；同时建立了在随机损坏模型下精确恢复的强相变边界。

Conclusion: TriDE有效地连接了局部成对估计与全局相机位姿几何，为全局运动恢复结构中的相机定位提供了一种实用且高效的联合方向估计方法。

Original Abstract: Pairwise translation directions are a key input to camera location estimation in global structure-from-motion. Existing estimators usually process each image pair independently, producing directions that may be locally plausible but inconsistent with the other relative directions in the viewing graph. To jointly estimate the direction, we propose TriDE, which exploits camera-triangle consistency as an efficient higher-order verification signal. Instead of solving a costly global nonlinear optimization problem that is sensitive to initialization, TriDE refines unreliable pairwise directions through message passing between directions and their incident weighted triangles. This information propagation strategy enables us to establish a strong phase-transition bound for exact recovery under a realistic random corruption model. Experiments on real image graphs show that TriDE improves direction accuracy by a large margin and yields better downstream camera locations, providing a practical link between local pairwise estimation and global camera pose geometry.

中文摘要翻译: 成对平移方向是全局运动恢复结构中相机位置估计的关键输入。现有估计器通常独立处理每对图像，生成的方向虽然在局部看似合理，但在视图图中与其他相对方向不一致。为了联合估计方向，我们提出TriDE，利用相机-三角形一致性作为高效的高阶验证信号。TriDE不求解对初始化敏感且成本高昂的全局非线性优化问题，而是通过方向与其关联的加权三角形之间的消息传递来优化不可靠的成对方向。这种信息传播策略使我们能够在真实的随机损坏模型下为精确恢复建立强相变边界。在真实图像图上的实验表明，TriDE大幅提升了方向估计精度，并得到更优的下游相机位置，为局部成对估计与全局相机位姿几何之间提供了实用桥梁。

</details>


### [8] [Towards Fairness under Label Bias in Image Segmentation: Impact, Measurement and Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06891)
*Aditya Parikh,Stella Frank,Sneha Das,Aasa Feragen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种数据中心的标注偏差检测与缓解方法，将置信学习（Confident Learning）适配到图像分割任务，无需干净无偏的真实标注即可检测训练数据中的标签偏差，并通过特征空间子群可分性进行偏差缓解，实现跨子群的公平性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 图像分割中的标签偏差（label bias）问题尚未被充分探索，因为检测这类偏差通常需要干净无偏的标注，而这在实际中很难获得。

Method: 将置信学习（Confident Learning）从分类任务适配到图像分割任务，通过比较训练标签与模型的置信预测，隔离出方向性错误以量化偏差的存在性和性质；进一步利用编码器特征空间中子群可分性的伪影（artifact）进行偏差缓解。

Result: 在合成偏差和真实偏差的三种数据集上，该方法能够在没有干净标签的情况下可靠地检测和缓解偏差，实现跨实验条件的公平性能表现。

Conclusion: 提出了一种无需干净无偏标注即可检测和缓解分割任务中标签偏差的框架，有效提升了模型在不同子群上的公平性。

Original Abstract: Labeled datasets reflect the biases of their annotation pipelines, which sometimes introduce label bias: group-conditional label errors that cause systematic performance disparities across demographic subgroups. Label bias in image segmentation remains underexplored, as even detecting it typically requires clean, unbiased annotations, which are not readily available. We present a data-centric adaptation of Confident Learning to segmentation, allowing detection of label bias directly in the training data without a clean, unbiased ground truth. By comparing the provided training labels to the model's confident predictions, we isolate directional errors that quantify the presence and nature of bias, where standard overlap metrics like Dice fail. We further show that label bias influences subgroup separability in the encoder's feature space, an artifact we leverage for bias mitigation rather than suppressing it. We evaluate three datasets, spanning from synthetic to real-life bias, showing how our framework reliably detects and mitigates bias without access to clean labels, achieving equitable performance across experimental conditions.

中文摘要翻译: 带标签的数据集反映了其标注流程中的偏差，这些偏差有时会引入标签偏差（label bias）：即分组条件性的标签错误，导致不同人口统计学子群之间出现系统性的性能差异。图像分割中的标签偏差尚未被充分探索，因为即使是检测它通常也需要干净无偏的标注，而这并不容易获得。我们提出了一种数据中心（data-centric）的置信学习（Confident Learning）方法，将其适配到分割任务，使得无需干净无偏的真实标注即可直接在训练数据中检测标签偏差。通过将提供的训练标签与模型的置信预测进行比较，我们隔离出方向性错误，从而量化偏差的存在性和性质——而标准重叠度量（如Dice）无法做到这一点。我们进一步表明，标签偏差会影响编码器特征空间中子群的可分性，我们利用这一伪影（artifact）进行偏差缓解，而非抑制它。我们在三个数据集上进行了评估，涵盖了从合成偏差到真实偏差的场景，展示了我们的框架如何在无需访问干净标签的情况下可靠地检测和缓解偏差，实现跨实验条件的公平性能。

</details>


### [9] [Not All Tokens Need 40 Steps: Heterogeneous Step Allocation in Diffusion Transformers for Efficient Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06892)
*Ernie Chu,Vishal M. Patel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出异构步长分配（HSA），一种无需训练的视频扩散Transformer推理加速算法，根据令牌的运动速度动态分配不同的去噪步数，并通过KV缓存同步机制和缓存欧拉更新解决序列长度不匹配问题，在Wan-2和LTX-2模型上实现了优于先前最先进方法的加速效果。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有扩散Transformer（DiT）在视频生成中对所有时空令牌采用统一的去噪步数，造成巨大计算浪费，而人类视觉系统会忽略大量冗余运动信息，受此启发提出更高效的计算分配方法。

Method: 提出异构步长分配（HSA）算法：1）根据令牌的速度动态为其分配不同的去噪步数；2）引入KV缓存同步机制，使活跃令牌能关注完整序列而完全跳过非活跃令牌；3）推导缓存欧拉更新，通过单次操作推进跳过令牌的隐状态，无需额外模型评估。

Result: 在Wan-2和LTX-2模型的文本到视频（T2V）和图像到视频（I2V）任务上，HSA显著优于先前最先进的缓存方法和基线Flow Matching，尤其在激进加速场景（50%和25%运行时）下优势显著，无需昂贵的离线分析即可实现更优的质量-运行时帕累托前沿。

Conclusion: HSA在严格计算预算下仍能稳健保持视频的结构完整性和生成质量，是一种高效且实用的视频生成推理加速方案。

Original Abstract: Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) have achieved state-of-the-art video generation quality, but they incur immense computational cost because standard inference applies the same number of denoising steps uniformly to every token in the sequence. It is well known that human vision ignores vast amounts of redundant motion. Why, then, do our densest models treat every spatiotemporal token with equal priority? In this paper, we introduce Heterogeneous Step Allocation (HSA), a training-free inference algorithm that assigns varying step budgets to different spatiotemporal tokens based on their velocity dynamics. To resolve the resulting sequence-length mismatch without sacrificing global context, HSA introduces a KV-cache synchronization mechanism that allows active tokens to attend to the full sequence while entirely bypassing inactive tokens. Furthermore, we derive a cached Euler update that advances the latent states of skipped tokens in a single operation without additional model evaluations. We evaluate HSA on the Wan-2 and LTX-2 models for both text-to-video (T2V) and image-to-video (I2V) generation. Our results demonstrate that HSA significantly outperforms previous state-of-the-art caching methods and the vanilla Flow Matching baseline, especially at aggressive acceleration regimes (e.g., 50% and 25% runtimes). Crucially, HSA achieves a superior quality-runtime Pareto frontier without the need for expensive offline profiling, robustly preserving structural integrity and generation quality even under tight computational budgets.
  Project page: https://ernestchu.github.io/hsa

中文摘要翻译: 扩散变换器（DiTs）在视频生成质量上已达到最先进水平，但由于标准推断对所有序列令牌统一采用相同数量的去噪步长，导致了巨大的计算开销。众所周知，人类视觉会忽略大量冗余运动。那么，为什么我们最密集的模型要对每个时空令牌给予同等的优先级呢？本文提出异构步长分配（HSA），一种无需训练的推理算法，该算法根据令牌的速度动态为不同的时空令牌分配不同的步长预算。为了解决由此产生的序列长度不匹配问题，同时不牺牲全局上下文，HSA引入了一种KV缓存同步机制，使得活跃令牌能够关注完整序列，同时完全跳过非活跃令牌。此外，我们推导了一种缓存欧拉更新方法，该方法可以在无需额外模型评估的情况下，通过单次操作推进被跳过令牌的隐状态。我们在Wan-2和LTX-2模型上评估了HSA在文本到视频（T2V）和图像到视频（I2V）生成任务中的表现。结果表明，HSA显著优于先前最先进的缓存方法和基线流匹配（Flow Matching）方法，尤其是在激进加速场景（例如50%和25%运行时）下。关键的是，HSA无需昂贵的离线分析即可实现更优的质量-运行时帕累托前沿，即使在严格的计算预算下也能稳健地保持结构完整性和生成质量。

</details>


### [10] [Advancing Reliable Synthetic Video Detection: Insights from the SAFE Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06912)
*Kirill Trapeznikov,Gabriel Mancino-Ball,Jonathan Li,Paul Cummer,Jai Aslam,Danial Samadi Vahdati,Tai Nguyen,Matthew C. Stamm,Peter Bautista,Michael Davinroy,Laura Cassani,Jill Crisman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了ICCV 2025 APAI研讨会上举办的SAFE合成视频检测挑战赛。该竞赛旨在评估和推动生成视频检测技术的发展，包含两个任务：检测多种生成模型产生的合成视频，以及检测经过常见后处理操作的合成视频。竞赛收集了13种现代高质量合成视频模型生成的内容，与来自21个真实视频源匹配，总计20小时/6000个样本，12支队伍提交了超过600份作品。研究结果显示了跨生成器泛化方面的进展，但也揭示了后处理伪影带来的持续脆弱性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 生成视频技术的快速发展使得可靠检测和表征合成媒体的方法变得迫切需要，因此组织了SAFE合成视频检测挑战赛来推动该领域的发展。

Method: 在Hugging Face平台上举办竞赛，设置两个主要任务：(1) 检测多种先进模型生成的合成视频；(2) 检测经过调整大小、重新压缩、运动模糊等常见后处理操作的合成内容。数据集包含13种现代高质量合成视频模型生成的内容，与来自21个多样化真实视频源的内容匹配，共计20小时/6000个视频样本。采用完全盲评估条件，为期90天。

Result: 12支队伍在90天内提交了超过600份作品；跨生成器泛化方面取得了可衡量的进展；但方法对后处理伪影仍存在持续脆弱性。

Conclusion: 当代合成视频检测方法在跨生成器泛化方面取得了一定进展，但对后处理操作（如调整大小、重新压缩、运动模糊等）带来的伪影仍存在鲁棒性不足的问题。

Original Abstract: The proliferation of generative video technologies has intensified the need for reliable methods to detect and characterize synthetic media. To address this challenge, we organized the \href{https://safe-video-2025.dsri.org}{SAFE: Synthetic Video Detection Challenge}, co-located with the \textit{Authenticity and Provenance in the Age of Generative AI (APAI) Workshop }at ICCV 2025. The competition invited participants to develop and evaluate algorithms capable of distinguishing real from synthetic videos under fully blind evaluation conditions with over 600 submissions from 12 teams over a 90 day span. Hosted on the Hugging Face platform, the challenge comprised two primary tasks: (1) detection of synthetic video content generated by diverse state-of-the-art models, and (2) detection of synthetic content following common post-processing operations such as resizing, re-compression, motion blur and others. The challenge data consisted of 13 modern high quality synthetic video models with generated content matched to real videos from 21 diverse and challenge sources, all adding up to 20 hours of 6,000 video samples. This paper describes the challenge design, dataset construction, evaluation methodology, and outcomes, offering insights into the generalization and robustness of contemporary synthetic video detection methods. Our findings highlight measurable progress in cross-generator generalization but also persistent vulnerabilities to post-processing artifacts. https://safe-video-2025.dsri.org

中文摘要翻译: 生成视频技术的广泛普及加剧了对可靠方法来检测和表征合成媒体的需求。为应对这一挑战，我们组织了SAFE合成视频检测挑战赛，该赛事与ICCV 2025上的"生成式AI时代的真实性和溯源"研讨会共同举办。该竞赛邀请参与者在完全盲评估条件下开发和评估能够区分真实视频与合成视频的算法，历时90天，来自12支队伍提交了超过600份作品。竞赛托管在Hugging Face平台上，包含两个主要任务：(1) 检测由多种先进模型生成的合成视频内容，(2) 检测经过常见后处理操作（如调整大小、重新压缩、运动模糊等）后的合成内容。挑战数据由13种现代高质量合成视频模型生成的内容组成，与来自21个多样化真实视频源的内容相匹配，总计20小时、6000个视频样本。本文描述了挑战赛的设计、数据集构建、评估方法和结果，为当代合成视频检测方法的泛化能力和鲁棒性提供了深入见解。我们的研究结果显示了跨生成器泛化方面的可衡量进展，但也揭示了对后处理伪影的持续脆弱性。

</details>


### [11] [A$^2$RD: Agentic Autoregressive Diffusion for Long Video Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06924)
*Do Xuan Long,Yale Song,Min-Yen Kan,Tomas Pfister,Long T. Le*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出A²RD，一种基于智能体自回归扩散的架构，通过检索-合成-精炼-更新循环实现长视频的分段合成与自改进，在一致性和叙事连贯性上显著超越现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有长视频合成方法在长时间跨度下存在语义漂移和叙事崩塌的问题，缺乏有效的长程一致性保持机制。

Method: A²RD采用智能体自回归扩散架构，将长视频合成建模为闭环过程，包含三个核心组件：(i)多模态视频记忆，跨模态跟踪视频进展；(ii)自适应片段生成，在不同生成模式间切换以平衡自然演进与视觉一致性；(iii)层次化测试时自改进，在帧级和视频级对每个片段进行自我优化以防止误差传播。

Result: 在多个公开基准和自建LVBench-C基准（涵盖1-10分钟视频）上，A²RD在一致性上提升高达30%，叙事连贯性提升20%；人工评估也证实了这些提升，同时在运动和过渡平滑性方面有显著改进。

Conclusion: A²RD通过解耦创造性合成与一致性保持，并引入闭环自改进机制，有效解决了长视频合成中的语义漂移和叙事崩塌问题，为长视频生成提供了新的有效范式。

Original Abstract: Synthesizing consistent and coherent long video remains a fundamental challenge. Existing methods suffer from semantic drift and narrative collapse over long horizons. We present A$^2$RD, an Agentic Auto-Regressive Diffusion architecture that decouples creative synthesis from consistency enforcement. A$^2$RD formulates long video synthesis as a closed-loop process that synthesizes and self-improves video segment-by-segment through a Retrieve--Synthesize--Refine--Update cycle. It comprises three core components: (i) Multimodal Video Memory that tracks video progression across modalities; (ii) Adaptive Segment Generation that switches among generation modes for natural progression and visual consistency; and (iii) Hierarchical Test-Time Self-Improvement that self-improves each segment at frame and video levels to prevent error propagation. We further introduce LVBench-C, a challenging benchmark with non-linear entity and environment transitions to stress-test long-horizon consistency. Across public and LVBench-C benchmarks spanning one- to ten-minute videos, A$^2$RD outperforms state-of-the-art baselines by up to 30% in consistency and 20% in narrative coherence. Human evaluations corroborate these gains while also highlighting notable improvements in motion and transition smoothness.

中文摘要翻译: 合成一致且连贯的长视频仍然是一个根本性挑战。现有方法在长时间跨度下存在语义漂移和叙事崩塌的问题。我们提出了A²RD，一种智能体自回归扩散架构，它将创造性合成与一致性保持解耦。A²RD将长视频合成建模为一个闭环过程，通过检索-合成-精炼-更新循环逐段合成并自我改进视频。它包含三个核心组件：(i) 多模态视频记忆，跨模态跟踪视频进展；(ii) 自适应片段生成，在不同生成模式间切换以实现自然演进和视觉一致性；(iii) 层次化测试时自改进，在帧级和视频级对每个片段进行自我优化以防止误差传播。我们还引入了LVBench-C，一个具有非线性实体和环境转换的挑战性基准，用于压力测试长程一致性。在涵盖1至10分钟视频的公开基准和LVBench-C基准上，A²RD在一致性上优于最先进的基线方法高达30%，在叙事连贯性上提升20%。人工评估证实了这些提升，同时还在运动和过渡平滑性方面显示出显著改进。

</details>


### [12] [XiYOLO: Energy-Aware Object Detection via Iterative Architecture Search and Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06927)
*Tony Tran,Richie R. Suganda,Bin Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种能量自适应框架XiYOLO，结合能量感知搜索空间、两阶段能量估计器和迭代搜索，在异构边缘设备上实现了比YOLOv12更好的能量-精度权衡，显著降低能耗。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有能量感知NAS方法部署场景有限，真实能耗高度依赖设备且测量成本高，难以直接优化。

Method: 提出能量自适应框架，包含能量感知XiResOFA搜索空间、两阶段能量估计器（提升少样本场景下的样本效率）和迭代搜索，先搜索单一高效基础架构，再通过复合缩放生成XiYOLO系列模型。

Result: 在PascalVOC上，中型XiYOLO达86.15 mAP50，比YOLOv12m在GPU上降低20.6%能耗、NPU上降低35.9%；在COCO上，小型XiYOLO在GPU上降低53.7%、NPU上降低51.6%能耗。两阶段估计器在仅2-20个目标设备样本下优于联合预测器。

Conclusion: XiYOLO在异构边缘设备上实现了更强的能量-精度权衡，为资源受限场景下的目标检测提供了高效解决方案。

Original Abstract: Object detection on heterogeneous edge devices must satisfy strict energy, latency, and memory constraints while still providing reliable perception for downstream autonomy. Existing energy-aware NAS methods often target limited deployment settings, while real energy remains difficult to optimize because it is highly device-dependent and costly to measure. We address these challenges with an energy-adaptive framework that combines an energy-aware XiResOFA search space, a two-stage energy estimator, and iterative search to identify a single energy-efficient base architecture. We then apply compound scaling to transform this base design into the XiYOLO family across deployment budgets, enabling interpretable accuracy-energy tradeoffs under sparse hardware measurements. Experiments on PascalVOC, COCO, and real-device deployment show that XiYOLO achieves a stronger energy-accuracy tradeoff than YOLO baselines. On PascalVOC, the medium XiYOLO model reaches 86.15 mAP50 while reducing energy relative to YOLOv12m by 20.6% on GPU and 35.9% on NPU. On COCO, XiYOLO reduces energy relative to YOLOv12 by up to 53.7% on GPU and 51.6% on NPU at the small scale. The proposed two-stage estimator also improves sample efficiency over a joint predictor under few-shot adaptation with only 2-20 target-device samples.

中文摘要翻译: 异构边缘设备上的目标检测必须满足严格的能量、延迟和内存约束，同时为下游自主任务提供可靠的感知能力。现有的能量感知NAS方法往往局限于有限的部署场景，而真实能耗因高度依赖设备且测量成本高昂而难以优化。我们提出了一种能量自适应框架来应对这些挑战，该框架结合了能量感知的XiResOFA搜索空间、两阶段能量估计器以及迭代搜索，以识别单一高效的基架构。随后，我们应用复合缩放将基架构转化为跨部署预算的XiYOLO系列，在稀疏硬件测量下实现可解释的精度-能量权衡。在PascalVOC、COCO以及真实设备部署上的实验表明，XiYOLO相比YOLO基线实现了更强的能量-精度权衡。在PascalVOC上，中型XiYOLO模型达到86.15 mAP50，同时相比YOLOv12m在GPU上降低20.6%的能量消耗，在NPU上降低35.9%。在COCO上，小型XiYOLO相比YOLOv12在GPU上降低高达53.7%的能量消耗，在NPU上降低51.6%。所提出的两阶段估计器在仅有2-20个目标设备样本的少样本自适应场景下，相比联合预测器提升了样本效率。

</details>


### [13] [Bringing Multimodal Large Language Models to Infrared-Visible Image Fusion Quality Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06969)
*Yuchen Guo,Junli Gong,Yao Lu,Xintong Xu,Yiuming Cheung,Weifeng Su*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FuScore，一种基于多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的红外-可见光图像融合质量评价方法，通过生成连续质量分数而非离散等级，并引入三维目标函数（分布监督、源内Thurstone保真度、跨源Thurstone保真度），实现了与人类视觉偏好最先进的相关性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有IVIF评价方法存在两个问题：一是过度优化手工无参考统计量或全参考指标（将源图像视为伪真值）；二是近年基于人类评分的奖励建模方法使用标量回归聚合分数，既未利用MLLM的推理能力，也未编码每幅图像的感知歧义。直接引入MLLM的离散独热监督会使相似质量融合图像坍缩到不同等级。

Method: 1）利用MLLM生成连续质量分数（而非离散等级预测），实现细粒度区分；2）利用四个IVIF特定子维度的一致性构建每幅图像的软标签，其锐度反映整体判断的共识程度；3）引入三部分目标函数：每幅图像分布监督、源内Thurstone保真度（方法级排序）、跨源Thurstone保真度（场景级排序）。

Result: 大量实验表明，FuScore与人类视觉偏好的相关性达到了最先进水平。

Conclusion: FuScore通过连续质量评分、软标签构建和三元目标函数设计，有效解决了现有IVIF评价方法中离散监督导致的信息丢失和感知歧义编码不足的问题，显著提升了质量评价与人类感知的一致性。

Original Abstract: Infrared-Visible image fusion (IVIF) aims to integrate thermal information and detailed spatial structures into a single fused image to enhance perception. However, existing evaluation approaches tend to over-optimize both hand-crafted no-reference statistics and full-reference metrics that treat the source images as pseudo ground truths. Recent IVIF reward-modelling efforts learn from human ratings but use scalar regression on aggregated scores, neither leveraging the reasoning of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) nor encoding per-image perceptual ambiguity in their supervision, but naively introducing MLLMs with discrete one-hot supervision likewise collapses fused images of similar quality into different rating levels. To address this, we introduce FuScore, which utilizes an MLLM to mimic human visual perception by producing continuous quality score, rather than discrete level predictions, enabling fine-grained discrimination among fused images of similar quality. We exploit the agreement among four IVIF-specific sub-dimensions to construct a per-image soft label whose sharpness reflects how consensual the overall judgment is. We further introduce a tripartite objective combining per-image distributional supervision, within-source-pair Thurstone fidelity for method-level ordering, and cross-source-pair Thurstone fidelity for scene-level ordering across scenes. Extensive experiments demonstrate that FuScore achieves state-of-the-art correlation with human visual preferences.

中文摘要翻译: 红外-可见光图像融合（IVIF）旨在将热红外信息与详细空间结构整合到单张融合图像中以增强感知。然而，现有评价方法倾向于过度优化手工设计的无参考统计量以及将源图像视为伪真值的全参考指标。近年来的IVIF奖励建模工作虽学习人类评分，但使用聚合分数的标量回归，既未利用多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的推理能力，也未在其监督中编码每幅图像的感知歧义；而简单引入MLLM的离散独热监督同样会将质量相似的融合图像坍缩到不同评级等级。为解决此问题，我们提出FuScore，该方法利用MLLM模拟人类视觉感知，生成连续质量分数而非离散等级预测，从而实现对相似质量融合图像的细粒度区分。我们利用四个IVIF特定子维度间的一致性构建每幅图像的软标签，其锐度反映了整体判断的共识程度。我们进一步引入了三部分目标函数，结合了每幅图像的分布监督、源内成对的Thurstone保真度（用于方法级排序）以及跨源成对的Thurstone保真度（用于跨场景的场景级排序）。大量实验表明，FuScore与人类视觉偏好的相关性达到了最先进水平。

</details>


### [14] [TRAJGANR: Trajectory-Centric Urban Multimodal Learning via Geospatially Aligned Neural Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06990)
*Maria Despoina Siampou,Gengchen Mai,Ni Lao,Jinmeng Rao,Neha Arora,Cyrus Shahabi,Shushman Choudhury*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TrajGANR，一个面向人类移动轨迹的多模态自监督学习框架，通过连续轨迹神经表示实现轨迹、街景图像和地理位置的细粒度对齐，在四个城市理解和道路任务上优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有地理空间多模态自监督学习方法主要针对静态模态对（如卫星图像、街景图像和文本）设计，依赖同地点或邻近地点的观测对齐，但这一假设不适用于人类移动轨迹——轨迹是沿路径的连续运动，而非离散位置的点观测。尽管轨迹对城市理解至关重要（可捕捉随时间变化的人车活动），但在当前地理空间MSSL框架中尚未被充分探索。

Method: TrajGANR学习一种连续神经表示，能够在轨迹路径上的任意点进行表征，从而实现与附近街景图像的细粒度对齐——即使图像位置与轨迹路点不重合。在此基础上，引入一个联合对齐三个模态（轨迹、街景图像、地理位置）的多模态自监督学习目标。

Result: 在四个城市移动性和道路理解任务上，TrajGANR consistently 优于现有的地理空间MSSL框架和一个专门的轨迹基础模型。消融实验表明，所提出的MSSL目标和多模态学习框架是性能提升的主要驱动力，细粒度地理空间对齐优于粗粒度聚合，地理空间多模态学习也优于单模态方法。

Conclusion: TrajGANR证明了将连续运动轨迹纳入地理空间多模态自监督学习的可行性和有效性，细粒度的跨模态对齐是关键优势，为轨迹驱动的城市理解提供了新范式。

Original Abstract: Multimodal self-supervised learning (MSSL) has emerged as a key paradigm for pretraining geospatial foundation models. However, existing geospatial MSSL methods are mainly designed for static pairs of modalities, such as satellite imagery, street-view imagery, and text, where learning is driven by aligning observations from the same or nearby locations. This assumption breaks down for human mobility trajectories, which represent continuous movement along paths rather than discrete observations at individual locations. Although trajectories are important for urban understanding through their ability to capture human activity across roads, neighborhoods, and places over time, they remain largely underexplored in current geospatial MSSL frameworks. We present TrajGANR, a novel trajectory-centric geospatial MSSL framework that aligns continuous movement patterns with static, location-based observations. TrajGANR learns a continuous neural representation of trajectories at arbitrary points along each path, which enables fine-grained alignment with nearby street-view images, even when they are not co-located with any trajectory waypoints. We leverage this capability to introduce an MSSL objective that jointly aligns three modalities: trajectories, street-view images, and their geographic locations. We evaluate TrajGANR on four urban mobility and road understanding tasks. Across these tasks, TrajGANR consistently outperforms existing geospatial MSSL frameworks and a trajectory-specific foundation model. Ablation studies further demonstrate that our proposed MSSL objective and the multimodal learning framework are the primary drivers of these improvements, highlighting the importance of fine-grained geospatial alignment over coarser aggregation, as well as geospatial multimodal learning.

中文摘要翻译: 多模态自监督学习（MSSL）已成为预训练地理空间基础模型的关键范式。然而，现有的地理空间MSSL方法主要针对静态模态对设计，例如卫星图像、街景图像和文本，其学习过程依赖于对齐来自同一或邻近位置的观测。这一假设对于人类移动轨迹而言不再成立，因为轨迹代表沿路径的连续运动，而非单个位置上的离散观测。尽管轨迹能够通过捕捉随时间推移跨道路、社区和地点的人类活动而对城市理解至关重要，它们在当前地理空间MSSL框架中仍 largely 未被充分探索。本文提出TrajGANR，一种新颖的以轨迹为中心的地理空间MSSL框架，它将连续运动模式与基于位置的静态观测对齐。TrajGANR学习轨迹在路径上任意点的连续神经表示，从而能够与附近的街景图像进行细粒度对齐——即使这些图像与任何轨迹路点不共位。我们利用这一能力引入了一个联合对齐三种模态（轨迹、街景图像及其地理位置）的MSSL目标。我们在四个城市移动性和道路理解任务上评估TrajGANR。在这些任务中，TrajGANR consistently 优于现有的地理空间MSSL框架以及一个特定于轨迹的基础模型。消融研究进一步表明，我们提出的MSSL目标和多模态学习框架是这些改进的主要驱动力，凸显了细粒度地理空间对齐相对于粗粒度聚合的重要性，以及地理空间多模态学习的价值。

</details>


### [15] [LensVLM: Selective Context Expansion for Compressed Visual Representation of Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07019)
*Roy Xie,Dan Friedman,Donghan Yu,Bowen Pan,Christopher Fifty,Jang-Hyun Kim,Xianzhi Du,Zhe Gan,Vivek Rathod,Bhuwan Dhingra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LensVLM 提出了一种推理框架和后训练方案，让视觉语言模型（VLM）先扫描压缩图像，再通过学到的工具选择性地将相关图像展开为未压缩形式，在 4.3 倍有效压缩下保持与全文本上限相当的准确率，并在 10.1 倍压缩下超越检索、文本压缩和视觉压缩基线。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: VLM 将文本渲染为图像处理可绕过分词，但固定尺寸图像编码器导致压缩时字符分辨率下降、准确率快速恶化，亟需一种既能压缩又能保持准确率的方法。

Method: LensVLM 是一个推理框架与后训练方案：首先让 VLM 扫描压缩后的图像，然后通过学到的工具（tool）选择性地将相关图像展开为未压缩形式，从而在不显著增加计算开销的前提下恢复关键细节。

Result: 基于 Qwen3.5-9B-Base，LensVLM 在 4.3 倍有效压缩下准确率与全文本上限相当，在 7 个文本 QA 基准上最高达 10.1 倍压缩时优于检索、文本压缩和视觉压缩基线；该方法还可泛化到多模态文档和代码理解任务，且压缩越大优势越明显。

Conclusion: 视觉压缩训练可使模型对渲染方式更鲁棒；随着压缩增大，模型越来越多依赖展开内容而非不可靠的视觉读取；实际工具选择上，渲染文本适合文本展开，含布局线索的原生文档则适合高分辨率图像展开。

Original Abstract: Vision Language Models (VLMs) offer the exciting possibility of processing text as rendered images, bypassing the need for tokenizing the text into long token sequences. Since VLM image encoders map fixed-size images to a fixed number of visual tokens, varying rendering resolution provides a fine-grained compression knob. However, accuracy deteriorates quickly as compression increases: characters shrink below the vision encoder's effective resolution, making them indistinguishable. To address this, we propose LensVLM, an inference framework and post-training recipe that enables VLMs to scan compressed images, then selectively expand only the relevant images to their uncompressed form via learned tools. Building on Qwen3.5-9B-Base, LensVLM maintains accuracy comparable to the full-text upper bound at 4.3x effective compression and outperforms retrieval-based, text- and visual-compression baselines up to 10.1x effective compression across seven text QA benchmarks. LensVLM also generalizes to multimodal document and code understanding tasks, with the accuracy gain over baselines growing as compression increases. Our analysis validates this approach: training makes visual compression robust to rendering choices, and as compression grows the model increasingly relies on expanded content rather than unreliable visual reading. The analysis also yields practical tool-choice guidance: text expansion is preferable for rendered text, while high-resolution image expansion suits native documents whose layout cues carry task-relevant information.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉语言模型（VLM）提供了将文本作为渲染图像进行处理的可能性，从而绕过了将文本分词为长序列的需求。由于 VLM 图像编码器将固定尺寸的图像映射到固定数量的视觉 token 上，改变渲染分辨率便提供了一种细粒度的压缩手段。然而，随着压缩程度的增加，准确率会迅速恶化：字符缩小到低于视觉编码器的有效分辨率，使其难以区分。为解决这一问题，我们提出了 LensVLM——一个推理框架和后训练方案，使 VLM 能够扫描压缩图像，然后通过学到的工具选择性地将相关图像展开为未压缩形式。基于 Qwen3.5-9B-Base，LensVLM 在 4.3 倍有效压缩下保持了与全文本上限相当的准确率，并在七个文本 QA 基准上，在高达 10.1 倍有效压缩下超越了基于检索、文本压缩和视觉压缩的基线。LensVLM 还能泛化到多模态文档和代码理解任务，且随着压缩增加，相比基线的准确率增益持续扩大。我们的分析验证了这一方法的有效性：训练使视觉压缩对渲染选择更加鲁棒，并且随着压缩增大，模型越来越依赖展开内容而非不可靠的视觉读取。分析还给出了实用的工具选择指导：对于渲染文本，文本展开更为可取；而对于布局线索承载任务相关信息的原生文档，高分辨率图像展开更合适。

</details>


### [16] [OneViewAll: Semantic Prior Guided One-View 6D Pose Estimation for Novel Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07023)
*Yang Luo,Yan Gong,Yongsheng Gao,Jie Zhao,Xinyu Zhang,Huaping Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出OneViewAll框架，在仅有一张真实RGB-D参考视图且无CAD模型的情况下，通过语义先验引导的Project-and-Compare范式实现6D物体姿态估计，在LINEMOD上达到92.5% ADD-0.1精度，大幅超越基线方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 实际6D姿态估计场景中常仅有一张真实世界RGB-D参考视图且无CAD模型，现有方法依赖显式3D模型或多视图数据，可扩展性受限。

Method: 提出OneViewAll框架，采用Project-and-Compare范式，在投影等变空间中直接对齐参考视图与查询观测，无需CAD渲染。渐进式整合三级层次化语义先验：(1)类别与场景级先验用于高效假设初始化；(2)物体级对称先验通过镜像融合进行几何补全；(3)补丁级先验用于判别式精炼。

Result: 在LINEMOD数据集上仅用一张真实参考视图达到92.5% ADD-0.1精度，远超CVPR 2025基线One2Any（52.6%）；在YCB-V、Real275、Toyota-Light上均取得一致性提升，且推理延迟低。

Conclusion: 对称感知投影在处理对称、无纹理和遮挡物体方面效果显著，验证了语义先验引导的单参考视图模型无关姿态估计的可行性与有效性。

Original Abstract: In many practical 6D object pose estimation scenarios, we often have access to only a single real-world RGB-D reference view per object, typically without CAD models. Existing methods largely rely on explicit 3D models or multi-view data, which limits their scalability. To address this challenging single-reference model-free setting, we propose \textbf{OneViewAll}, a semantic-prior-guided framework that performs pose estimation via a novel Project-and-Compare paradigm. Instead of relying on computationally expensive CAD-based rendering, our method directly aligns reference and query observations within a projection-equivariant space. OneViewAll progressively integrates hierarchical semantic priors across three levels: (1) \textit{category- and scene-level} priors for efficient hypothesis initialization; (2) \textit{object-level symmetry} priors for geometry completion via mirror fusion; and (3) \textit{patch-level} priors for discriminative refinement. Extensive experiments demonstrate that OneViewAll achieves \textbf{92.5\%} ADD-0.1 accuracy on the LINEMOD dataset using only one real reference view -- significantly outperforming the CVPR 2025 baseline One2Any (52.6\%). It also yields consistent improvements on YCB-V, Real275, and Toyota-Light while maintaining low inference latency. Our results underscore the efficacy of symmetry-aware projection in handling symmetric, texture-less, and occluded objects.

中文摘要翻译: 在许多实际的6D物体姿态估计场景中，我们通常只能获得每个物体单一的真实世界RGB-D参考视图，且通常没有CAD模型。现有方法大多依赖显式的3D模型或多视图数据，这限制了它们的可扩展性。为应对这一具有挑战性的单参考视图无模型设定，我们提出\textbf{OneViewAll}，一个语义先验引导的框架，通过新颖的Project-and-Compare（投影-比较）范式进行姿态估计。我们的方法不依赖计算昂贵的基于CAD的渲染，而是直接在投影等变空间中对齐参考观测与查询观测。OneViewAll渐进式地整合三个层次的层次化语义先验：(1)\textit{类别与场景级}先验用于高效的假设初始化；(2)\textit{物体级对称性}先验通过镜像融合进行几何补全；(3)\textit{补丁级}先验用于判别式精炼。大量实验表明，OneViewAll在LINEMOD数据集上仅使用一张真实参考视图即达到\textbf{92.5\%}的ADD-0.1精度——显著优于CVPR 2025基线方法One2Any的52.6%。它在YCB-V、Real275和Toyota-Light上也取得了一致的性能提升，同时保持较低的推理延迟。我们的结果强调了对称感知投影在处理对称、无纹理和遮挡物体方面的有效性。

</details>


### [17] [Learning to Track Instance from Single Nature Language Description](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07064)
*Yaozong Zheng,Bineng Zhong,Qihua Liang,Shuimu Zeng,Haiying Xia,Shuxiang Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自监督视觉语言跟踪方法（Tracker），通过动态令牌聚合模块，无需任何边界框标注即可实现基于自然语言描述的目标跟踪，在多个VL跟踪基准上超越现有自监督方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视觉语言跟踪方法依赖边界框真实标注，成本高昂且难以扩展；自监督VL跟踪尚属空白，本文旨在探索无需任何边界框标注、仅凭视频序列中的自然语言描述即可实现跟踪的自监督方法。

Method: 提出动态令牌聚合模块：1）基于锚点令牌从模板帧选择多个重要目标令牌；2）根据注意力分数合并所选目标令牌并聚合到语言令牌中，消除冗余视觉噪声并增强语义对齐；3）融合后的语言令牌作为引导信号从搜索帧提取潜在目标令牌并传播至后续帧，增强时序提示，实现无需标注视频的自监督实例跟踪学习。

Result: 在多个视觉语言跟踪基准上的大量实验表明，所提Tracker超越了当前最优的自监督方法。

Conclusion: 本文提出的动态令牌聚合方法有效实现了自监督视觉语言跟踪，无需大规模边界框标注即可学习语言引导的跟踪表征，为无标注场景下的VL跟踪提供了新范式。

Original Abstract: How to achieve vision-language (VL) tracking using natural language descriptions from a video sequence \textbf{without relying on any bounding-box ground truth}? In this work, we achieve this goal by tackling \textit{self-supervised VL tracking}, which aims to evaluate tracking capabilities guided by natural language descriptions. We introduce \textbf{\tracker}, a novel self-supervised VL tracker that is capable of tracking any referred object by a language description. Unlike traditional methods that equally fuse all language and visual tokens, we propose an efficient Dynamic Token Aggregation Module, which treats each visual token \textbf{unequally}. The module consists of three main steps: i) Based on an anchor token, it selects multiple important target tokens from the template frame. ii) The selected target tokens are merged according to their attention scores and aggregated into the language tokens, thereby eliminating redundant visual token noise and enhancing semantic alignment. iii) Finally, the fused language tokens serve as guiding signals to extract potential target tokens from the search frame and propagate them to subsequent frames, enhancing temporal prompts and encouraging the tracker to autonomously learn instance tracking from unlabeled videos. This new modeling approach enables the effective self-supervised learning of language-guided tracking representations without the need for large-scale bounding box annotations. Extensive experiments on VL tracking benchmarks show that {\tracker} surpasses SOTA self-supervised methods.

中文摘要翻译: 如何仅使用视频序列中的自然语言描述来实现视觉语言（VL）跟踪，而无需依赖任何边界框真实标注？在本文中，我们通过解决**自监督视觉语言跟踪**问题来实现这一目标，该任务旨在评估由自然语言描述引导的跟踪能力。我们提出了**Tracker**，一种新颖的自监督VL跟踪器，能够通过语言描述跟踪任何被引用的目标。与传统的平等融合所有语言和视觉令牌的方法不同，我们提出了一种高效的动态令牌聚合模块，该模块对每个视觉令牌进行**不平等**处理。该模块包含三个主要步骤：i) 基于一个锚点令牌，从模板帧中选择多个重要的目标令牌。ii) 根据注意力分数合并所选的目标令牌，并将其聚合到语言令牌中，从而消除冗余的视觉令牌噪声并增强语义对齐。iii) 最后，融合后的语言令牌作为引导信号，从搜索帧中提取潜在的目标令牌并将其传播到后续帧，增强时序提示，并鼓励跟踪器从无标签视频中自主地学习实例跟踪。这种新的建模方式使得无需大规模边界框标注即可有效进行语言引导的跟踪表征的自监督学习。在VL跟踪基准上的大量实验表明，Tracker超越了当前最先进的自监督方法。

</details>


### [18] [Learning Visual Feature-Based World Models via Residual Latent Action](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07079)
*Xinyu Zhang,Zhengtong Xu,Yutian Tao,Yeping Wang,Yu She,Abdeslam Boularias*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出残差潜在动作（RLA）表示及其世界模型RLA-WM，通过流匹配预测RLA，在仿真和真实数据集上性能超越现有基于特征和视频扩散的世界模型，并支持无需在线交互的机器人学习。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于特征的世界模型依赖直接回归导致预测模糊或崩溃，而生成式建模在高维特征空间中仍具挑战；同时视频扩散模型虽然效果好但计算成本极高。

Method: 从DINO残差中学习残差潜在动作（RLA）表示，并基于流匹配（flow matching）预测RLA值，构建RLA-WM世界模型。

Result: RLA-WM在仿真和真实数据集上超越现有基于特征和视频扩散的世界模型，同时推理速度比视频扩散快数个数量级；基于RLA-WM的机器人学习方法实现了无动作演示视频学习和完全在离线视频训练的世界模型内进行视觉强化学习。

Conclusion: RLA是一种高效、可预测、可泛化且编码时间进程的潜在动作表示，RLA-WM为机器人学习提供了一种高效且实用的新范式。

Original Abstract: World models predict future transitions from observations and actions. Existing works predominantly focus on image generation only. Visual feature-based world models, on the other hand, predict future visual features instead of raw video pixels, offering a promising alternative that is more efficient and less prone to hallucination. However, current feature-based approaches rely on direct regression, which leads to blurry or collapsed predictions in complex interactions, while generative modeling in high-dimensional feature spaces still remains challenging. In this work, we discover that a new type of latent action representation, which we refer to as *Residual Latent Action* (RLA), can be easily learned from DINO residuals. We also show that RLA is predictive, generalizable, and encodes temporal progression. Building on RLA, we propose *RLA World Model* (RLA-WM), which predicts RLA values via flow matching. RLA-WM outperforms both state-of-the-art feature-based and video-diffusion world models on simulation and real-world datasets, while being orders of magnitude faster than video diffusion. Furthermore, we develop two robot learning techniques that use RLA-WM to improve policy learning. The first one is a minimalist world action model with RLA that learns from actionless demonstration videos. The second one is the first visual RL framework trained entirely inside a world model learned from offline videos only, using a video-aligned reward and no online interactions or handcrafted rewards. Project page: https://mlzxy.github.io/rla-wm

中文摘要翻译: 世界模型根据观测和动作预测未来的状态转移。现有工作主要集中于图像生成。相比之下，基于视觉特征的世界模型预测未来的视觉特征而非原始视频像素，提供了一种更高效且不易产生幻觉的替代方案。然而，当前基于特征的方法依赖直接回归，导致在复杂交互中预测模糊或崩溃，而在高维特征空间中进行生成式建模仍然具有挑战性。在这项工作中，我们发现一种新型的潜在动作表示——称为残差潜在动作（RLA）——可以轻松地从DINO残差中学习得到。我们还证明了RLA具有预测性、泛化性，并且能够编码时间进程。基于RLA，我们提出了RLA世界模型（RLA-WM），通过流匹配预测RLA值。RLA-WM在仿真和真实世界数据集上均优于最先进的基于特征和视频扩散的世界模型，同时推理速度比视频扩散快数个数量级。此外，我们开发了两种利用RLA-WM来改进策略学习的机器人学习技术。第一种是基于RLA的极简世界动作模型，可从无动作演示视频中学习。第二种是首个完全在仅从离线视频学习的世界模型内部训练的视觉强化学习框架，使用视频对齐的奖励，无需在线交互或手工设计的奖励。

</details>


### [19] [InfoGeo: Information-Theoretic Object-Centric Learning for Cross-View Generalizable UAV Geo-Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07099)
*Hongyang Zhang,Maonnan Wang,Ziyao Wang,Hongrui Yin,Man OnPun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出InfoGeo框架，利用信息瓶颈和物体中心学习解决跨视角地理定位中的域偏移和视觉杂乱问题，显著优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有跨视角地理定位方法依赖全局特征对齐，难以应对不同区域纹理和天气条件导致的域偏移，尤其在无人机视角下密集细粒度物体造成严重视觉杂乱。

Method: 提出InfoGeo框架，将优化目标重构成信息瓶颈过程，包含两个核心目标：(i) 通过对齐跨视角的物体中心结构关系最大化视角不变信息，(ii) 通过跨视角知识约束最小化视角特定噪声信号。

Result: 在多个基准数据集和挑战性场景上的广泛评估表明，InfoGeo显著优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: InfoGeo通过信息论框架有效增强了跨视角地理定位的鲁棒性和泛化能力，为处理域偏移和视觉杂乱问题提供了新的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Cross-view geo-localization (CVGL) is fundamental for precise localization and navigation in GPS-denied environments, aiming to match ground or UAV imagery with satellite views. While existing approaches rely on global feature alignment, they often suffer from substantial domain shifts induced by varying regional textures and weather conditions. This issue becomes even more pronounced in UAV-based scenarios, where the broader perspective inevitably introduces dense, fine-grained objects, creating significant visual clutter. To address this, we draw inspiration from Object-Centric Learning (OCL) and propose InfoGeo, an information-theoretic framework designed to enhance robustness and generalization. InfoGeo reformulates the optimization as an information bottleneck process with two core objectives: (i) maximizing view-invariant information by aligning the object-centric structural relations across views, and (ii) minimizing view-specific noisy signals through cross-view knowledge constraints. Extensive evaluations across diverse benchmarks and challenging scenarios demonstrate that InfoGeo significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods.

中文摘要翻译: 跨视角地理定位（CVGL）是GPS信号缺失环境下实现精确定位与导航的基础技术，旨在将地面或无人机图像与卫星视图进行匹配。现有方法依赖全局特征对齐，但常因不同区域纹理和天气条件导致的显著域偏移而表现不佳。这一问题在无人机场景中更为突出——更广阔的视野不可避免地引入密集的细粒度物体，造成严重的视觉杂乱。为解决这一挑战，我们从物体中心学习（OCL）中汲取灵感，提出InfoGeo——一个信息论框架，旨在增强鲁棒性和泛化能力。InfoGeo将优化目标重构为信息瓶颈过程，包含两个核心目标：(i) 通过对齐跨视角的物体中心结构关系最大化视角不变信息，(ii) 通过跨视角知识约束最小化视角特定噪声信号。在多个基准数据集和挑战性场景上的广泛评估表明，InfoGeo显著优于现有最先进方法。

</details>


### [20] [Neurosymbolic Framework for Concept-Driven Logical Reasoning in Skeleton-Based Human Action Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07140)
*Talha Ilyas,Deval Mehta,Zongyuan Ge*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种神经符号框架REASON，将基于骨架的人体动作识别重构为基于运动基元的一阶逻辑推理，在保持竞争性识别性能的同时提供可解释的逻辑结构级解释。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于骨架的人体动作识别模型虽然性能优异，但大多是黑盒模型，难以解释其决策过程，缺乏可解释性。

Method: 1) 使用标准时空骨架编码器提取潜在运动表示；2) 通过时空概念解码器将潜在表示映射为可解释的概念谓词（分离姿态中心和动态中心抽象）；3) 通过可微分一阶逻辑层组合概念谓词，学习人类可读的逻辑规则；4) 利用LLM描述的对齐骨架表示与原子运动基元，建立感知与推理的共享概念空间。

Result: 在NTU RGB+D 60/120和NW-UCLA数据集上取得了有竞争力的识别性能，同时能够提供基于逻辑结构的显式、可解释的解释。

Conclusion: 神经符号推理是实现可解释的时空动作理解的有效范式。

Original Abstract: Skeleton-based human activity recognition has achieved strong empirical performance, yet most existing models remain black boxes and difficult to interpret. In this work, we introduce a neurosymbolic formulation of skeleton-based HAR that reframes action recognition as concept-driven first-order logical reasoning over motion primitives. Our framework bridges representation learning and symbolic inference by grounding first-order logic predicates in learnable spatial and temporal motion concepts. Specifically, we employ a standard spatio-temporal skeleton encoder to extract latent motion representations, which are then mapped to interpretable concept predicates via a spatio-temporal concept decoder that explicitly separates pose-centric and dynamics-centric abstractions. These concept predicates are composed through differentiable first-order logic layers, enabling the model to learn human-readable logical rules that govern action semantics. To impose semantic structure on the learned concepts, we align skeleton representations with LLM-derived descriptions of atomic motion primitives, establishing a shared conceptual space for perception and reasoning. Extensive experiments on NTU RGB+D 60/120 and NW-UCLA demonstrate that our approach achieves competitive recognition performance while providing explicit, interpretable explanations grounded in logical structure. Our results highlight neurosymbolic reasoning as an effective paradigm for interpretable spatio-temporal action understanding. Code: https://github.com/Mr-TalhaIlyas/REASON

中文摘要翻译: 基于骨架的人体动作识别已取得强大的经验性能，但大多数现有模型仍然是黑盒且难以解释。在这项工作中，我们提出了一种基于骨架的人体动作识别的神经符号化公式，将动作识别重构为基于运动基元的概念驱动的一阶逻辑推理。我们的框架通过将一阶逻辑谓词锚定在可学习的时空运动概念上，桥接了表示学习与符号推理。具体来说，我们采用标准的时空骨架编码器提取潜在运动表示，然后通过一个明确分离姿态中心和动力学中心抽象的时空概念解码器，将这些表示映射为可解释的概念谓词。这些概念谓词通过可微分的一阶逻辑层进行组合，使模型能够学习控制动作语义的人类可读的逻辑规则。为了向学习到的概念施加语义结构，我们将骨架表示与LLM派生的原子运动基元描述进行对齐，从而为感知和推理建立共享的概念空间。在NTU RGB+D 60/120和NW-UCLA上的大量实验表明，我们的方法在取得有竞争力的识别性能的同时，提供了基于逻辑结构的显式、可解释的解释。我们的结果凸显了神经符号推理作为可解释的时空动作理解的有效范式。代码：https://github.com/Mr-TalhaIlyas/REASON

</details>


### [21] [Qwen3-VL-Seg: Unlocking Open-World Referring Segmentation with Vision-Language Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07141)
*Yuan Yao,Qiushi Yang,Humen Zhong,Jiangning Wei,Yifang Men,Shuai Bai,Miaomiao Cui,Zhibo Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Qwen3-VL-Seg提出了一种轻量级、参数高效的框架，将多模态大模型预测的边界框作为结构先验，通过仅17M参数的框引导掩码解码器实现开放世界指代分割，并构建了SA1B-ORS数据集和ORS-Bench基准，在封闭和开放场景下均表现优异。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有MLLM在开放世界视觉定位中仅能输出稀疏的边界框坐标，无法实现密集的像素级分割预测。已有的分割方法要么直接预测稀疏轮廓坐标导致边界不连续，要么依赖外部分割基础模型（如SAM）带来巨大的架构和部署开销。因此需要一种参数高效、不依赖外部模型的方法来实现开放世界的指代分割。

Method: 提出Qwen3-VL-Seg框架，核心是轻量级框引导掩码解码器（仅17M参数，约基座模型的0.4%），包含四个模块：多尺度空间特征注入、空间-语义查询构建、框引导的高分辨率像素融合、迭代掩码感知查询精化。同时构建SA1B-ORS数据集（含SA1B-CoRS和SA1B-DeRS两个子集）用于开放世界训练，以及ORS-Bench基准（含分布内和分布外子集）用于评估。

Result: 在指代表达分割、视觉定位和ORS-Bench上的大量实验表明，Qwen3-VL-Seg在封闭集和开放世界设置下均表现强劲，在语言密集型指令上具有明显优势，且具有较强的分布外泛化能力。通用多模态基准评估显示，模型在进行分割适配后仍保持了通用的多模态能力。

Conclusion: 通过参数高效的框引导掩码解码器设计，结合大规模开放世界训练数据和评估基准，Qwen3-VL-Seg在不依赖外部分割模型的前提下实现了出色的开放世界指代分割性能，验证了将MLLM预测的边界框作为语义结构先验进行像素级解码的有效性。

Original Abstract: Open-world referring segmentation requires grounding unconstrained language expressions to precise pixel-level regions. Existing multimodal large language models (MLLMs) exhibit strong open-world visual grounding, but their outputs remain limited to sparse bounding-box coordinates and are insufficient for dense visual prediction. Recent MLLM-based segmentation methods either directly predict sparse contour coordinates, struggling to reconstruct continuous object boundaries, or rely on external segmentation foundation models such as the Segment Anything Model (SAM), introducing substantial architectural and deployment overhead. We present Qwen3-VL-Seg, a parameter-efficient framework that treats the MLLM-predicted box as a semantically grounded structural prior and decodes it into pixel-level referring segmentation. At its core, a lightweight box-guided mask decoder combines multi-scale spatial feature injection, spatial-semantic query construction, box-guided high-resolution pixel fusion, and iterative mask-aware query refinement, introducing only 17M parameters (about 0.4\% of the base model). For scalable open-world training, we construct SA1B-ORS, an SA-1B-derived dataset with two subsets: SA1B-CoRS (category-oriented samples) and SA1B-DeRS (descriptive, instance-specific samples). For evaluation, we curate ORS-Bench, a manually screened benchmark with in-distribution and out-of-distribution subsets covering diverse referring expression types. Extensive experiments on referring expression segmentation, visual grounding, and ORS-Bench show that Qwen3-VL-Seg performs strongly across closed-set and open-world settings, with clear advantages on language-intensive instructions and strong out-of-distribution generalization. Evaluations on general multimodal benchmarks further show that the model broadly preserves general-purpose multimodal competence after segmentation-oriented adaptation.

中文摘要翻译: 开放世界指代分割要求将不受约束的语言表达与精确的像素级区域对应起来。现有的多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）展现出强大的开放世界视觉定位能力，但它们的输出仍然局限于稀疏的边界框坐标，不足以用于密集的视觉预测。近期基于MLLM的分割方法要么直接预测稀疏的轮廓坐标，难以重建连续的物体边界，要么依赖外部分割基础模型（如Segment Anything Model, SAM），引入了大量的架构和部署开销。我们提出了Qwen3-VL-Seg，一个参数高效的框架，它将MLLM预测的边界框视为语义结构先验，并将其解码为像素级指代分割。其核心是一个轻量级的框引导掩码解码器，该解码器结合了多尺度空间特征注入、空间-语义查询构建、框引导的高分辨率像素融合以及迭代掩码感知查询精化，仅引入了17M参数（约为基座模型的0.4%）。为了进行可扩展的开放世界训练，我们构建了SA1B-ORS，这是一个基于SA-1B的数据集，包含两个子集：SA1B-CoRS（类别导向样本）和SA1B-DeRS（描述性、实例特定样本）。为了进行评估，我们整理了ORS-Bench，一个经过人工筛选的基准，包含分布内和分布外子集，覆盖了多样的指代表达类型。在指代表达分割、视觉定位和ORS-Bench上的大量实验表明，Qwen3-VL-Seg在封闭集和开放世界设置下均表现强劲，在语言密集型指令上具有明显优势，且具有较强的分布外泛化能力。在通用多模态基准上的评估进一步表明，该模型在进行分割适配后，广泛保留了通用的多模态能力。

</details>


### [22] [AGA3DNet: Anatomy-Guided Gaussian Priors with Multi-view xLSTM for 3D Brain MRI Subtype Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07142)
*Peiyu Duan,Xueqi Guo,Sepehr Farhand,Mehmet Berk Sahin,Xinyuan Zheng,James S. Duncan,Gerardo Hermosillo Valadez,Yoshihisa Shinagawa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AGA3DNet 是一个报告引导的3D脑MRI分类框架，通过将放射学报告中的简短解剖短语转换为空间先验，与轻量级3D CNN和多视图xLSTM聚合相结合，实现准确的脑MRI亚型分类，并支持可解释的临床定位。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 准确的3D脑MRI亚型分类需要同时利用局部解剖线索和长距离上下文推理，而现有方法往往缺乏可解释的解剖引导，且需要密集的体素标注。本文旨在利用放射学报告中现成的简短解剖短语作为软先验，提供解剖学引导且无需密集标注。

Method: 从放射学报告中提取简短解剖短语，映射到图谱定义的脑区，通过符号距离变换和高斯加权生成平滑的空间先验；将该先验作为软解剖先验通道，与轻量级3D CNN和多视图xLSTM聚合模块融合，实现分类。

Result: 在回顾性机构脑MRI队列的异常亚型区分任务上，AGA3DNet相比可复现的3D分类基线，在多个性能指标上取得了更优的整体平衡，并通过先验通道支持临床可解释的定位。

Conclusion: AGA3DNet有效利用放射学报告中的解剖短语作为先验，提升了3D脑MRI分类性能及可解释性，但存在单队列评估以及缺乏大规模公开脑MRI-报告配对数据集的局限性。

Original Abstract: Accurate 3D brain MRI subtype classification benefits from both localized anatomical cues and long-range contextual reasoning. We present AGA3DNet, a report-grounded framework that incorporates brief anatomical phrases extracted from radiology reports as a soft anatomical prior channel and fuses it with a lightweight 3D CNN and multi-view xLSTM aggregation. Specifically, extracted anatomical phrases are mapped to atlas-defined regions and converted into smooth spatial priors using a signed-distance transform followed by Gaussian weighting, providing interpretable, anatomy-grounded guidance without requiring dense voxel annotations. We evaluate AGA3DNet on a retrospective institutional brain MRI cohort for abnormal subtype discrimination and compare against reproducible 3D classification baselines. AGA3DNet achieves improved overall balance across performance metrics and supports clinically interpretable localization through the prior channel. We discuss limitations related to single-cohort evaluation and the lack of large-scale public brain MRI datasets paired with radiology reports under broadly usable terms.

中文摘要翻译: 准确的3D脑MRI亚型分类既需要局部解剖线索，也需要长距离上下文推理。本文提出AGA3DNet，一种报告引导的框架，它将从放射学报告中提取的简短解剖短语作为软解剖先验通道，并与轻量级3D CNN和多视图xLSTM聚合相融合。具体而言，提取的解剖短语被映射到图谱定义的脑区，并通过符号距离变换和高斯加权转换为平滑的空间先验，从而在无需密集体素标注的情况下提供可解释的、基于解剖的引导。我们在回顾性机构脑MRI队列上评估AGA3DNet的异常亚型区分能力，并与可复现的3D分类基线进行比较。AGA3DNet在多个性能指标上取得了更优的整体平衡，并通过先验通道支持临床可解释的定位。我们讨论了单队列评估以及缺乏可在广泛可用条款下使用的大规模公开脑MRI数据集与放射学报告配对数据的局限性。

</details>


### [23] [TriP: A Triangle Puzzle Approach to Robust Translation Averaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07143)
*Zhekai Fan,Wanze Li,Jinxin Wang,Yunpeng Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于三角形的平移平均框架TriP，通过对三角形几何推断局部相对边尺度，再在对数域中同步重叠三角形的尺度来恢复全局一致的边长和相机位置。该方法对对抗性、循环一致等结构化异常具有鲁棒性，且无需额外约束即可避免退化零解，在合成和真实数据集上大幅优于以往方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 平移平均问题中方向测量缺乏距离信息，导致估计问题高度病态且对异常观测极其敏感。现有方法难以同时应对结构化异常和避免解退化问题。

Method: TriP方法分为两步：首先从三角形几何中推断局部相对边缘尺度；然后在对数域中对重叠三角形的尺度进行同步，恢复全局一致的边长和相机位置。该方法利用三角形之间的高阶一致性来抵御异常，且由于在对数域中进行尺度同步，退化零解被自然排除。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上，TriP在所有先前平移平均方法中表现最优，且大幅领先。该方法完全可并行化、计算高效，可自然扩展到百万级相机规模的图。

Conclusion: TriP通过引入三角形高阶一致性约束，有效解决了平移平均问题中异常敏感性和解退化两大核心难题，在理论和实践上均表现出显著优势。

Original Abstract: Translation averaging aims to recover camera locations from pairwise relative translation directions and is a fundamental component of global Structure-from-Motion pipelines. The problem is challenging because direction measurements contain no distance information, making the estimation problem highly ill-conditioned and highly sensitive to corrupted observations. In this paper, we propose TriP, a triangle-based framework for robust translation averaging. TriP first infers local relative edge scales from triangle geometry, and then synchronizes the scales of overlapping triangles in the logarithmic domain to recover globally consistent edge lengths and camera locations. By leveraging higher-order consistency across triangles, the proposed method is robust to adversarial, cycle-consistent, and other structured corruptions. In addition, TriP avoids the collapse issue without requiring any extra anti-collapse constraints, since log-scale synchronization excludes the degenerate zero-scale solution by construction. These structural advantages enable a particularly strong theory for exact location recovery. On the practical side, TriP is fully parallelizable, computationally efficient, and naturally scalable to graphs with millions of cameras. Moreover, it outperforms all previous translation averaging methods by a large margin on both synthetic and real datasets.

中文摘要翻译: 平移平均旨在从成对相对平移方向中恢复相机位置，是全局运动恢复结构（Structure-from-Motion）流程的基础组成部分。该问题极具挑战性，因为方向测量不包含距离信息，导致估计问题高度病态且对异常观测极其敏感。本文提出TriP，一种基于三角形的鲁棒平移平均框架。TriP首先从三角形几何中推断局部相对边尺度，然后在对数域中同步重叠三角形的尺度，从而恢复全局一致的边长和相机位置。通过利用三角形之间的高阶一致性，所提方法对对抗性异常、循环一致异常及其他结构化异常均具有鲁棒性。此外，TriP无需任何额外的抗塌缩约束即可避免塌缩问题，因为对数尺度同步机制从结构上排除了退化的零解。这些结构优势使得该方法在精确位置恢复方面具有极强的理论保证。在实际应用方面，TriP完全可并行化、计算高效，且可自然扩展到包含数百万台相机的大规模图。此外，在合成数据集和真实数据集上，TriP的性能均大幅超越所有先前的平移平均方法。

</details>


### [24] [Uncovering and Shaping the Latent Representation of 3D Scene Topology in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07148)
*Haoming Wang,Wei Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现视觉-语言模型（VLM）内部存在隐式的3D场景拓扑地图，但该空间被非几何语义（颜色、形状）掩盖。通过跨场景线性特征提取分离出干净的空间子空间，并证明其与场景3D高斯核图的拉普拉斯特征映射等价。基于此提出Dirichlet能量正则化方法，仅需500步微调即可在空间拓扑理解任务上提升最高12.1%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 认知科学表明人类通过认知地图（即3D空间的异中心且拓扑保持的表征）来导航环境。虽然现代VLM从2D自我中心输入中展现出涌现的空间推理能力，但尚不清楚它们是否构建了类似的3D内部表征。本文旨在探究VLM中是否存在这种隐式3D空间表征，并开发方法利用和增强它。

Method: (1) 通过跨场景线性特征提取，从VLM的潜在表征中分离出支配空间输出的干净空间子空间；(2) 数学上证明该空间子空间与场景3D高斯核图的拉普拉斯特征映射在连续极限下收敛到物理3D空间；(3) 基于Dirichlet能量提出一种数学上严谨的潜在正则化方法，在简单合成数据上进行极少量（500步）的有监督微调。

Result: (1) 证明了VLM确实拥有潜在3D场景拓扑地图，但被非几何视觉语义掩盖；(2) 分离出的空间子空间能因果控制模型的空间输出；(3) 该空间与场景3D高斯核图的拉普拉斯特征映射对应；(4) 基于Dirichlet能量的正则化方法在500步微调后，在真实空间基准上提升高达12.1%，超过标准SFT和竞争基线。

Conclusion: VLM中存在隐式的3D空间拓扑表征，通过适当的数学方法可以分离并增强这一空间能力。所提出的Dirichlet能量正则化方法简单有效，证明了从几何原理出发设计正则化策略在提升VLM空间推理能力上的巨大潜力。

Original Abstract: Decades of cognitive science establish that humans navigate environments by forming cognitive maps, defined as allocentric and topology-preserving representations of 3D space. While modern Vision-Language Models (VLMs) demonstrate emergent spatial reasoning from 2D egocentric inputs, it remains unclear whether they construct an analogous 3D internal representation. In this paper, we demonstrate that current VLMs do possess a latent topological map of 3D scenes, but it is heavily overshadowed by non-geometric visual semantics, such as color and shape. By isolating this spatial subspace through cross-scene linear feature extraction, we extract a clean spatial subspace that causally controls the model's spatial outputs. We mathematically shape this latent representation and prove its correspondence to the Laplacian eigenmaps of the scene's 3D Gaussian-kernel graph, converging to the physical 3D space in the continuous limit. Motivated by this geometric identification, we further introduce a mathematically principled latent regularization method for VLMs, based on Dirichlet energy. Applying this single-term regularizer to a minimal 500-step supervised VLM fine-tuning (SFT) on simple synthetic data yields significant improvements on real-world spatial benchmarks, outperforming standard SFT and competitive baselines by up to 12.1\% in spatial tasks involving scene topology understanding. Source code is available at https://github.com/pittisl/vlm-latent-shaping

中文摘要翻译: 数十年的认知科学研究表明，人类通过形成认知地图（即3D空间的异中心且拓扑保持的表征）来导航环境。尽管现代视觉-语言模型（VLM）从2D自我中心输入中展现出涌现的空间推理能力，但尚不清楚它们是否构建了类似的3D内部表征。在本文中，我们证明了当前的VLM确实拥有3D场景的潜在拓扑地图，但它被非几何的视觉语义（如颜色和形状）严重掩盖。通过跨场景线性特征提取来隔离这一空间子空间，我们提取出一个干净的空间子空间，该子空间因果性地控制着模型的空间输出。我们在数学上塑造了这一潜在表征，并证明其与场景3D高斯核图的拉普拉斯特征映射相对应，并在连续极限下收敛到物理3D空间。受此几何识别的启发，我们进一步基于Dirichlet能量引入了一种数学上严谨的VLM潜在正则化方法。将这一单项正则化器应用于最小500步的简单合成数据上有监督VLM微调（SFT），在真实世界的空间基准上取得了显著改进，在涉及场景拓扑理解的空间任务中，比标准SFT和竞争基线高出高达12.1%。源代码可在https://github.com/pittisl/vlm-latent-shaping获取。

</details>


### [25] [Real-IAD MVN: A Multi-View Normal Vector Dataset and Benchmark for High-Fidelity Industrial Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07149)
*Wenbing Zhu,Jianing Liang,Linjie Cheng,Yurui Pan,Zhuhao Chen,Qingwang Yan,Yudong Cheng,Jianghui Zhang,Mingmin Chi,Bo Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对工业异常检测中现有方法难以检测细微几何缺陷的问题，提出Real-IAD-MVN大规模多视图表面法线数据集，用密集多视图伪3D（表面法线）替代稀疏3D点云，并基于跨模态统一原型重建的基线方法验证了该数据集的有效性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有工业异常检测方法在处理细微几何缺陷（如划痕、凹坑）时存在局限性：2D RGB图像对纹理和光照敏感但易忽略几何异常，3D点云虽能捕捉宏观形状但因稀疏性难以检测微观缺陷。因此需要一种兼具微观细节和几何表征能力的数据与方案。

Method: 1) 升级采集系统，从五个不同视角捕获高保真表面法线图，构建Real-IAD-MVN数据集，完全取代稀疏3D点云；2) 提出基于重建的基线方法，学习从RGB图像和法线图流中提取跨模态统一原型，用于异常检测。

Result: 1) 密集多视图伪3D（表面法线）相比稀疏3D点云显著提升了检测性能；2) 所提出的跨模态统一原型方法在检测精度上超越了现有的最先进多模态融合方法。

Conclusion: 新的Real-IAD-MVN数据集和跨模态统一原型基线方法有效推动了几何异常检测的发展，证实了表面法线作为密集几何表征在工业异常检测中的优越性。

Original Abstract: Industrial Anomaly Detection (IAD) is critical for quality control, but existing methods struggle with subtle, geometric defects. Standard 2D (RGB) images are sensitive to texture and lighting but often miss fine geometric anomalies. While 3D point clouds capture macro-shape, they are typically too sparse to detect micro-defects like scratches or pits. We address this fundamental data limitation by introducing Real-IAD-MVN (Multi-View Normal), a large-scale industrial dataset. By upgrading our acquisition system, Real-IAD-MVN captures high-fidelity surface normal maps from five distinct viewpoints, replacing sparse 3D data entirely. This provides a comprehensive geometric representation at a micro-detail level, making previously invisible side-wall and occluded defects explicitly detectable. Our experiments, conducted on this new dataset, first provide evidence that incorporating dense, multi-view pseudo-3D (surface normals) yields significantly better detection performance than using sparse 3D point cloud data. To further validate the dataset and provide a strong benchmark, we introduce a baseline method based on reconstruction, which learns to extract cross-modal unified prototypes from the image and normal map streams. We demonstrate that this unified prototype approach surpasses existing state-of-the-art multimodal fusion methods, highlighting the rich potential of our new dataset for advancing geometric anomaly detection.

中文摘要翻译: 工业异常检测（IAD）对于质量控制至关重要，但现有方法难以检测细微的几何缺陷。标准2D（RGB）图像对纹理和光照敏感，但往往遗漏细微的几何异常。3D点云虽能捕捉宏观形状，但因过于稀疏而无法检测划痕或凹坑等微观缺陷。我们针对这一基础性数据限制，提出了Real-IAD-MVN（多视图法线）——一个大规模工业数据集。通过升级采集系统，Real-IAD-MVN从五个不同视角捕获高保真表面法线图，完全取代了稀疏的3D数据。这使得在微观细节层面提供了全面的几何表征，使先前不可见的侧壁和遮挡缺陷能够被显式检测。在该数据集上的实验首先证明，引入密集的多视图伪3D（表面法线）相比使用稀疏3D点云数据能获得显著更优的检测性能。为进一步验证该数据集并提供强基准，我们提出了一种基于重建的基线方法，该方法学习从图像和法线图流中提取跨模态统一原型。实验表明，这种统一原型方法超越了现有的最先进多模态融合方法，凸显了我们的新数据集在推动几何异常检测发展方面的丰富潜力。

</details>


### [26] [PRIMED: Adaptive Modality Suppression for Referring Audio-Visual Segmentation via Biased Competition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07154)
*Yuchen He,Jing Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PRIMED 提出一种受认知神经科学中竞争偏置理论启发的自适应模态抑制方法，通过模态先验解码器、Token 蒸馏器和空间感知语义对齐损失，实现更准确的指代性视听分割。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法将多模态线索视为同质输入进行融合、提示或推理，容易受到无关或误导性模态的影响，缺乏对不同指代表达和场景下模态相关性的自适应建模能力。

Method: 1) 模态先验解码器估计指代表达依赖音频、视觉还是两者联合交互，生成模态先验自适应引导高层注意力；2) Token 蒸馏器从高层特征提取紧凑全局视觉token，经竞争感知跨模态融合模块共享分层全局上下文；3) 空间感知语义对齐损失通过对比学习增强前景-背景区分。

Result: 在 Ref-AVS 基准上取得了最先进的整体性能。

Conclusion: 受认知神经科学启发的自适应模态抑制策略能有效处理指代性视听分割中多模态相关性动态变化的问题，显著提升分割准确性。

Original Abstract: Referring Audio-Visual Segmentation (Ref-AVS) seeks to localize and segment target objects in video frames based on visual, auditory, and textual referring cues. The task is challenging because the relevance of different modalities varies across referring expressions and scenes, while existing methods typically treat multimodal cues as homogeneous inputs for fusion, prompting, or reasoning, making them vulnerable to irrelevant or misleading modalities. To address this problem, we propose PRIMED, inspired by the biased competition theory in cognitive neuroscience, which explicitly models both visual perception and language-driven prior modulation, and enables more accurate Ref-AVS by adaptive modality suppression. Specifically, a Modality Prior Decoder first estimates whether the referring expression relies primarily on audio, vision, or their joint interaction, generating a modality prior to adaptively guide high-level attention. A Token Distiller further extracts compact global visual tokens from high-level features and shares them across Competition-aware Cross-modal Fusion modules to provide hierarchical global context. Additionally, we introduce a Spatial-Aware Semantic Alignment loss to further enhance foreground-background discrimination through contrastive learning. Extensive experiments on the Ref-AVS benchmark demonstrate that PRIMED achieves state-of-the-art overall performance.

中文摘要翻译: 指代性视听分割（Ref-AVS）旨在基于视觉、听觉和文本指代线索，在视频帧中定位并分割目标对象。该任务具有挑战性，因为不同模态的相关性在不同指代表达和场景中会发生变化，而现有方法通常将多模态线索视为同质输入进行融合、提示或推理，使其容易受到无关或误导性模态的影响。为了解决这一问题，我们受认知神经科学中竞争偏置理论的启发，提出了 PRIMED，它显式地建模视觉感知和语言驱动的先验调制，通过自适应模态抑制实现更准确的 Ref-AVS。具体而言，模态先验解码器首先估计指代表达主要依赖音频、视觉还是二者的联合交互，生成模态先验以自适应地引导高层注意力。Token 蒸馏器进一步从高层特征中提取紧凑的全局视觉token，并将其共享给竞争感知跨模态融合模块，以提供分层全局上下文。此外，我们引入了空间感知语义对齐损失，通过对比学习进一步增强前景-背景区分能力。在 Ref-AVS 基准上的大量实验表明，PRIMED 取得了最先进的整体性能。

</details>


### [27] [Masks Can Talk: Extracting Structured Text Information from Single-Modal Images for Remote Sensing Change Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07178)
*Kai Zheng,Hang-Cheng Dong,Jiatong Pan,Zhenkai Wu,Fupeng Wei,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出S2M框架，直接从变化检测数据集的标签中提取结构化文本语义（四元组：地点、类型、方式、数量），以零额外标注成本实现精准的多模态变化检测，在Gaza-Change-v2数据集上超越基于大语言模型的方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有遥感变化检测方法中，单模态方法易混淆真正语义变化与视觉相似变化；多模态方法虽引入文本辅助监督，但描述要么语义粗糙、非结构化，要么由模型生成而含噪声。关键问题是：这些方法都忽略了变化检测数据集自带的标签中本就隐式编码了细粒度变化语义信息。

Method: 提出S2M框架，首先将每个变化区域自动转录为语义四元组（where, what, how, how many），并转换为固定模板的文本描述，提供精确、密集、无噪声的多模态监督；其次采用两阶段训练策略，先在遥感图像上微调获得领域鲁棒表示，再引入带有双向对比损失的多模态解码器实现视觉特征与结构化文本嵌入的深度对齐。

Result: 在新建的Gaza-Change-v2多类变化检测数据集上，S2M的Sek达17.80%，F_scd达66.14%，显著超过甚至包括使用大语言模型的多模态方法。

Conclusion: 标签本身就能提供精确的变化语义信息，无需额外标注成本；验证了从标签中提取结构化文本特征作为多模态监督的有效性。

Original Abstract: Remote sensing change detection is pivotal for urban monitoring, disaster assessment, and environmental resource management. Yet, unimodal deep learning methods frequently confuse genuine semantic changes with visually similar but irrelevant variations. Recent multimodal approaches incorporate text as auxiliary supervision, but their descriptions are either semantically coarse and unstructured or model-generated and thus noisy. Critically, all of them overlook a simple fact: fine-grained change semantics are already implicitly encoded in the ground-truth mask labels that come standard with every change detection dataset. These masks know where the change happened, what the land-cover types were before and after, how the transition occurred, and how many objects were involved. In this paper, we propose S2M, a framework that obtains structured textual features directly from change labels at zero additional annotation cost. Specifically, each change region is automatically transcribed into a semantic quadruple (where, what, how, how many) and converted into several fixed-template text descriptions, providing precise, dense, and noise-free multimodal supervision. We adopts a two-stage training strategy to fine-tune on remote sensing imagery firstly for robust domain-specific representation, after which a multimodal decoder with a bi-directional contrastive loss is introduced to achieve deep alignment between visual features and structured textual embeddings. To validate our method, we construct Gaza-Change-v2, a new multi-class change detection (MCD) dataset about the Gaza Strip. On this MCD dataset, S2M achieves a Sek of 17.80\% and an F$_{\text{scd}}$ of 66.14\%, notably surpassing even multimodal methods that leverage large language models. Our work demonstrates that masks can indeed talk. They tell us exactly what, where, how, and how many changes have occurred.

中文摘要翻译: 遥感变化检测对于城市监测、灾害评估和环境资源管理至关重要。然而，单模态深度学习方法常常混淆真正的语义变化与视觉相似但无关的变化。近年来的多模态方法引入文本作为辅助监督，但其描述要么语义粗糙且非结构化，要么由模型生成而带有噪声。关键在于，这些方法都忽略了一个简单事实：细粒度的变化语义已经隐式编码在每一个变化检测数据集标配的标注掩码中。这些掩码知道变化发生的位置、变化前后的土地覆盖类型、变化如何发生以及涉及了多少目标。本文提出S2M，一个以零额外标注成本直接从变化标签中获取结构化文本特征的框架。具体而言，每个变化区域被自动转录为语义四元组（位置、类型、方式、数量），并转换为若干固定模板的文本描述，从而提供精确、密集且无噪声的多模态监督。我们采用两阶段训练策略：首先在遥感图像上微调以获得鲁棒的领域特定表示，然后引入带有双向对比损失的多模态解码器，实现视觉特征与结构化文本嵌入的深度对齐。为了验证我们的方法，我们构建了Gaza-Change-v2，一个关于加沙地带的新多类变化检测（MCD）数据集。在该MCD数据集上，S2M达到了17.80%的Sek和66.14%的F_scd，显著超越了甚至包括使用大语言模型的多模态方法。我们的工作表明：标签确实可以说话。它们准确地告诉我们变化的地点、类型、方式和数量。

</details>


### [28] [SatSurfGS: Generalizable 2D Gaussian Splatting for Sparse-View Satellite Surface Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07181)
*Min Chen,Wei Guo,Bin Wang,Wen Li,Tong Fang,Jinbo Zhang,Junqi Zhao,Hong Kuang,Han Hu,Xuming Ge,Qing Zhu,Bo Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SatSurfGS，一个基于2DGS的可泛化稀疏视角卫星影像表面重建方法，通过粗到细的高斯属性预测框架和三级显式局部几何可靠性建模，在渲染质量、重建精度、跨数据集泛化和推理效率上优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 稀疏视角卫星影像表面重建面临多视角匹配可靠性高度空间异质性的根本挑战，光度差异大、弱纹理和重复纹理导致几何约束稀疏且局部不可靠。现有2DGS虽比3DGS更适合连续表面显式表示，但缺乏针对稀疏视角卫星表面重建的可泛化前馈框架。

Method: 构建粗到细的高斯属性预测框架，在特征学习、参数估计和训练优化三个层面显式建模局部几何可靠性：(1)置信感知单目多视图特征融合模块，根据局部置信度自适应融合单目先验与多视图匹配特征；(2)跨阶段自一致性残差引导模块，利用前一阶段渲染高度图与当前阶段MVS高度图的残差及置信度信息稳定逐阶段高斯参数精化；(3)置信双向路由损失，实现几何与外观监督的差异化分配。

Result: 在卫星数据集上，相较于代表性可泛化基线和有竞争力的逐场景优化方法，在渲染质量、表面重建精度、跨数据集泛化能力和推理效率方面均取得更优结果。

Conclusion: SatSurfGS通过显式建模局部几何可靠性的可泛化2DGS框架，有效解决了稀疏视角卫星影像表面重建中多视角匹配不可靠的问题，实现了高质量、高效率且具备跨数据集泛化能力的表面重建。

Original Abstract: Sparse-view satellite image surface reconstruction remains highly challenging, fundamentally because the reliability of multi-view matching under satellite imaging conditions is strongly spatially heterogeneous. Affected by large photometric differences, weak textures, and repetitive textures, multi-view geometric constraints are often sparse, unevenly distributed, and locally unreliable. Although 2D Gaussian Splatting (2DGS) is more suitable than 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) for the explicit representation of continuous surfaces, research on generalizable feed-forward 2DGS frameworks for sparse-view satellite surface reconstruction is still lacking. To address this issue, we propose SatSurfGS, a generalizable sparse-view surface reconstruction method for satellite imagery based on 2DGS. The proposed method builds a coarse-to-fine Gaussian attribute prediction framework and explicitly models local geometric reliability at three levels: feature learning, Gaussian parameter estimation, and training optimization. Specifically, we propose a confidence-aware monocular multi-view feature fusion module to adaptively integrate monocular priors and multi-view matching features according to local confidence; a cross-stage self-consistency residual guidance module to stabilize stage-wise Gaussian parameter refinement using the residual between the rendered height map from the previous stage and the current-stage MVS height map, together with confidence information; and a confidence bidirectional routing loss to achieve differentiated allocation of geometric and appearance supervision. Experiments on satellite datasets show that the proposed method achieves improved rendering quality, surface reconstruction accuracy, cross-dataset generalization, and inference efficiency compared with representative generalizable baselines and competitive per-scene optimization methods.

中文摘要翻译: 稀疏视角卫星影像表面重建仍然极具挑战性，根本原因在于卫星成像条件下多视图匹配的可靠性具有强烈的空间异质性。受大的光度差异、弱纹理和重复纹理的影响，多视图几何约束往往是稀疏的、分布不均匀的且局部不可靠的。尽管2D高斯泼溅（2DGS）比3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）更适合连续表面的显式表示，但目前仍缺乏用于稀疏视角卫星表面重建的可泛化前馈2DGS框架的研究。为解决这一问题，我们提出SatSurfGS，一种基于2DGS的可泛化稀疏视角卫星影像表面重建方法。该方法构建了一个粗到细的高斯属性预测框架，并在特征学习、高斯参数估计和训练优化三个层面显式建模局部几何可靠性。具体而言，我们提出：(1)置信感知单目多视图特征融合模块，根据局部置信度自适应融合单目先验与多视图匹配特征；(2)跨阶段自一致性残差引导模块，利用前一阶段渲染高度图与当前阶段MVS高度图之间的残差及置信度信息，稳定逐阶段高斯参数精化；(3)置信双向路由损失，实现几何与外观监督的差异化分配。在卫星数据集上的实验表明，与代表性可泛化基线和有竞争力的逐场景优化方法相比，所提方法在渲染质量、表面重建精度、跨数据集泛化能力和推理效率方面均取得了显著提升。

</details>


### [29] [PicoEyes: Unified Gaze Estimation Framework for Mixed Reality with a Large-Scale Multi-View Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07188)
*Fuxin Duan,Hui Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PicoEyes 是一个统一的注视估计框架，可从单目或双目输入中直接预测所有关键注视属性（3D眼部参数、眼部分割、光轴、视轴和深度图），同时支持标定、注视预测和不同设备姿态，在大规模多视角近眼数据集上实现了最先进的性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的注视估计方法通常只能预测部分注视属性，难以同时处理标定、注视预测和不同设备姿态等实际应用中的多种挑战，缺乏统一且鲁棒的端到端解决方案，特别是在混合现实（MR）应用中需要高泛化性和实用性的注视估计框架。

Method: PicoEyes 采用统一的端到端框架，通过联合估计眼部参数和深度图，从单目或双目输入中直接预测3D眼部参数、眼部分割、光轴、视轴和深度图等所有关键注视属性，并在同一框架内同时解决标定、注视预测和不同设备姿态适应问题。

Result: 在包含train、test、rewear-test和calibration会话的大规模多视角近眼数据集上，PicoEyes 在无标定、有标定、标定后重新佩戴以及注视预测等多种设置下，均一致优于学术界和工业界的注视追踪方法，达到了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一种实用、端到端的范式，为混合现实（MR）应用中的鲁棒且可泛化的注视估计提供了有效解决方案。

Original Abstract: We present PicoEyes, a unified gaze estimation framework that directly predicts all key attributes of gaze, including 3D eye parameters, eye-region segmentation, optical axis, visual axis, and depth maps, from either monocular or binocular inputs. The framework simultaneously addresses calibration, gaze forecasting, and varying device postures, while also supporting 3D eye reconstruction via joint estimation of eye parameters and depth maps in an end-to-end manner. In addition, we introduce a large-scale multi-view near-eye dataset containing comprehensive 2D and 3D annotations under diverse conditions, including train, test, rewear-test, and calibration sessions.
