Extensive experiments demonstrate that PicoEyes achieves state-ofthe-art performance, consistently outperforming both academic and industrial gaze tracking methods across nocalibration, calibration, rewear-after-calibration, and forecasting settings. This work establishes a practical, end-toend paradigm for robust and generalizable gaze estimation in mixed reality (MR) applications.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出PicoEyes，一个统一的注视估计框架，能够从单目或双目输入中直接预测所有关键注视属性，包括3D眼部参数、眼部分割、光轴、视轴和深度图。该框架同时解决了标定、注视预测和不同设备姿态等挑战，并通过联合估计眼部参数和深度图以端到端的方式支持3D眼部重建。此外，我们引入了一个大规模多视角近眼数据集，包含在多种条件下（包括训练、测试、重新佩戴测试和标定会话）的全面2D和3D标注。大量实验表明，PicoEyes实现了最先进的性能，在无标定、有标定、标定后重新佩戴以及注视预测等设置下，均一致优于学术界和工业界的注视追踪方法。这项工作为混合现实（MR）应用中鲁棒且可泛化的注视估计建立了一种实用、端到端的范式。

</details>


### [30] [Attention Transfer Is Not Universally Effective for Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07191)
*Huaiyuan Qin,Muli Yang,Gabriel James Goenawan,Peng Hu,Chen Gong,Xi Peng,Hongyuan Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现注意力迁移（Attention Transfer）并非普遍有效：在11个知名ViT家族的20个教师模型中，7个家族迁移成功，4个家族迁移失败（性能比从头训练基线低5.1%）。失败的原因是师生架构不匹配——教师原生的架构组件对于学生利用注意力模式至关重要。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 重新审视前人"注意力迁移足以恢复教师预训练权重全部收益"的结论，验证其在不同ViT家族上的普适性。

Method: 在11个ViT家族的20个教师模型上进行系统的注意力迁移实验；通过控制变量分析定位失败原因；通过添加教师原生架构组件到学生模型中消除迁移失败。

Result: 1) 7个ViT家族迁移成功，4个家族迁移失败（性能比从头训练基线低5.1%）；2) 失败具有家族一致性，且无法通过延长训练、更换数据集或OOD评估消除；3) 失败定位于注意力路由通道；4) 架构不匹配是失败的根本原因；5) 添加教师原生组件到学生模型可完全逆转失败，但组件本身对从头训练无帮助。

Conclusion: 注意力迁移的有效性依赖于师生架构匹配：只有当学生架构与教师匹配时，注意力才足以传递预训练收益。

Original Abstract: A recent work shows that Attention Transfer, which transfers only the attention patterns from a pre-trained teacher Vision Transformer (ViT) to a randomly initialized standard student ViT, is sufficient to recover the full benefit of the teacher's pre-trained weights. We revisit this finding on a comprehensive benchmark of 20 teachers from 11 well-known ViT families and reveal that Attention Transfer is not universally effective. While 7 families transfer successfully, 4 consistently fail, falling up to 5.1\% below the from-scratch no-transfer baseline. Further results demonstrate that this failure is family-consistent across model sizes, and persists under extended training durations, different transfer datasets, and out-of-distribution evaluations. Controlled analyses then consistently localize the problem to the attention-routing channel, indicating that the key issue is not whether the student can match the teacher's attention patterns, but whether the matched patterns remain functional for the student. Crucially, we identify architectural mismatch between the pre-trained teacher and the standard student as the primary mechanism. By adding only the teacher's native architectural components to the student in a randomly initialized state, we completely reverse the failure for all 4 families. Notably, these components alone do not improve from-scratch training, confirming that they specifically unlock the usability of the teacher's attention. We further systematically show that this failure is not explained by the inadequate choice of transfer loss or by differences in pre-training recipes. Our findings refine the prevailing understanding of attention in ViT representations: attention is sufficient \textit{only} when the student architecture matches the teacher.

中文摘要翻译: 最近的研究表明，注意力迁移（Attention Transfer）——仅将预训练教师视觉Transformer（ViT）的注意力模式迁移到随机初始化的标准学生ViT——就足以恢复教师预训练权重的全部收益。我们在一个包含来自11个知名ViT家族的20个教师模型的综合基准上重新审视了这一发现，并揭示出注意力迁移并非普遍有效。虽然7个家族成功迁移，但4个家族持续失败，性能比从头训练的无迁移基线低5.1%。进一步结果表明，这种失败在模型规模上具有家族一致性，并且在延长训练时长、使用不同迁移数据集以及分布外评估下依然存在。控制变量分析一致地将问题定位到注意力路由通道，表明关键问题不在于学生能否匹配教师的注意力模式，而在于匹配后的模式对学生是否仍然功能完好。至关重要的是，我们将预训练教师与标准学生之间的架构不匹配确定为主要机制。仅将教师原生的架构组件以随机初始化状态添加到学生中，我们就完全逆转了所有4个家族的迁移失败。值得注意的是，这些组件本身并不能改善从头训练的性能，这证实了它们专门解锁了教师注意力的可用性。我们进一步系统地证明，这种失败既不能用迁移损失的选择不当来解释，也不能用预训练配方的差异来解释。我们的研究结果修正了当前关于ViT表示中注意力的理解：只有当学生架构与教师匹配时，注意力才足以实现迁移。

</details>


### [31] [AsyncEvGS: Asynchronous Event-Assisted Gaussian Splatting for Handheld Motion-Blurred Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07192)
*Jun Dai,Renbiao Jin,Bo Xu,Yutian Chen,Linning Xu,Mulin Yu,Tianfan Xue,Shi Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种灵活的高分辨率异步RGB-事件双相机系统及重建框架，通过事件数据重建清晰图像、跨域位姿估计和结构化事件损失，在严重运动模糊条件下实现了鲁棒的3D重建，并贡献了AsyncEv-Deblur数据集。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有3D重建方法（如3DGS和NeRF）在输入图像存在严重运动模糊时效果不佳；而现有事件辅助方法依赖低分辨率传感器和严格同步，限制了其在手持设备（如智能手机）上的实用性。

Method: 1) 从事件数据重建清晰图像；2) 基于VGGT的跨域位姿估计模块，为3DGS提供鲁棒初始化；3) 采用结构化事件损失和视角一致性正则化，缓解传统事件损失和去模糊损失的病态问题。

Result: 在自建的AsyncEv-Deblur高分辨率RGB-事件数据集和现有基准上均达到最先进性能，在严重运动模糊下显著提升了重建鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 所提出的异步RGB-事件双相机系统及重建框架有效解决了运动模糊下的3D重建难题，为手持设备上的高质量3D捕获提供了实用方案。

Original Abstract: 3D reconstruction methods such as 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) and Neural Radiance Fields (NeRF) achieve impressive photorealism but fail when input images suffer from severe motion blur. While event cameras provide high-temporal-resolution motion cues, existing event-assisted approaches rely on low-resolution sensors and strict synchronization, limiting their practicality for handheld 3D capture on common devices, such as smartphones. We introduce a flexible, high-resolution asynchronous RGB-Event dual-camera system and a corresponding reconstruction framework. Our approach first reconstructs sharp images from the event data and then employs a cross-domain pose estimation module based on the Visual Geometry Transformer (VGGT) to obtain robust initialization for 3DGS. During optimization, we employ a structure-driven event loss and view-specific consistency regularizers to mitigate the ill-posed behavior of traditional event losses and deblurring losses, ensuring both stable and high-fidelity reconstruction. We further contribute AsyncEv-Deblur, a new high-resolution RGB-Event dataset captured with our asynchronous system. Experiments demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance on both our challenging dataset and existing benchmarks, substantially improving reconstruction robustness under severe motion blur. Project page: https://openimaginglab.github.io/AsyncEvGS/

中文摘要翻译: 三维重建方法（如3D高斯泼溅和神经辐射场）在输入图像存在严重运动模糊时虽然能实现令人印象深刻的光真实感，但会失效。虽然事件相机能提供高时间分辨率的运动线索，但现有事件辅助方法依赖于低分辨率传感器和严格同步，限制了它们在常见设备（如智能手机）上进行手持三维捕获的实用性。我们引入了一种灵活的高分辨率异步RGB-事件双相机系统及相应的重建框架。我们的方法首先从事件数据重建清晰图像，然后基于视觉几何变换器（VGGT）采用跨域位姿估计模块，为3DGS获得鲁棒的初始化。在优化过程中，我们采用结构化事件损失和视角特定一致性正则化，以缓解传统事件损失和去模糊损失的病态行为，确保稳定且高保真的重建。我们进一步贡献了AsyncEv-Deblur，一个使用我们的异步系统捕获的高分辨率RGB-事件新数据集。实验表明，我们的方法在我们具有挑战性的数据集和现有基准上均达到了最先进的性能，在严重运动模糊下显著提高了重建鲁棒性。项目页面：https://openimaginglab.github.io/AsyncEvGS/

</details>


### [32] [See Tomorrow, Act Today: Foresight-Driven Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07195)
*Bozhou Zhang,Nan Song,Yuang Wang,Jiankang Deng,Xiatian Zhu,Li Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ForeSight提出了一种以基础世界模型为核心的自动驾驶规划框架，通过先想象未来场景再决策的方式，将自动驾驶从反应式规划转变为预期式规划，在NAVSIM和nuScenes上取得了SOTA性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有端到端自动驾驶规划器本质上是反应式的，仅依赖历史和当前观测来预测未来动作。作者认为自动驾驶智能体应像人类驾驶员一样，在行动前先模拟“接下来会发生什么”，从而实现真正的预期式决策。

Method: ForeSight采用两阶段框架：(1) 利用预训练的世界模型生成合理的未来视觉世界；(2) 在这些想象出的未来场景条件下进行动作规划。核心是将未来场景想象作为动作预测的主要驱动因素，而非辅助组件。

Result: 在NAVSIM和nuScenes上的大量实验表明，显式的未来想象方法显著优于之前的SOTA方法，验证了预期驱动方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 将决策建立在预期情境而非仅当前观测的基础上，能够更有效地导航动态、交互式场景，实现从“我现在该做什么？”到“将会发生什么，我该如何应对？”的范式转变。

Original Abstract: Current end-to-end autonomous driving planners are fundamentally reactive: they condition on historical and present observations to predict future actions. We argue that autonomous agents should instead imagine future scenes before deciding, just as human drivers mentally simulate ``what will happen next" before acting. We introduce ForeSight, a foundation world model centric planning framework that reframes autonomous driving as anticipatory decision-making. Rather than treating world models as auxiliary components, ForeSight makes future scene imagination the primary driver of action prediction. Our approach operates in two stages: (1) generating plausible future visual worlds via a pretrained world model, and (2) planning actions conditioned on these imagined futures. This paradigm shift from ``what should I do now?" to ``what will happen, and how should I respond?" enables genuinely anticipatory rather than reactive planning. By grounding decisions in anticipated contexts rather than present observations alone, ForeSight navigates dynamic, interactive scenarios more effectively. Extensive experiments on NAVSIM and nuScenes demonstrate that explicit future imagination significantly outperforms previous state-of-the-art alternatives, validating our foresight-driven approach.

中文摘要翻译: 当前的端到端自动驾驶规划器本质上是反应式的：它们以历史和当前观测为条件来预测未来动作。我们认为自动驾驶智能体应在决策之前想象未来场景，就像人类驾驶员在行动前会在大脑中模拟“接下来会发生什么”。我们提出了ForeSight，一个以基础世界模型为核心的规划框架，将自动驾驶重新定义为预期式决策。ForeSight不是将世界模型视为辅助组件，而是将未来场景想象作为动作预测的主要驱动因素。我们的方法分两个阶段进行：(1) 通过预训练的世界模型生成合理的未来视觉世界；(2) 在这些想象出的未来条件下规划动作。这种从“我现在该做什么？”到“将会发生什么，我该如何应对？”的范式转变，实现了真正的预期式而非反应式规划。通过将决策建立在预期情境而非仅当前观测的基础上，ForeSight能够更有效地导航动态、交互式场景。在NAVSIM和nuScenes上的大量实验表明，显式的未来想象显著优于之前的最先进方法，验证了我们的预期驱动方法的有效性。

</details>


### [33] [From Pixels to Primitives: Scene Change Detection in 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07203)
*Chamuditha Jayanga Galappaththige,Jason Lai,Timothy Patten,Donald Dansereau,Niko Suenderhauf,Dimity Miller*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GS-DIFF方法，直接在3D高斯原语空间而非渲染后的像素空间进行场景变化检测，利用原语固有属性（位置、各向异性协方差、颜色）结合各向异性漂移模型和可观测性项，实现多视图一致且区分几何与外观变化的变化检测，在真实基准上mIoU提升约17%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于高斯泼溅的场景变化检测方法遵循"先渲染再比较"的像素级范式，但该方法存在两个问题：多视图一致性需要额外优化目标来学习；无法区分几何变化与外观变化的类型。论文旨在直接在3D原语空间进行比较，以解决上述问题。

Method: 提出GS-DIFF方法：(1) 直接利用高斯原语的固有属性（位置、各向异性协方差、颜色）进行变化检测；(2) 构建各向异性几何漂移模型和光度漂移模型来应对原语空间比较中因独立优化导致的差异；(3) 引入逐原语可观测性项，反映每个高斯体受相机几何约束的程度；(4) 分别对几何变化和外观变化进行评分，实现变化类型的区分。

Result: 在真实世界基准数据集上，GS-DIFF相比先前最先进的方法，平均交并比（mIoU）提升了约17%。

Conclusion: 直接在3D原语空间进行场景变化检测是可行且优越的，能够天然实现多视图一致性，并能无监督地区分结构变化与表面变化，显著优于像素级渲染后比较的方法。

Original Abstract: Scene change detection methods built on Gaussian splatting universally follow a render-then-compare paradigm: the pre-change scene is rendered into 2D and compared against post-change images via pixel or feature residuals. This change detection problem with Gaussian Splatting has been treated as a question about pixels; we treat it as a question about primitives. We provide direct evidence that native primitive attributes alone -- position, anisotropic covariance, and color -- carry sufficient signal for scene change detection. What makes primitive-space comparison hard is the under-constrained nature of Gaussian splatting representation: independent optimizations yield primitive solutions whose count, positions, shapes, and colors differ even where nothing has changed. We address this challenge with anisotropic models of geometric and photometric drift, complemented by a per-primitive observability term that reflects the extent to which each Gaussian is constrained by the camera geometry. Operating directly on primitives gives our method, GD-DIFF, two properties that distinguish it from render-then-compare methods. First, change maps are multi-view consistent by construction, where prior work had to learn this through an additional optimization objective. Second, geometric and appearance changes are scored separately, identifying not just where but what kind of change occurred, distinguishing structural changes (e.g., an added object) from surface-level ones (e.g., a color change) without supervision or external model dependencies. On real-world benchmarks, GS-DIFF surpasses the prior state-of-the-art approach by approximatelt 17% in mean Intersection over Union.

中文摘要翻译: 基于高斯泼溅的场景变化检测方法普遍遵循"先渲染再比较"的范式：将变化前的场景渲染为2D图像，然后通过像素或特征残差与变化后的图像进行比较。这种基于高斯泼溅的变化检测问题一直被视为像素层面的问题；而我们将其视为原语层面的问题。我们提供直接证据表明，原语自身的固有属性——位置、各向异性协方差和颜色——携带了足够的信号用于场景变化检测。原语空间比较的难点在于高斯泼溅表示本身的欠约束特性：独立的优化会产生在原语数量、位置、形状和颜色上均有差异的原始解，即使场景未发生任何变化。我们通过各向异性的几何漂移和光度漂移模型来应对这一挑战，并辅以逐原语的可观测性项，该反映了每个高斯体受相机几何约束的程度。直接在原语上操作使我们的方法GS-DIFF具备两个区别于"先渲染再比较"方法的特性。第一，变化图天然具有多视图一致性，而先前的工作需要通过额外的优化目标来学习这一点。第二，几何变化和外观变化被分别评分，不仅能识别变化发生的位置，还能识别变化的类型，无需监督或外部模型依赖即可区分结构变化（例如新增物体）与表面变化（例如颜色变化）。在真实世界基准数据集上，GS-DIFF的平均交并比相比先前最先进的方法提升了约17%。

</details>


### [34] [CASCADE: Context-Aware Relaxation for Speculative Image Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07230)
*Selin Yildirim,Subhajit Dutta Chowdhury,Mohammad Mahdi Kamani,Vikram Appia,Deming Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CASCADE方法，通过利用树状推测解码中目标模型隐藏状态表示的冗余性（语义互换性与收敛性），在不需额外训练的情况下放宽接受条件，并改进草稿模型训练，在文本到图像生成任务中实现最高3.6倍加速，同时保持图像质量与文本保真度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 自回归图像合成虽然效果优异但计算成本高昂、速度缓慢。现有推测解码方法在图像生成中因目标模型不确定性高、草稿令牌拒绝率高而未能达到文本生成中的加速效果，亟需更高效的加速策略。

Method: 识别并形式化了树状推测解码中目标模型隐藏状态表示的两种冗余属性——语义互换性（semantic interchangeability）与收敛性（convergence）；利用这些冗余性在预测令牌树的深度和广度上实现无需额外训练的接受条件放宽；同时将目标模型的冗余信号注入草稿模型训练中以提升其性能。

Result: 在多种文本到图像模型和草稿架构上，CASCADE在基于草稿模型的推测解码中取得了最优加速效果，最高可达3.6倍加速，同时保持了图像质量和文本提示保真度。

Conclusion: 通过挖掘目标模型隐藏状态中的固有冗余模式，CASCADE有效解决了图像生成中推测解码效率低下的问题，为自回归图像合成提供了显著的加速方案。

Original Abstract: Autoregressive generation is a powerful approach for high-fidelity image synthesis, but it remains computationally demanding and slow even on the most advanced accelerators. While speculative decoding has been explored to mitigate this bottleneck, existing approaches fail to achieve efficiency gains comparable to those observed in text generation. A key limitation is the target model's high uncertainty during image generation, which leads to high draft token rejection rates. In this work, we identify previously overlooked patterns in the target model's behavior that emerge naturally in tree-based speculative decoding. Specifically, we formalize two properties, semantic interchangeability and convergence, arising from the redundancies in the target model's hidden state representations. By capturing these redundancies across the depth and breadth of the predicted token tree, our method identifies principled opportunities for acceptance relaxation without requiring additional training. Additionally, we enhance standalone drafter performance by injecting the redundancy signals from the target model into drafter training with minimal modification. We evaluate our approach across multiple text-to-image models and drafter architectures. Results show that CASCADE achieves state-of-the-art speedups for drafter-based speculative decoding, with up to 3.6x acceleration, while maintaining image quality and text-prompt fidelity.

中文摘要翻译: 自回归生成是一种实现高保真图像合成的强大方法，但即使在最先进的加速器上，它仍然计算密集且速度缓慢。虽然推测解码已被探索用于缓解这一瓶颈，但现有方法未能达到与文本生成中相当的效率提升。一个关键限制是图像生成过程中目标模型的高不确定性，导致草稿令牌拒绝率居高不下。在这项工作中，我们识别出在树状推测解码中自然产生的、目标模型行为中先前被忽视的模式。具体而言，我们形式化了两种属性——语义互换性与收敛性——它们源自目标模型隐藏状态表示中的冗余性。通过捕获这些在预测令牌树深度和广度上的冗余性，我们的方法识别出无需额外训练即可放宽接受条件的原则性机会。此外，我们通过将目标模型的冗余信号以最小修改注入草稿模型训练中，增强了独立草稿模型的性能。我们在多种文本到图像模型和草稿架构上评估了我们的方法。结果表明，CASCADE在基于草稿模型的推测解码中实现了最先进的加速效果，最高可达3.6倍加速，同时保持了图像质量和文本提示保真度。

</details>


### [35] [Towards multi-modal forgery representation learning for AI-generated video detection and localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07232)
*Dat Le,Khoa Nguyen,Xin Wang,Shu Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种多模态架构，联合LMM语义分支、时空视觉分支和多尺度部分伪造音频分支，实现对AI生成视频中部分伪造内容的检测与时序定位，性能优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有AI生成视频检测器大多依赖单一或部分模态的数据建模，且缺乏细粒度的时间伪造定位能力，无法应对日益复杂的多模态、部分伪造视频带来的语义扭曲与滥用风险。

Method: 提出一种核心架构，联合集成三个分支：(1) LMM语义分支，负责语义层面的分析；(2) 时空视觉分支，捕获视频中的时空特征；(3) 多尺度部分伪造音频分支，处理音频片段中的局部伪造。三分支协同实现多模态检测与细粒度时序定位。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法在检测和时序定位性能上均优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 多模态联合建模（语义、时空视觉、音频）能够有效提升AI生成视频中部分伪造内容的检测与时间定位能力，为视频伪造检测提供了更全面的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Recent advances in generative AI have democratized video creation at scale. AI-generated videos, including partially manipulated clips across visual and audio channels, pose escalating risks of semantic distortion and misuse, which motivates the need for reliable detection tools. Most existing AI-generated video detectors remain limited by single- or partial-modality of data modeling and the lack of fine-grained temporal forgery localization. To address these challenges, our primary novelty introduces a core architecture that jointly integrates an LMM semantic branch with a spatio-temporal (ST) visual branch and a multi-scale partial-spoof (PS) audio branch. This multi-modal approach enables simultaneous detection and fine-grained temporal localization of partially manipulated AI-generated video forgeries. Extensive experiments show that this approach outperforms existing state-of-the-art methods.

中文摘要翻译: 生成式AI的最新进展已使大规模视频创作变得普及。AI生成的视频，包括跨视觉和音频通道的部分篡改片段，带来了日益严重的语义扭曲和滥用风险，这推动了对可靠检测工具的需求。大多数现有的AI生成视频检测器仍然受限于单模态或部分模态的数据建模，且缺乏细粒度的时间伪造定位能力。为应对这些挑战，我们的主要创新在于引入了一个核心架构，该架构联合集成了LMM语义分支、时空视觉分支和多尺度部分伪造音频分支。这种多模态方法能够同时检测并细粒度地定位部分篡改的AI生成视频伪造。大量实验表明，该方法在性能上优于现有最先进方法。

</details>


### [36] [Hard to Read, Easy to Jailbreak: How Visual Degradation Bypasses MLLM Safety Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07250)
*Zhixue Song,Boyan Han,Yiwei Wang,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了视觉上下文压缩的一种关键安全漏洞：降低图像分辨率会意外地削弱多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的安全防御机制，并提出了“结构化认知卸载”策略来缓解这一风险。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有工作将文本渲染为图像以实现视觉上下文压缩，但作者发现降低分辨率会意外地催化“越狱”攻击（jailbreaking），使模型的安全防御急剧下降，亟需揭示这一漏洞并提出应对方案。

Method: 提出“结构化认知卸载”（Structured Cognitive Offloading）策略，通过强制实施串行化流水线，将视觉转录与安全评估解耦，从而降低认知负载对安全审计的干扰。

Result: 实验表明，随着分辨率下降，SOTA模型的安全防御能力急剧恶化，即使在文本仍可阅读的情况下也持续存在；该现象在噪声、几何畸变等多种视觉扰动下均一致出现。提出的策略能有效缓解这一风险。

Conclusion: 该工作暴露了基于视觉的上下文压缩方法的重大安全风险，并为未来多模态大语言模型的安全设计提供了关键见解。

Original Abstract: Recent advancements in visual context compression enable MLLMs to process ultra-long contexts efficiently by rendering text into images. However, we identify a critical vulnerability inherent to this paradigm: lowering image resolution inadvertently catalyzes jailbreaking. Our experiments reveal that the safety defenses of SOTA models deteriorate sharply as resolution degrades, surprisingly persisting even when text remains legible. We attribute this to ``Cognitive Overload'', hypothesizing that the effort required to decipher degraded inputs diverts attentional resources from safety auditing. This phenomenon is consistent across various visual perturbations, including noise and geometric distortion. To address this, we propose a simple ``Structured Cognitive Offloading'' strategy that mitigates these risks by enforcing a serialized pipeline to decouple visual transcription from safety assessment. Our work exposes a significant risk in vision-based compression and provides critical insights for the secure design of future MLLMs.

中文摘要翻译: 近期视觉上下文压缩的进展使得多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）能够通过将文本渲染为图像来高效处理超长上下文。然而，我们识别出该范式存在一个关键的安全漏洞：降低图像分辨率会意外地催化越狱攻击。实验表明，随着分辨率下降，最先进模型的安全防御能力急剧恶化，且令人惊讶的是，即使文本仍可阅读时该现象依然持续。我们将其归因于“认知过载”（Cognitive Overload），假设解码退化输入所需的努力会转移安全审计的注意力资源。这一现象在包括噪声和几何畸变在内的各种视觉扰动中均保持一致。为解决该问题，我们提出了一种简单的“结构化认知卸载”（Structured Cognitive Offloading）策略，通过强制实施串行化流水线将视觉转录与安全评估解耦，从而缓解这些风险。我们的工作暴露了基于视觉的压缩方法的重大风险，并为未来多模态大语言模型的安全设计提供了关键见解。

</details>


### [37] [High-Fidelity Surface Splatting-Based 3D Reconstruction from Multi-View Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07254)
*Nandhana Sunil,Abhirami R Iyer,Avirup Mandal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对多视图网格重建中高频几何细节恢复难题，提出了一种基于紧凑多项式核的隐式移动最小二乘（IMLS）方法，结合随机正则化与拉普拉斯滤波，实现了更高质量的表面重建与渲染。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有3DGS和NeRF等方法依赖后处理进行网格提取，限制了几何与外观的联合优化；而现有IMLS方法使用指数核函数，难以恢复高频细节。因此需要一种能更好保留高频几何信息且支持端到端优化的重建方法。

Method: 1) 提出一种紧凑支撑的多项式核替代传统指数核，具有更大的灵活性，能更好地控制频域内容并提升几何保真度；2) 引入基于拉普拉斯滤波的随机正则化，进一步增强高频细节的保持能力，同时保持优化过程的稳定性。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在表面重建和渲染任务上均达到最先进水平，从多视图数据中重建出更精确的几何结构和更清晰的视觉效果。

Conclusion: 所提出的紧凑多项式核IMLS方法结合随机正则化，能有效解决高频几何细节恢复问题，在多视图网格重建与渲染领域取得了优于现有方法的性能。

Original Abstract: Multi-view mesh reconstruction remains a core challenge in computer graphics and vision, especially for recovering high-frequency geometry from sparse observations. Recent methods such as 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) and Neural Radiance Fields (NeRF) rely on post-processing for mesh extraction, thereby limiting joint optimization of geometry and appearance. Implicit Moving Least Squares (IMLS) instead enables direct conversion of point clouds into signed distance and texture fields, supporting end-to-end reconstruction and rendering. However, existing IMLS formulations use exponential kernels that struggle with high-frequency detail. We introduce a compact polynomial kernel with local support and greater flexibility, allowing better control over frequency content and improved geometric fidelity. To further enhance fine details, we incorporate stochastic regularization with Laplacian filtering. Together, these improve the preservation of high-frequency structure while maintaining stable optimization. Experiments show state-of-the-art performance in both surface reconstruction and rendering, yielding more accurate geometry and sharper visuals from multi-view data.

中文摘要翻译: 多视图网格重建仍然是计算机图形学和视觉领域的核心挑战，尤其是在从稀疏观测中恢复高频几何信息方面。近期方法如3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）和神经辐射场（NeRF）依赖后处理进行网格提取，从而限制了几何与外观的联合优化。隐式移动最小二乘（IMLS）方法则能将点云直接转化为符号距离场和纹理场，支持端到端的重建与渲染。然而，现有IMLS公式使用指数核函数，难以处理高频细节。我们引入了一种具有局部支撑且更灵活的紧凑多项式核，能够更好地控制频域内容并提升几何保真度。为进一步增强细粒度细节，我们引入了结合拉普拉斯滤波的随机正则化。这些改进共同提升了高频结构的保持能力，同时维持了优化的稳定性。实验表明，该方法在表面重建和渲染任务上均达到最先进水平，从多视图数据中重建出更精确的几何结构和更清晰的视觉效果。

</details>


### [38] [TAS-LoRA: Transformer Architecture Search with Mixture-of-LoRA Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07256)
*Jeimin Jeon,Hyunju Lee,Bumsub Ham*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TAS-LoRA通过引入参数高效的低秩适配（LoRA）和混合LoRA专家（MoLE）策略，解决了视觉Transformer架构搜索中由于权重共享导致的特征坍塌问题，显著提升了子网性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有TAS方法因超网中权重共享导致特征坍塌问题，子网无法学习子网特异性特征，限制了个体子网的性能。

Method: 提出TAS-LoRA方法：引入参数高效的低秩适配（LoRA）实现子网特异性特征学习；采用混合LoRA专家（MoLE）策略，通过轻量级路由器根据子网架构动态分配LoRA专家；引入分组式路由器初始化技术，促进训练早期各专家的多样化特征学习。

Result: 在ImageNet及CIFAR-10/100、Flowers、CARS、INAT-19等多个迁移学习基准上，TAS-LoRA有效缓解了特征坍塌，性能显著优于现有最先进TAS方法。

Conclusion: TAS-LoRA通过LoRA和MoLE策略有效解决了TAS中的特征坍塌问题，在保持计算效率的同时显著提升了子网性能。

Original Abstract: Transformer architecture search (TAS) discovers optimal vision transformer (ViT) architectures automatically, reducing human effort to manually design ViTs. However, existing TAS methods suffer from the feature collapse problem, where subnets within a supernet fail to learn subnet-specific features, mainly due to the shared weights in a supernet, limiting the performance of individual subnets. To address this, we propose TAS-LoRA, a novel method that introduces parameter-efficient low-rank adaptation (LoRA) to enable subnet-specific feature learning, while maintaining computational efficiency. TAS-LoRA incorporates a Mixture-of-LoRAExperts (MoLE) strategy, where a lightweight router dynamically assigns LoRA experts based on subnet architectures, and introduces a group-wise router initialization technique to encourage diverse feature learning across experts early in training. Extensive experiments on ImageNet and several transfer learning benchmarks, including CIFAR-10/100, Flowers, CARS, and INAT-19, demonstrate that TAS-LoRA mitigates feature collapse effectively, improving performance over state-of-the-art TAS methods significantly.

中文摘要翻译: Transformer架构搜索（TAS）能够自动发现最优的视觉Transformer（ViT）架构，减少了人工设计ViT的工作量。然而，现有的TAS方法存在特征坍塌问题，即超网中的子网由于权重共享而无法学习子网特异性特征，这限制了个体子网的性能。为解决这一问题，我们提出TAS-LoRA，一种新颖的方法，引入参数高效的低秩适配（LoRA）以实现子网特异性特征学习，同时保持计算效率。TAS-LoRA采用了混合LoRA专家（MoLE）策略，其中轻量级路由器根据子网架构动态分配LoRA专家，并引入分组式路由器初始化技术，以在训练早期促进各专家的多样化特征学习。在ImageNet以及包括CIFAR-10/100、Flowers、CARS和INAT-19在内的多个迁移学习基准上的大量实验表明，TAS-LoRA有效缓解了特征坍塌问题，性能显著优于现有的最先进TAS方法。

</details>


### [39] [Adaptive Subspace Projection for Generative Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07257)
*Van-Anh Nguyen,Anh Tuan Bui,Tamas Abraham,Junae Kim,Amardeep Kaur,Rollin Omari,Thuy-Trang Vu,Dinh Phung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AdaptSP，一种无需训练的方法，通过自适应子空间投影在测试时调整嵌入，以解决生成式个性化中的语义坍塌问题（SCP），在不损失主体身份的前提下提升提示保真度与上下文对齐。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 生成式个性化中，学习到的个性化概念会压制文本提示中的其他上下文信息，导致语义坍塌问题（SCP）。本文旨在分析SCP的成因并提出有效的解决方法。

Method: 首先分析发现SCP背后的语义漂移并非随机，而是集中在特定的低维子空间中，且个性化过程扰乱了原始基础概念的嵌入，使其成为不稳定的参考点。基于此，提出无需训练的测试时嵌入调整方法AdaptSP，利用稳定的预训练嵌入作为锚点，隔离语义漂移并将其投影到识别出的子空间上，进行精准调整以缓解SCP同时保持主体身份。

Result: 实验表明，这种针对性方法显著提升了提示保真度和上下文对齐能力。

Conclusion: AdaptSP通过子空间投影精准调整嵌入，有效解决了生成式个性化中的语义坍塌问题，在保持主体身份的同时大幅改善了对文本提示的忠实度。

Original Abstract: Generative personalization often suffers from the semantic collapsing problem (SCP), where a learned personalized concept overpowers the rest of the text prompt, causing the model to ignore important contextual details. To address this, we first analyze the underlying cause, revealing that the semantic drift responsible for SCP is not random but is concentrated within a specific low-dimensional subspace. We also discover that the personalization process perturbs the embedding of the original base concept, making it an unstable reference point. Based on these insights, we introduce Test-time Embedding Adjustment with Adaptive Subspace Projection (AdaptSP), a training-free method that uses the stable, pre-trained embedding as an anchor. AdaptSP isolates the semantic drift and projects it onto the identified subspace, performing a precise adjustment that mitigates SCP while maintaining the subject identity. Our experiments show that this targeted approach significantly improves prompt fidelity and contextual alignment.

中文摘要翻译: 生成式个性化经常遭受语义坍塌问题（SCP）的困扰，即学习到的个性化概念压制了文本提示中的其余部分，导致模型忽略重要的上下文细节。为解决此问题，我们首先分析了其根本原因，揭示出导致SCP的语义漂移并非随机，而是集中在特定的低维子空间中。我们还发现，个性化过程扰乱了原始基础概念的嵌入，使其成为一个不稳定的参考点。基于这些洞察，我们提出了具有自适应子空间投影的测试时嵌入调整方法（AdaptSP），这是一种无需训练的方法，利用稳定的预训练嵌入作为锚点。AdaptSP隔离语义漂移并将其投影到识别出的子空间上，进行精准调整，从而在保持主体身份的同时缓解SCP。我们的实验表明，这种针对性方法显著提升了提示保真度和上下文对齐能力。

</details>


### [40] [From Clouds to Hallucinations: Atmospheric Retrieval Hijacking in Remote Sensing Vision-Language RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07273)
*Jiaju Han,Chao Li,Chengyin Hu,Qike Zhang,Xuemeng Sun,Xin Wang,Fengyu Zhang,Xiang Chen,Yiwei Wei,Jiahuan Long,Jiujiang Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CloudWeb，一种针对遥感多模态RAG系统检索阶段的大气检索劫持攻击，通过在输入图像上叠加参数化的云/雾模式并优化检索导向目标，使检索结果被劫持到目标大气证据上，首次揭示了自然外观的大气变化可在生成前破坏证据检索的安全隐患。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有对抗性研究主要操纵RAG的检索语料库或内存，或直接攻击视觉-语言和遥感模型的最终预测任务，而针对遥感多模态RAG系统证据检索阶段的输入空间攻击尚未被充分探索。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: CloudWeb在遥感图像上叠加参数化的云/雾模式，并通过检索导向目标进行优化，该目标包括：将对抗性图像嵌入拉向目标大气证据、抑制源场景证据、强制排名分离，以及正则化自然度和覆盖率。攻击过程中保持检索器、生成器和知识库固定不变。

Result: 在包含七个数据集、五种CLIP风格检索器（GeoRSCLIP, RemoteCLIP, OpenAI CLIP, OpenCLIP）的遥感RAG基准上，CloudWeb持续优于干净检索、手工大气基线、随机云扰动和固定变体。在GeoRSCLIP ViT-B/32上，Weather@5从0.71%提升至43.29%。下游生成出现可测量的天气幻觉和语义偏移。

Conclusion: 自然外观的大气变化可以在生成之前破坏遥感多模态RAG系统的证据检索，且检索阶段的劫持会传播到最终的RAG响应，揭示了一个实际的安全失效模式。

Original Abstract: Multimodal RAG systems increasingly rely on vision-language retrievers to ground visual queries in external textual evidence. Existing adversarial studies on RAG mainly manipulate the retrieval corpus or memory, while attacks on vision-language and remote sensing models typically target end-task predictions. Input-space threats to the evidence retrieval stage of remote sensing multimodal RAG remain underexplored. To address this gap, we introduce CloudWeb, an atmospheric retrieval hijacking attack that modifies only the input image while keeping the retriever, generator, and knowledge base fixed at deployment. CloudWeb overlays parameterized cloud- and haze-like patterns on remote sensing images and optimizes them with a retrieval-oriented objective that pulls adversarial image embeddings toward target atmospheric evidence, suppresses source-scene evidence, enforces rank separation, and regularizes naturalness and coverage. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first study of retrieval-stage atmospheric evidence hijacking in remote sensing multimodal RAG. We evaluate CloudWeb on a seven-dataset remote sensing RAG benchmark with five CLIP-style retrievers, including GeoRSCLIP, RemoteCLIP, OpenAI CLIP, and OpenCLIP, together with downstream vision-language generators. Across retrievers, CloudWeb consistently outperforms clean retrieval, handcrafted atmospheric baselines, random cloud perturbations, and fixed variants in injecting weather-related evidence into top-ranked results. On GeoRSCLIP ViT-B/32, Weather@5 increases from 0.71\% to 43.29\%. Downstream generation further shows measurable weather hallucination and semantic shift, indicating that retrieval-stage hijacking can propagate to the final RAG response. These findings reveal a practical failure mode: natural-looking atmospheric changes can compromise evidence retrieval before generation begins.

中文摘要翻译: 多模态检索增强生成（RAG）系统越来越依赖视觉-语言检索器来基于视觉查询从外部文本证据中进行检索。现有的RAG对抗性研究主要操纵检索语料库或记忆，而对视觉-语言和遥感模型的攻击通常针对最终任务预测。针对遥感多模态RAG系统证据检索阶段的输入空间威胁尚未得到充分探索。为填补这一空白，我们提出了CloudWeb——一种大气检索劫持攻击，仅修改输入图像，同时保持检索器、生成器和知识库在部署时固定不变。CloudWeb在遥感图像上叠加参数化的云和雾状模式，并通过检索导向目标进行优化，该目标将对抗性图像嵌入拉向目标大气证据、抑制源场景证据、强制排名分离，并对自然度和覆盖范围进行正则化。据我们所知，这是首个针对遥感多模态RAG中检索阶段大气证据劫持的研究。我们在包含七个数据集的遥感RAG基准上，使用五种CLIP风格检索器（包括GeoRSCLIP、RemoteCLIP、OpenAI CLIP和OpenCLIP）以及下游视觉-语言生成器评估了CloudWeb。在所有检索器上，CloudWeb在将天气相关证据注入排名靠前的结果方面，持续优于干净检索、手工大气基线、随机云扰动和固定变体。在GeoRSCLIP ViT-B/32上，Weather@5从0.71%提升到43.29%。下游生成进一步显示出可测量的天气幻觉和语义偏移，表明检索阶段的劫持可以传播到最终的RAG响应。这些发现揭示了一个实际失效模式：自然外观的大气变化可以在生成开始之前破坏证据检索。

</details>


### [41] [SplatWeaver: Learning to Allocate Gaussian Primitives for Generalizable Novel View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07287)
*Yecong Wan,Fan Li,Mingwen Shao,Wangmeng Zuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SplatWeaver提出了一种可泛化的新视角合成框架，通过像素级路由机制和基数高斯专家动态分配高斯原语数量，在减少冗余原语的同时提升复杂区域的表示能力，在多个场景下优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于3D高斯泼溅的前馈方法为每个像素或体素分配固定数量的高斯原语，忽略了真实场景中不同区域的空间复杂度差异，导致平滑区域浪费原语而精细结构、复杂几何和高频细节区域表示能力不足。

Method: 提出SplatWeaver框架，包含：(1) 基数高斯专家（cardinality Gaussian experts），每个专家专门生成0到M个特定数量的原语；(2) 像素级路由方案，协调各专家自适应决定每个空间位置应分配的高斯原语数量；(3) 高频先验及伴随的引导模块和路由正则化，稳定专家选择并促进复杂度感知的分配。

Result: 在多种场景下的广泛实验表明，SplatWeaver持续优于最先进方法，能够使用更少的高斯原语生成更忠实的新视角渲染结果。

Conclusion: 通过动态分配高斯原语而非固定预算，能够在保持紧凑表示的同时提升复杂区域的渲染质量，验证了区域自适应的原语分配策略的有效性。

Original Abstract: Generalizable novel view synthesis aims to render unseen views from uncalibrated input images without requiring per-scene optimization. Recent feed-forward approaches based on 3D Gaussian Splatting have achieved promising efficiency and rendering quality. However, most of them assign a fixed number of Gaussians to each pixel or voxel, ignoring the spatially varying complexity of real-world scenes. Such uniform allocation often wastes Gaussian primitives in smooth regions while providing insufficient capacity for fine structures, complex geometry, and high-frequency details. This motivates us to predict region-dependent primitive cardinalities rather than impose a fixed primitive budget everywhere, enabling a more expressive yet compact 3D scene representation. Therefore, we propose SplatWeaver, a generalizable novel view synthesis framework that is able to dynamically allocate Gaussian primitives over different regions in a feed-forward manner. Specifically, SplatWeaver introduces cardinality Gaussian experts and a pixel-level routing scheme, wherein each expert specializes in producing a specific number of primitives from 0 to M, and the routing scheme coordinates these experts to adaptively determine how many Gaussian primitives should be allocated to each spatial location. Moreover, SplatWeaver incorporates a high-frequency prior with attendant guidance module and routing regularization to stabilize expert selection and promote complexity-aware allocation. By leveraging high-frequency structural cues, the routing process is encouraged to assign more Gaussian primitives to fine structures, complex geometry, and textured regions, while suppressing redundant primitives in smooth areas. Extensive experiments across diverse scenarios show that SplatWeaver consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods, delivering more faithful novel-view renderings with fewer Gaussian primitives.

中文摘要翻译: 可泛化的新视角合成旨在无需每场景优化的情况下，从无标定输入图像渲染未见过的视角。基于3D高斯泼溅的近期前馈方法已取得有前景的效率和渲染质量。然而，大多数方法为每个像素或体素分配固定数量的高斯原语，忽略了真实场景中空间变化的复杂度。这种均匀分配常在平滑区域浪费高斯原语，同时无法为精细结构、复杂几何和高频细节提供足够的容量。这促使我们预测区域依赖的原语基数，而非在处处施加固定原语预算，从而能够实现更具表达力且更紧凑的3D场景表示。因此，我们提出SplatWeaver——一种可泛化的新视角合成框架，能够以前馈方式在不同区域上动态分配高斯原语。具体而言，SplatWeaver引入了基数高斯专家和像素级路由方案，其中每个专家专门生成从0到M个特定数量的原语，路由方案协调这些专家自适应地决定每个空间位置应分配多少个高斯原语。此外，SplatWeaver结合了高频先验及其伴随的引导模块和路由正则化，以稳定专家选择并促进复杂度感知的分配。通过利用高频结构线索，路由过程被鼓励将更多高斯原语分配给精细结构、复杂几何和纹理区域，同时抑制平滑区域中的冗余原语。在多种场景下的广泛实验表明，SplatWeaver持续优于最先进方法，能够使用更少的高斯原语生成更忠实的新视角渲染结果。

</details>


### [42] [Sword: Style-Robust World Models as Simulators via Dynamic Latent Bootstrapping for VLA Policy Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07288)
*Jiaxuan Gao,Yongjian Guo,Zhong Guan,Wen Huang,Wanlun Ma,Xi Xiao,Junwu Xiong,Sheng Wen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Sword框架，通过结构引导风格增强和动态隐空间自举提升世界模型作为VLA策略优化模拟器的泛化能力与生成质量，在LIBERO基准上显著超越基线WoVR。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有世界模型作为模拟器时存在泛化性差和长程误差累积问题，对初始状态扰动（颜色、光照等视觉变化）高度敏感，导致级联幻觉、模糊或过曝，限制了其在策略优化中的可靠性。

Method: 1) 结构引导风格增强：解耦交互环境的视觉纹理与任务相关动态，提升泛化能力；2) 动态隐空间自举：保持训练与推理一致性，同时控制内存消耗。

Result: 在LIBERO基准上的大量实验表明，Sword在泛化性、生成质量、鲁棒性、保真度以及VLA模型强化学习后训练的成功率上均显著优于基线WoVR。

Conclusion: Sword框架有效缓解了世界模型作为模拟器的两大核心问题（泛化差与误差累积），提升了其在具身智能策略优化中的实用性和可靠性。

Original Abstract: The integration of Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models with World Models has gained increasing attention. One representative approach treats learned World Models as generative simulators, enabling policy optimization entirely within "imagination." However, when deployed as simulators for specific environments such as the LIBERO benchmark, existing World Models often suffer from poor generalization and long-horizon error accumulation. During closed-loop rollouts, these models are highly sensitive to initial-state perturbations; minor changes in color, illumination, and other visual factors can trigger cascading hallucinations, leading to severe blurriness or overexposure. Moreover, long-horizon error accumulation further degrades the quality and fidelity of predicted future states. These issues limit the reliability of World Models as simulators. To mitigate these problems, we propose Sword, a robust World Model framework. Our method introduces Structure-Guided Style Augmentation to disentangle the visual textures of interactive environments from task-relevant dynamics, thereby improving generalization. We further propose Dynamic Latent Bootstrapping, which maintains consistency between training and inference while keeping memory consumption low. Extensive experiments on the LIBERO benchmark show that our method significantly outperforms the baseline WoVR in terms of generalization, generation quality, robustness, fidelity, and the success rate of reinforcement-learning post-training for VLA models.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉-语言-动作模型与世界模型的融合日益受到关注。一种代表性方法将学习到的世界模型作为生成式模拟器，使得策略优化完全在"想象"中进行。然而，当部署为特定环境（如LIBERO基准）的模拟器时，现有世界模型往往存在泛化性差和长程误差累积的问题。在闭环 rollout 过程中，这些模型对初始状态扰动高度敏感；颜色、光照等视觉因素的细微变化可能引发级联幻觉，导致严重的模糊或过曝。此外，长程误差累积进一步降低了预测未来状态的质量和保真度。这些问题限制了世界模型作为模拟器的可靠性。为缓解这些问题，我们提出Sword，一个鲁棒的世界模型框架。我们的方法引入结构引导风格增强，将交互环境的视觉纹理与任务相关动态解耦，从而提升泛化能力。我们进一步提出动态隐空间自举，在保持训练与推理一致性的同时维持低内存消耗。在LIBERO基准上的大量实验表明，我们的方法在泛化性、生成质量、鲁棒性、保真度以及VLA模型强化学习后训练的成功率上均显著优于基线WoVR。

</details>


### [43] [EgoPro-Bench: Benchmarking Personalized Proactive Interaction in Egocentric Video Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07299)
*Dongchuan Ran,Linyu Ou,Xueheng Li,Wenwen Tong,Chenxu Guo,Hewei Guo,Kaibing Wang,Lewei Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EgoPro-Bench基准，包含2400个评估视频和12000+训练视频，用于训练和评估基于流式自我中心视频的主动交互能力，并引入“短思考、更好交互”原则，实现了MLLM在主动人机交互中意图理解和时机把握的提升。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有MLLM主要是被动反应的，缺乏持续感知环境和主动帮助用户的能力；现有主动交互基准局限于警报场景、忽略个性化上下文、未能评估人机交互的精确时机。

Method: 构建EgoPro-Bench基准：利用模拟用户画像生成多样化用户意图，在12个领域构建高保真HMI数据；提出专门评估协议和指标；训练面向流式视频数据的主动交互模型；引入“短思考、更好交互”原则，在意图识别前分配有限的token预算以提升交互性能。

Result: 实验表明EgoPro-Bench显著增强了MLLM的意图理解能力，并能准确识别HMI的适当时机。

Conclusion: EgoPro-Bench为下一代以用户为中心的主动交互智能体奠定了坚实基础。

Original Abstract: Existing Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) remain primarily reactive, failing to continuously perceive environments or proactively assist users. While emerging benchmarks address proactivity, they are largely confined to alert scenarios, neglect personalized context, and fail to evaluate the precise timing of human-machine interactions (HMI).In this paper, we introduce EgoPro-Bench, a novel benchmark for training and evaluating proactive interaction capabilities based on streaming egocentric videos; it comprises 2,400 videos in the evaluation set and over 12,000 videos in the training set.Unlike previous works, EgoPro-Bench leverages simulated user profiles to generate diverse user intentions and to construct high-fidelity HMI data across 12 distinct domains.Subsequently, we propose a specialized evaluation protocol and metrics, train proactive interaction models designed for efficient reasoning and low-latency interaction on streaming video data, and conduct comprehensive evaluations.Furthermore, we introduce an interaction principle termed "short thinking, better interaction", which allocates a limited token budget prior to intent recognition, thereby enhancing interaction performance.The experiments demonstrate that EgoPro-Bench substantially enhances the intention understanding capabilities of MLLMs and enables accurate identification of appropriate timings for HMI, thereby laying a solid foundation for next-generation user-centric proactive interactive agents.

中文摘要翻译: 现有的多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）仍然主要是被动反应的，无法持续感知环境或主动帮助用户。尽管新兴基准开始关注主动性，但它们大多局限于警报场景，忽略个性化上下文，且未能评估人机交互（HMI）的精确时机。本文提出EgoPro-Bench，一个基于流式自我中心视频训练和评估主动交互能力的新型基准；其评估集包含2,400个视频，训练集包含超过12,000个视频。与以往工作不同，EgoPro-Bench利用模拟用户画像生成多样化的用户意图，并在12个不同领域构建高保真的HMI数据。随后，我们提出专门的评估协议和指标，训练专为流式视频数据设计的高效推理和低延迟交互的主动交互模型，并进行全面评估。此外，我们引入一项称为“短思考，更好交互”的交互原则，该原则在意图识别前分配有限的token预算，从而提升交互性能。实验表明，EgoPro-Bench显著增强了MLLM的意图理解能力，并使其能够准确识别HMI的适当时机，从而为下一代以用户为中心的主动交互智能体奠定了坚实基础。

</details>


### [44] [Amortized-Precision Quantization for Early-Exit Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07317)
*Rui Fang,Hsi-Wen Chen,Ming-Syan Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MAQEE框架，通过联合优化退出阈值和量化位宽，在低精度提前退出场景下实现视觉Transformer的稳定推理，大幅降低计算量同时保持精度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有量化方法假设静态全深度执行，当退出决策被量化噪声干扰时不稳定，导致沿动态推理路径的误差放大，需要一种能感知利用率的量化方案来解决低精度提前退出中的不稳定问题。

Method: 提出摊销精度量化（APQ）这一利用率感知公式，刻画逐层对量化噪声的随机暴露及深度-精度权衡；在此基础上提出MAQEE双层框架，在显式风险控制下联合优化退出阈值和位宽。

Result: 在分类、检测和分割任务上，MAQEE建立了更优的精度-效率帕累托前沿，减少高达95%的BOPs（位操作数），同时保持精度，比强基线方法性能提升高达20%。

Conclusion: MAQEE通过联合优化退出阈值和量化位宽，有效解决了低精度提前退出中的推理不稳定问题，在精度和效率之间取得了更优的权衡。

Original Abstract: Vision Transformers (ViTs) achieve strong performance across vision tasks, yet their deployment with low-precision early exiting remains fragile. Existing quantization methods assume static full-depth execution, making them unstable when exit decisions are perturbed by quantization noise, which can amplify errors along dynamic inference paths. In this paper, we introduce Amortized-Precision Quantization (APQ), a utilization-aware formulation that accounts for layer-wise stochastic exposure to quantization noise and reveals depth-precision trade-offs. Building on APQ, we propose Mutual Adaptive Quantization with Early Exiting (MAQEE), a bi-level framework that jointly optimizes exit thresholds and bit-widths under explicit risk control to improve inference stability. MAQEE establishes a superior Pareto frontier in the accuracy-efficiency trade-off, reducing BOPs by up to 95% while maintaining accuracy and outperforming strong baselines by up to 20\% across classification, detection, and segmentation tasks.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉Transformer（ViTs）在各类视觉任务中取得了优异性能，但其在低精度提前退出（early exiting）部署中仍然脆弱。现有量化方法假设静态全深度执行，当退出决策被量化噪声干扰时，这些方法会变得不稳定，量化噪声可能沿动态推理路径放大误差。本文提出摊销精度量化（APQ）——一种利用率感知的公式，它刻画了逐层对量化噪声的随机暴露程度，并揭示了深度-精度之间的权衡。在APQ的基础上，我们提出互适应量化与提前退出（MAQEE）——一种双层框架，在显式风险控制下联合优化退出阈值和位宽，以提高推理稳定性。MAQEE在精度-效率权衡中建立了更优的帕累托前沿，在分类、检测和分割任务中减少高达95%的位操作数（BOPs）的同时保持精度，比强基线方法性能提升高达20%。

</details>


### [45] [GEM: Generating LiDAR World Model via Deformable Mamba](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07326)
*Yang Wu,Zhaojiang Liu,Qiang Meng,Youquan Liu,Renliang Weng,Jianjun Qian,Jian Yang,Jin Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GEM，一种基于可变形Mamba架构的生成式激光雷达世界模型，通过LiDAR点云标记化、动态-静态特征解耦和三路径可变形Mamba扫描，显著提升了自动驾驶中环境建模的保真度和想象力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 激光雷达点云固有的无序性以及难以区分动态物体与静态结构的问题，导致基于LiDAR的世界模型进展滞后于基于相机视频或占用数据的方法。

Method: (1) 利用连续激光扫描与Mamba处理机制的结构相似性，通过自定义LiDAR场景分词器将LiDAR扫描转换为紧凑表示；(2) 通过动态-静态分离器对分词特征进行无监督解耦；(3) 引入三路径可变形Mamba，对解耦特征进行选择性扫描和自适应门控融合，增强时空理解；(4) 可选集成规划器和BEV布局控制器，实现自主推演和“假设”场景生成。

Result: 在多个基准和评估设置上取得了最先进的性能，证明了其优越性和有效性。

Conclusion: GEM是一种有效的LiDAR世界模型方法，通过创新的可变形Mamba架构解决了点云无序性和动态-静态区分难题，在自动驾驶场景建模中展现出领先性能。

Original Abstract: World models, which simulate environmental dynamics and generate sensor observations, are gaining increasing attention in autonomous driving. However, progress in LiDAR-based world models has lagged behind those built on camera videos or occupancy data, primarily due to two core challenges: the inherent disorder of LiDAR point clouds and the difficulty of distinguishing dynamic objects from static structures. To address these issues, we propose GEM: a Generative LiDAR world model that leverages deformable mamba architecture, significantly improving fidelity and imaginative capability. Specifically, leveraging the structural similarity between sequential laser scanning and Mamba's processing mechanism, we first tokenize LiDAR sweeps into compact representations via a custom LiDAR scene tokenizer. After unsupervised disentanglement of tokenized features via a dynamic-static separator, a tri-path deformable Mamba is introduced to perform selective scanning and adaptive gating fusion over the disentangled features, leading to enhanced spatial-temporal understanding of the world evolution. Optionally, a planner and a BEV layout controller can be integrated to explore the model's capability for autonomous rollout and its potential to generate ``what-if" scenarios. Extensive experiments show that GEM achieves state-of-the-art performances across diverse benchmarks and evaluation settings, demonstrating its superiority and effectiveness. Project page: https://github.com/wuyang98/GEM.

中文摘要翻译: 世界模型通过模拟环境动态并生成传感器观测数据，在自动驾驶领域正受到越来越多的关注。然而，基于激光雷达的世界模型的进展落后于基于相机视频或占用数据构建的模型，这主要是由于两个核心挑战：激光雷达点云固有的无序性以及区分动态物体与静态结构的困难。为了解决这些问题，我们提出了GEM：一种基于可变形Mamba架构的生成式激光雷达世界模型，显著提升了保真度和想象力。具体而言，利用连续激光扫描与Mamba处理机制之间的结构相似性，我们首先通过自定义的LiDAR场景分词器将LiDAR扫描转换为紧凑表示。在对分词后的特征通过动态-静态分离器进行无监督解耦之后，引入三路径可变形Mamba对解耦特征进行选择性扫描和自适应门控融合，从而增强对世界演化的时空理解。可选地，可以集成规划器和BEV布局控制器，以探索模型的自主推演能力及其生成“假设”场景的潜力。大量实验表明，GEM在多种基准和评估设置下均取得了最先进的性能，证明了其优越性和有效性。

</details>


### [46] [GC-ART: Global Learnable Second-Order Rational Tone Curves for Illumination Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07329)
*Wei Huang,Joyce Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GC-ART提出了一种轻量级、可微分的全局自适应有理色调映射预处理模块，通过直方图条件化的有理曲线进行逐点映射，在图像分类中提升了鲁棒性，尤其在对比度损坏场景下表现优异。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有图像增强方法通常计算开销大、不可微分或无法保持边缘结构，亟需一种轻量级、可端到端训练且能保持边缘位置的预处理模块，以提升分类器在各类图像损坏条件下的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出GC-ART模块，首先从每个通道的软直方图中通过一个643参数的MLP预测端点固定的有理色调曲线，然后在分类器之前对图像逐点应用该曲线。模块使用交叉熵损失和软单调性惩罚进行端到端训练。

Result: 在CIFAR-10上使用ResNet-18，GC-ART在干净精度上与基线持平；在乘法变暗场景下优于基线；在对比度损坏场景下达到48.45%（基线46.27%，Zero-DCE++ 47.13%），且计算量远低于卷积式学习增强器。

Conclusion: 直方图条件化的有理曲线能够学习有用的全局色调校正（包括对比度扩展行为），同时通过逐点映射固有地保持边缘位置；当前超参数未经系统调优，仍有改进空间。

Original Abstract: We introduce GC-ART (Global Curve Adaptive Rational Tone-mapping), a lightweight differentiable pre-processing module for robust image classification. GC-ART predicts an endpoint-pinned rational tone curve from per-channel soft histograms using a 643-parameter MLP, then applies the curve pointwise before the classifier. The module is trained end-to-end with cross-entropy and a soft monotonicity penalty. On CIFAR-10 with a CIFAR-style ResNet-18, GC-ART matches clean accuracy with the unenhanced baseline and other learned enhancers, improves over the baseline on multiplicative darkening, and achieves the best learned-method result on contrast corruption (48.45% vs. 46.27% for the baseline and 47.13% for Zero-DCE++). These results suggest that histogram-conditioned rational curves can learn useful global tone corrections, including contrast-expanding behavior, while preserving edge locations by construction through pointwise mapping. GC-ART also uses substantially fewer FLOPs than convolutional learned enhancers at 32 x 32. The current hyperparameters are untuned, leaving room for systematic improvement.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了GC-ART（全局曲线自适应有理色调映射），一种轻量级、可微分的预处理模块，用于鲁棒图像分类。GC-ART从每个通道的软直方图中通过一个643参数的MLP预测端点固定的有理色调曲线，然后在分类器之前对图像逐点应用该曲线。该模块使用交叉熵损失和软单调性惩罚进行端到端训练。在CIFAR-10上使用CIFAR风格的ResNet-18时，GC-ART在干净精度上与未增强的基线和其它学习型增强器持平；在乘法变暗场景下优于基线；在对比度损坏场景下取得了最佳的学习型方法结果（48.45%，基线为46.27%，Zero-DCE++为47.13%）。这些结果表明，直方图条件化的有理曲线可以学习有用的全局色调校正（包括对比度扩展行为），同时通过逐点映射固有地保持边缘位置。在32×32分辨率下，GC-ART的计算量也远低于卷积式学习增强器。当前超参数未经调优，留有系统改进的空间。

</details>


### [47] [RCoT-Seg: Reinforced Chain-of-Thought for Video Reasoning and Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07334)
*Junwei Wen,Deshui Miao,Guangming Lu,Xin Li,Wenjie Pei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RCoT-Seg，一种将视频推理分割分解为时间视频推理（TVR）和关键帧目标感知（KTP）的视频思维框架。TVR阶段通过基于GRPO的智能体关键帧选择模块增强时间定位与推理；KTP阶段对所选关键帧进行高分辨率分割并利用SAM2传播掩码，提升空间精度与帧间一致性。实验表明该方法达到SOTA性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于MLLM的视频推理分割方法通过简单采样或辅助MLLM选取帧，存在监督信号有限、帧-语言相似性规则导致关键帧选择范围狭窄的问题，削弱了整体时间理解能力，在复杂多目标场景中定位脆弱。

Method: 提出RCoT-Seg框架，将VRS分解为两个阶段：（1）TVR阶段：设计智能体关键帧选择模块，先用CoT-start语料库初始化，再通过GRPO算法在任务对齐奖励下优化，通过自评估生成并重新选择关键帧，增强时间定位与推理；（2）KTP阶段：对所选关键帧进行高分辨率分割，并基于SAM2方法将掩码传播到整个视频序列，替代启发式采样与外部选择器。

Result: 大量实验结果表明，提出的RCoT-Seg在多个基准上优于现有最先进方法，在复杂多目标场景中展现出更强的时间推理和空间分割能力。

Conclusion: 通过将时间推理与空间感知显式分离，RCoT-Seg有效解决了现有方法在关键帧选择上的局限性，实现了更鲁棒的时间定位和更精确的空间分割，为视频推理分割任务提供了新的范式。

Original Abstract: Video Reasoning Segmentation (VRS) aims to segment target objects in videos based on implicit instructions that convey human intent and temporal logic. Existing MLLM-based methods predict masks with a [SEG] token after selecting frames via simple sampling or an auxiliary MLLM, where limited supervision and frame-language similarity rules often yield narrow-scope keyframe choices that weaken holistic temporal understanding and lead to brittle localization in complex multi-object scenes. To address these issues, we introduce RCoT-Seg, a video-of-thought framework that factorizes VRS into temporal video reasoning (TVR) and keyframe target perception (KTP), explicitly separating temporal reasoning from spatial perception. Specifically, in the TVR stage, an agentic keyframe selection module, initialized with a curated CoT-start corpus and refined by GRPO under task-aligned rewards, is proposed to generate and reselect the keyframe through self-evaluation, strengthening moment localization and temporal reasoning. In the KTP stage, RCoT-Seg performs high-resolution segmentation on the selected frame and propagates masks with SAM2-based methods across the sequence, replacing heuristic sampling and external selectors while improving spatial precision and inter-frame consistency. Extensive experimental results demonstrate that the proposed RCoT-Seg achieves favorable performance against the state-of-the-art methods. The code and models will be publicly released at https://github.com/Victor-wjw/RCoT-Seg.

中文摘要翻译: 视频推理分割（VRS）旨在基于传达人类意图和时间逻辑的隐式指令，在视频中分割目标物体。现有的基于多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的方法在通过简单采样或辅助MLLM选取帧后，使用[SEG]标记预测掩码。由于有限的监督信号和帧-语言相似性规则，这类方法往往导致关键帧选择范围狭窄，削弱了整体的时间理解能力，并在复杂多目标场景中产生脆弱的定位结果。为解决这些问题，我们引入了RCoT-Seg，一种视频思维框架，将VRS分解为时间视频推理（TVR）和关键帧目标感知（KTP），显式地将时间推理与空间感知分离。具体而言，在TVR阶段，我们提出了一种智能体关键帧选择模块，该模块使用精心构建的CoT-start语料库初始化，并通过任务对齐奖励下的GRPO算法进行优化，能够通过自评估生成并重新选择关键帧，从而增强时刻定位和时间推理。在KTP阶段，RCoT-Seg对所选帧执行高分辨率分割，并基于SAM2方法将掩码传播到整个序列，取代了启发式采样和外部选择器，同时提高了空间精度和帧间一致性。大量实验结果表明，所提出的RCoT-Seg相较于最先进方法取得了优越的性能。代码和模型将公开发布于 https://github.com/Victor-wjw/RCoT-Seg。

</details>


### [48] [Disambiguating 2D-3D Correspondences in Gaussian Splatting-based Feature Fields for Visual Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07351)
*Miso Lee,Sangeek Hyun,Yerim Jeon,Jae-Pil Heo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SplitGS-Loc，通过混合高斯分割和基于光栅化权重的选择策略，将光度量优化的高斯特征场转化为适用于视觉定位的精准特征场，实现无需逐场景训练或迭代优化的SOTA定位性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于高斯泼溅的特征场（GSFFs）经光度优化后本质不适合2D-3D匹配：每个高斯的体积范围导致多对一的像素-点映射，使PnP位姿估计不稳定；光度优化还产生缺乏多视角一致性的冗余高斯。需要一种方法从GSFF中提取适用于定位的判别性特征。

Method: 提出SplitGS-Loc框架，核心包括：1）基于混合高斯（Mixture-of-Gaussians）的分割策略，将每个高斯分解为多个更小的高斯，将模糊的多对一对应关系替换为精确的一对一对应关系；2）利用GS光栅化中的合成权重，筛选在多视角下显著且一致贡献的高斯，并通过强像素-高斯关联聚合判别性特征，强制多视角一致性。

Result: 在多个定位基准上达到最先进性能，实验验证SplitGS-Loc通过利用高斯属性扩展了光度GSFFs在精确高效定位中的实用性，且无需逐场景训练或迭代位姿优化。

Conclusion: SplitGS-Loc有效解决了GSFFs在多视角一致性和对应精度上的固有缺陷，将光度量优化的特征场成功应用于视觉定位任务，实现了稳定、精确且高效的定位效果。

Original Abstract: While Gaussian Splatting-based Feature Fields (GSFFs) have shown promise for visual localization, this paper highlights that photometrically optimized GSFFs are inherently ill-suited for 2D-3D matching. The volumetric extent of each Gaussian induces many-to-one pixel-to-point mappings that destabilize PnP-based pose estimation, while photometric optimization gives rise to superfluous Gaussians devoid of multi-view consistency. To address these issues, we propose SplitGS-Loc, a localization-specialized GSFFs construction framework that disambiguates 2D-3D correspondences by exploiting Gaussian attributes. Our key design, Mixture-of-Gaussians-based splitting, decomposes each Gaussian into smaller Gaussians, replacing ambiguous many-to-one with precise one-to-one correspondences. In parallel, we exploit composition weights from GS rasterization to select Gaussians that significantly and consistently contribute across multiple views and aggregate discriminative features through strong pixel-Gaussian associations, enforcing multi-view consistency. The resulting compact yet discriminative feature fields enable stable PnP convergence, achieving state-of-the-art performance on localization benchmarks. Extensive experiments validate that SplitGS-Loc extends the utility of photometric GSFFs to accurate and efficient localization by exploiting Gaussian attributes, without per-scene training or iterative pose refinement.

中文摘要翻译: 尽管基于高斯泼溅的特征场（GSFFs）在视觉定位中展现出了潜力，但本文指出，经过光度优化的GSFFs本质上并不适合2D-3D匹配。每个高斯的体积范围导致了多对一的像素-点映射，从而破坏了基于PnP的位姿估计的稳定性，而光度优化则会产生缺乏多视角一致性的冗余高斯。为解决这些问题，我们提出SplitGS-Loc，一种面向定位定制的GSFFs构建框架，通过利用高斯属性来消歧2D-3D对应关系。我们的关键设计——基于混合高斯（Mixture-of-Gaussians）的分割策略——将每个高斯分解为更小的高斯，将模糊的多对一对应关系替换为精确的一对一对应关系。与此同时，我们利用GS光栅化中的合成权重，筛选出在多视角下具有显著且一致贡献的高斯，并通过强像素-高斯关联来聚合判别性特征，从而强制多视角一致性。由此产生的紧凑且具有判别性的特征场能够实现稳定的PnP收敛，在多个定位基准上达到了最先进的性能。大量实验验证，SplitGS-Loc通过利用高斯属性，将光度优化的GSFFs的实用性扩展到了精确高效的定位任务中，且无需逐场景训练或迭代位姿优化。

</details>


### [49] [TTF: Temporal Token Fusion for Efficient Video-Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07355)
*Simin Huo,Ning LI*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需训练的即插即用视觉令牌压缩框架Temporal Token Fusion (TTF)，通过利用视频中的结构时域冗余，在保持99.5%基线精度的同时移除约67%的视觉令牌，显著加速视频-语言模型推理。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 视频-语言模型在处理长视频时，视觉令牌数量随视频长度线性增长（如Qwen3-VL中32帧448×448分辨率产生超过8000个视觉令牌），导致LLM预填充阶段成为吞吐量瓶颈。现有压缩方法依赖全局相似性或注意力引导，计算开销大，抵消了压缩收益。

Method: TTF是一种训练无关的预LLM令牌压缩框架。首先自动选择锚定帧，然后对后续每一帧，在局部窗口（如3×3）内进行相似性搜索，将超过阈值的令牌进行融合。通过坐标对齐保持压缩序列在预填充和解码阶段的位置一致性，实现与现有VLM管线的无缝集成。

Result: 在Qwen3-VL-8B模型上，阈值t=0.70时，TTF移除约67%的视觉令牌，保留99.5%的基线精度，仅引入约0.16 GFLOPs的匹配开销。

Conclusion: TTF为视频理解提供了一种实用且高效的解决方案，能够在不显著牺牲精度的情况下大幅降低视觉令牌数量，有效缓解VLM推理吞吐量瓶颈。

Original Abstract: Video-language models (VLMs) face rapid inference costs as visual token counts scale with video length. For example, 32 frames at $448{\times}448$ resolution already yield >8,000 visual tokens in Qwen3-VL, making LLM prefill the dominant throughput bottleneck. Existing methods often rely on global similarity or attention-guided compression, incurring offsets to their gains. We propose \textbf{Temporal Token Fusion (TTF)}, a training-free, plug-and-play pre-LLM token compression framework that exploits structured temporal redundancy in video. TTF automatically selects an anchor frame, then for each subsequent frame, performs a local window similarity search (e.g.,$3\times 3$), fusing tokens that exceed a threshold. The compressed sequence maintains positional consistency across both prefill and decoding through coordinate realignment, enabling seamless integration with existing VLM pipelines. On Qwen3-VL-8B with threshold t=0.70, TTF removes about 67\% of visual tokens while retaining 99.5\% of the baseline accuracy and introducing only ${\approx}0.16$\,GFLOPs of matching overhead. Overall, TTF offers a practical, efficient solution for video understanding. The code is available at \href{https://github.com/Cominder/ttf}{https://github.com/Cominder/ttf}

中文摘要翻译: 视频-语言模型面临快速增长的推理成本问题，因为视觉令牌数量随视频长度线性增长。例如，在Qwen3-VL中，32帧448×448分辨率已产生超过8000个视觉令牌，使得大语言模型的预填充阶段成为主要的吞吐量瓶颈。现有方法通常依赖全局相似性或注意力引导的压缩，其收益被引入的计算开销所抵消。我们提出**时序令牌融合 (Temporal Token Fusion, TTF)**，一种无需训练、即插即用的预LLM令牌压缩框架，利用视频中结构的时域冗余。TTF自动选择锚定帧，然后对后续每一帧执行局部窗口相似性搜索（如3×3），融合超过阈值的令牌。通过坐标对齐，压缩序列在预填充和解码阶段均保持位置一致性，从而能够与现有VLM管线无缝集成。在Qwen3-VL-8B上，阈值t=0.70时，TTF移除约67%的视觉令牌，同时保留99.5%的基线精度，仅引入约0.16 GFLOPs的匹配开销。总体而言，TTF为视频理解提供了一种实用且高效的解决方案。代码已开源：https://github.com/Cominder/ttf

</details>


### [50] [A Marine Debris Detection Framework for Ocean Robots via Self-Attention Enhancement and Feature Interaction Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07388)
*Yuyang Li,Jiashu Han,Yinyi Lai,Wenbin Kang,Zenghui Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出YOLO-MD框架，通过DB-CASA注意力模块、轻量级移位操作和SFG-Loss损失函数，解决海洋机器人低质量图像中的模糊、复杂背景和小目标检测难题，在UODM数据集上达到SOTA性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 海洋机器人进行海洋碎片检测时，低质量图像（模糊、复杂背景、小目标）导致检测性能严重下降，现有方法难以有效应对这些退化场景。

Method: 1) 提出双分支卷积增强自注意力模块(DB-CASA)，增强空间-通道交互以改善退化图像的表示；2) 引入轻量级移位操作，提升多尺度目标的细粒度特征提取并保持参数效率；3) 提出SFG-Loss，通过动态样本重加权缓解类别不平衡和优化不稳定问题。

Result: 在UODM数据集上，YOLO-MD达到0.875精确率、0.822 F1分数和0.849 mAP50，优于最新SOTA方法，并在真实机器人边缘部署实验中验证了有效性。

Conclusion: YOLO-MD有效提升了海洋机器人在低质量图像下的碎片检测能力，且具备实际边缘部署的可行性。

Original Abstract: Marine debris detection for ocean robot is crucial for ecological protection, yet performance is often degraded by low-quality images with blur, complex backgrounds, and small targets. To address these challenges, we propose YOLO-MD, an enhanced YOLO-based detection framework. A Dual-Branch Convolutional Enhanced Self-Attention (DB-CASA) module is designed to strengthen spatial-channel interactions, improving feature representation in degraded images. Additionally, a lightweight shift-based operation is introduced to enhance fine-grained feature extraction for objects of varying scales while maintaining parameter efficiency. We further propose SFG-Loss to mitigate class imbalance and optimization instability via dynamic sample reweighting. Experiments on the UODM dataset demonstrate that YOLO-MD achieves 0.875 precision, 0.822 F1-score, and 0.849 mAP50, outperforming the latest state-of-the-art methods. The effectiveness of this method has also been verified through real-world robotic edge deployment experiments.

中文摘要翻译: 海洋机器人的海洋碎片检测对于生态保护至关重要，但模糊、复杂背景和小目标等低质量图像常常导致性能下降。为应对这些挑战，我们提出了YOLO-MD，一种增强型基于YOLO的检测框架。设计了双分支卷积增强自注意力模块(DB-CASA)，以加强空间-通道交互，改善退化图像中的特征表示。此外，引入了一种轻量级移位操作，以增强对不同尺度物体的细粒度特征提取，同时保持参数效率。我们进一步提出了SFG-Loss，通过动态样本重加权缓解类别不平衡和优化不稳定问题。在UODM数据集上的实验表明，YOLO-MD实现了0.875的精确率、0.822的F1分数和0.849的mAP50，优于最新的最先进方法。该方法在真实机器人边缘部署实验中也验证了其有效性。

</details>


### [51] [ST-Gen4D: Embedding 4D Spatiotemporal Cognition into World Model for 4D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07390)
*Haonan Wang,Hanyu Zhou,Tao Gu,Luxin Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ST-Gen4D，一种基于4D时空认知的世界模型框架，通过全局外观图与局部动态图的语义融合，实现结构合理且拓扑一致的4D生成，并在新构建的ST-4D数据集上验证了优越性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有4D生成模型仅关注全局外观一致性而忽略了局部动态拓扑信息，导致无法揭示物理世界的局部动力学特性。作者认为全局外观结构与局部动态拓扑共同赋能4D时空认知，从而产生具有时空规律性的4D生成。

Method: ST-Gen4D包含四个关键设计：(1) 时空表征——将多模态信息编码为多种特征基；(2) 时空认知——将表征雕琢为全局外观图和局部动态图，并通过语义桥接的时空融合得到4D认知图；(3) 时空推理——利用世界模型基于4D认知图推导未来状态；(4) 时空生成——以推理得到的认知为条件引导潜扩散模型生成4D高斯表示。

Result: 在3D和4D生成任务上的大量实验表明，ST-Gen4D在结构合理性和拓扑一致性方面均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过将4D内在认知与生成先验深度整合，ST-Gen4D能够保证4D生成的结构合理性与拓扑一致性，为物理世界4D生成提供了新范式。

Original Abstract: Generative models have achieved success in producing apparently coherent 2D videos, but remain challenging in the physical world due to lack of 4D spatiotemporal scale. Typically, existing 4D generative models directly embed macro scale constraints to enhance overall spatiotemporal consistency. However, these methods only ensure global appearance coherence and fail to reveal the local dynamics of the physical world. Our insight is that global appearance structure and local dynamic topology empower 4D spatiotemporal cognition, thereby enabling 4D generation with spatiotemporal regularities. In this work, we propose ST-Gen4D, a 4D generation framework with 4D spatiotemporal cognition-based world model. Our model is guided by four key designs: 1) Spatiotemporal representation. We encode various modalities into multiple representations as a feature basis. 2) Spatiotemporal cognition. We sculpture these representations into global appearance graph and local dynamic graph, and fuse them via semantic-bridged spatiotemporal fusion to obtain a 4D cognition graph. 3) Spatiotemporal reasoning. We utilize a world model to derive future state based on the 4D cognition. 4) Spatiotemporal generation. We leverage the derived cognition as condition to guide latent diffusion for 4D Gaussian generation. By deeply integrating 4D intrinsic cognition with generative priors, our model guarantees the structural rationality and topological consistency of 4D generation. Moreover, we propose ST-4D datasets by aggregating public 4D datasets and self-built subset. Extensive experiments demonstrate the superiority of our ST-Gen4D across 3D and 4D generation tasks.

中文摘要翻译: 生成模型在生成表面连贯的2D视频方面已取得成功，但由于缺乏4D时空尺度，在物理世界中仍面临挑战。通常，现有的4D生成模型直接嵌入宏观尺度约束以增强整体时空一致性。然而，这些方法仅能确保全局外观连贯性，而无法揭示物理世界的局部动力学。我们的洞见是：全局外观结构与局部动态拓扑共同赋能4D时空认知，从而能够实现具有时空规律性的4D生成。本文提出ST-Gen4D，一种基于4D时空认知的世界模型框架。我们的模型由四个关键设计引导：(1) 时空表征——将多种模态编码为多个表征作为特征基；(2) 时空认知——将这些表征雕琢为全局外观图和局部动态图，并通过语义桥接的时空融合得到4D认知图；(3) 时空推理——利用世界模型基于4D认知图推导未来状态；(4) 时空生成——以推理得到的认知为条件引导潜扩散模型进行4D高斯生成。通过深度整合4D内在认知与生成先验，我们的模型保证了4D生成的结构合理性与拓扑一致性。此外，我们通过聚合公开4D数据集和自建子集提出了ST-4D数据集。大量实验表明，我们的ST-Gen4D在3D和4D生成任务上均具有优越性。

</details>


### [52] [BalCapRL: A Balanced Framework for RL-Based MLLM Image Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07394)
*Shaokai Ye,Vasileios Saveris,Yihao Qian,Jiaming Hu,Elmira Amirloo,Peter Grasch*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向图像描述的平衡强化学习框架，通过多目标奖励优化（结合效用、参考覆盖和语言质量）和GDPO风格奖励解耦归一化及长度条件奖励掩码，在多个多模态大模型上显著提升了描述质量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有图像描述的强化学习方法及评估指标往往只强调单一维度的描述质量（如效用导向或竞技场风格），导致不同核心维度之间出现权衡（如产生冗长、幻觉化描述或流畅但泛泛的描述），缺乏一个平衡的优化框架。

Method: 提出一个联合优化效用感知正确性、参考覆盖率和语言质量的平衡RL框架；采用GDPO风格的奖励解耦归一化处理连续值的描述奖励；引入长度条件奖励掩码作为更合适的长度惩罚机制。

Result: 在LLaVA-1.5-7B和Qwen2.5-VL 3B/7B基础模型上一致提升了描述质量，最佳提升包括DCScore +13.6、CaptionQA +9.0、CapArena +29.0。

Conclusion: 所提出的平衡多目标强化学习方法能够有效克服现有方法在图像描述各核心维度间的权衡问题，在不同规模和架构的多模态大模型上均取得了显著且一致的性能提升。

Original Abstract: Image captioning is one of the most fundamental tasks in computer vision. Owing to its open-ended nature, it has received significant attention in the era of multimodal large language models (MLLMs). In pursuit of ever more detailed and accurate captions, recent work has increasingly turned to reinforcement learning (RL). However, existing captioning-RL methods and evaluation metrics often emphasize a narrow notion of caption quality, inducing trade-offs across core dimensions of captioning. For example, utility-oriented objectives can encourage noisy, hallucinated, or overlong captions that improve downstream question answering while harming fluency, whereas arena-style objectives can favor fluent but generic descriptions with limited usefulness. To address this, we propose a more balanced RL framework that jointly optimizes utility-aware correctness, reference coverage, and linguistic quality. In order to effectively optimize the resulting continuous multi-objective reward formulation, we apply GDPO-style reward-decoupled normalization to continuous-valued captioning rewards and show that it improves performance over vanilla GRPO. Additionally, we introduce length-conditional reward masking, yielding a more suitable length penalty for captioning. Across LLaVA-1.5-7B and Qwen2.5-VL 3B and 7B base models, our method consistently improves caption quality, with peak gains of +13.6 DCScore, +9.0 CaptionQA, and +29.0 CapArena across different models.

中文摘要翻译: 图像描述是计算机视觉中最基础的任务之一。由于其开放式的特性，在多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）时代受到了广泛关注。为了追求更加详细和准确的描述，最近的研究越来越多地转向强化学习（RL）。然而，现有的描述-RL方法和评估指标往往强调一种狭隘的描述质量观念，导致在描述的核心维度之间产生权衡。例如，效用导向的目标会鼓励包含噪声、幻觉或过长的描述，这些描述能改善下游的问答任务但损害了流畅性；而竞技场风格的目标则可能偏好流畅但通用、实用性有限的描述。为了解决这个问题，我们提出了一个更加平衡的RL框架，该框架联合优化了效用感知的正确性、参考覆盖率和语言质量。为了有效优化由此产生的连续多目标奖励公式，我们将GDPO风格的奖励解耦归一化应用于连续值的描述奖励，并证明其性能优于原始GRPO。此外，我们引入了长度条件奖励掩码，为描述提供了更合适的长度惩罚。在LLaVA-1.5-7B和Qwen2.5-VL 3B及7B基础模型上，我们的方法一致地提高了描述质量，在不同模型上取得了最高+13.6 DCScore、+9.0 CaptionQA和+29.0 CapArena的提升。

</details>


### [53] [Exposing and Mitigating Temporal Attack in Deepfake Video Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07398)
*Zheyuan Gu,Minghao Shao,Zhen Wang,Yusong Wang,Mingkun Xu,Shijie Zhang,Hao Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SpInShield，一种时间频谱不变性防御框架，通过解耦语义运动与频谱伪影、引入可学习频谱对抗器和捷径抑制优化策略，使深度伪造检测器从依赖脆弱频谱线索转向学习鲁棒的语义因果特征，在模拟振幅频谱攻击下AUC比最强基线提升21.30个百分点。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有时空深度伪造检测器虽然AUC很高，但容易受到规避攻击，因为它们倾向于过拟合脆弱的时域频谱线索，而非学习鲁棒的语义因果关系。

Method: 提出SpInShield框架：1) 设计可学习频谱对抗器，动态合成严重的频谱形变，模拟极端攻击场景；2) 采用捷径抑制优化策略，迫使编码器提取可靠的取证线索，同时从潜在空间中清除不稳定的频谱统计量。

Result: 在广泛使用的数据集上获得有竞争力的性能，在模拟振幅频谱攻击下AUC比最强基线高出21.30个百分点。

Conclusion: SpInShield能够有效解耦语义运动与可操作的频谱伪影，显著提升深度伪造检测器对频谱攻击的鲁棒性。

Original Abstract: While spatiotemporal deepfake detectors achieve high AUC, our experiments reveal their susceptibility to evasion attacks. These models tend to overfit on fragile temporal spectrum cues, rather than learning robust semantic causality. To mitigate this vulnerability, we propose SpInShield, a temporal spectral-invariant defense framework explicitly designed to decouple semantic motion from manipulatable spectral artifacts. We propose a learnable spectral adversary that dynamically synthesizes severe spectral deformations, simulating extreme attack scenarios. By employing a shortcut suppression optimization strategy, SpInShield compels the encoder to extract reliable forensic cues while purging unstable spectral statistics from the latent space. Experiments show that SpInShield obtains competitive performance on widely used datasets and outperforms the strongest baseline by 21.30 percentage points in AUC under simulated amplitude spectral attacks.

中文摘要翻译: 尽管时空深度伪造检测器能够取得较高的AUC，但我们的实验揭示了它们容易被规避攻击的脆弱性。这些模型倾向于过拟合脆弱的时域频谱线索，而非学习鲁棒的语义因果关系。为缓解这一缺陷，我们提出SpInShield——一种专门设计用于解耦语义运动与可操作频谱伪影的时间频谱不变性防御框架。我们提出了一种可学习的频谱对抗器，能够动态合成严重的频谱形变，模拟极端攻击场景。通过采用捷径抑制优化策略，SpInShield迫使编码器提取可靠的取证线索，同时从潜在空间中清除不稳定的频谱统计量。实验表明，SpInShield在广泛使用的数据集上获得了有竞争力的性能，并在模拟振幅频谱攻击下以AUC指标超越最强基线21.30个百分点。

</details>


### [54] [GPO-V: Jailbreak Diffusion Vision Language Model by Global Probability Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07399)
*Yu Pan,Andi Zhang,Yi Wang,Sibei Yang,Wenjie Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文揭示了基于扩散大语言模型的视觉-语言模型（dVLMs）在面对传统越狱攻击时的虚假鲁棒性，发现其独特的拒绝模式，并提出全局概率优化（GPO）这一通用越狱范式，以及首个视觉模态越狱框架GPO-V，实验证明该框架能生成具有跨模型迁移性的隐蔽扰动，暴露了非自回归生成架构中的关键安全漏洞。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统观点认为dVLMs对基于固定前缀优化的越狱攻击具有天然鲁棒性，但作者发现这种鲁棒性是虚假的。dVLMs存在一种独特的拒绝模式（即时拒绝与渐进式拒绝），而渐进式拒绝暴露了潜在的隐式攻击面。作者旨在利用这一漏洞，针对扩散模型的去噪轨迹设计通用的越狱范式。

Method: 提出全局概率优化（GPO）范式，通过操纵掩码扩散模型的全局生成动力学绕过安全护栏，而非传统的基于前缀优化方法。在此基础上，进一步提出GPO-V，首个针对dVLMs的视觉模态越狱框架。

Result: 实验结果表明GPO-V能生成具有出色跨模型迁移性的隐蔽扰动，揭示了非自回归生成架构中的关键安全漏洞。

Conclusion: 当前dVLMs的安全对齐存在严重不足，必须立即从根本层面重新评估现有防御范式，以应对基于扩散的生成模型带来的独特安全风险。

Original Abstract: Diffusion Vision-Language Models (dVLMs), built upon the non-causal foundations of Diffusion Large Language Models (dLLMs), have demonstrated remarkable efficacy in multimodal tasks by departing from the traditional autoregressive generation paradigm. While dVLMs appear inherently robust against conventional jailbreak tactics, which we categorize as Fixed Prefix Optimization (FPO) (e.g., anchoring responses with "Sure, here is"), this perceived resilience is deceptive. Our investigation into the safety landscape of dVLMs reveals a unique refusal pattern: Immediate Refusal and Progressive Refusal. We find that while FPO-based attacks often fail by triggering the latter, the progressive refinement process itself uncovers a novel, latent attack surface. To exploit this vulnerability, we propose Global Probability Optimization (GPO), a general jailbreak paradigm designed specifically for the denoising trajectory of masked diffusion models. Unlike prefix-based methods, GPO manipulates the global generative dynamics to bypass guardrails in diffusion language models. Building on this, we introduce GPO-V, the first visual-modality jailbreak framework tailored for dVLMs. Empirical results demonstrate that GPO-V produces stealthy perturbations with exceptional cross-model transferability, revealing a critical security gap in non-sequential generative architectures. Our findings underscore the critical urgency of addressing safety alignment in dVLMs. These results necessitate an immediate and fundamental re-evaluation of current defense paradigms to mitigate the unique risks of diffusion-based generation. Our code is available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/GPO-V-0250.

中文摘要翻译: 基于扩散大语言模型（dLLMs）的非因果基础构建的扩散视觉-语言模型（dVLMs），通过摆脱传统自回归生成范式，在多模态任务中展现出了显著的效果。尽管dVLMs看起来对传统的越狱策略（我们将其归类为固定前缀优化，如将响应锚定为"当然，这是"）具有内在鲁棒性，但这种感知到的鲁棒性具有欺骗性。我们对dVLMs安全景观的调查揭示了一种独特的拒绝模式：即时拒绝与渐进式拒绝。我们发现，基于固定前缀优化的攻击常因触发渐进式拒绝而失败，但渐进式精炼过程本身却暴露了一个新颖的隐式攻击面。为利用这一漏洞，我们提出了全局概率优化（GPO）——一种专门针对掩码扩散模型去噪轨迹设计的通用越狱范式。与基于前缀的方法不同，GPO操纵全局生成动力学以绕过扩散语言模型中的安全护栏。在此基础上，我们进一步提出了GPO-V，首个专为dVLMs设计的视觉模态越狱框架。实验结果表明，GPO-V能产生具有出色跨模型迁移性的隐蔽扰动，揭示了非自回归生成架构中的关键安全漏洞。我们的发现强调了对dVLMs进行安全对齐的迫切性。这些结果要求立即从根本层面重新评估当前防御范式，以减轻基于扩散的生成所带来的独特风险。本项目的代码已开源：https://anonymous.4open.science/r/GPO-V-0250。

</details>


### [55] [InsHuman: Towards Natural and Identity-Preserving Human Insertion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07402)
*Jie Li,Shulian Zhang,Yangyang Gao,Wenbo Li,Yulun Zhang,Yong Guo,Jian Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: InsHuman提出了一种用于自然且保持身份一致性的人体插入方法，通过人体-背景自适应融合、面部身份保持和双向数据配对策略，解决了现有人体插入中姿态不当、人数不一致和身份改变等问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有图像编辑模型在进行人体插入时存在三个主要问题：人体姿态与背景不协调、人数不一致以及面部身份被改变。同时，公开人体数据集缺乏全身肖像和人物与背景之间的真实物理交互，亟需一种能够保持身份一致性的自然人体插入方法。

Method: 1）人体-背景自适应融合（HBAF）：检测前景人体获得二值掩码，采用区域感知加权对齐预测与真实潜空间中的人体区域，确保姿态、数量和外观与目标背景协调；2）面部身份保持（FFIP）：检测并匹配生成图像与源图像的人脸，利用人脸识别特征强制保持每个面部的身份一致性；3）双向数据配对（BDP）策略：构建BDP-InsHuman高质量数据集，包含真实的人-背景交互。

Result: 实验表明，InsHuman在生成合理图像方面取得了显著改进，同时保持了人体身份不变。

Conclusion: InsHuman通过融合人体-背景自适应融合、面部身份保持和双向数据配对策略，有效解决了人体插入任务中的姿态协调、人数一致性和身份保持等关键挑战，生成了更自然且身份保留的图像。

Original Abstract: Human insertion aims to naturally place specific individuals into a target background. Although existing image editing models may have such ability, they often produce failure cases, including inappropriate human pose in new background, inconsistent number of people, and modified facial identity. Moreover, publicly available human datasets often lack full-body portraits and realistic physical interaction between humans and their background. To address these challenges, we propose InsHuman for natural and identity-preserving human insertion. Specifically, we propose Human-Background Adaptive Fusion (HBAF), which detects foreground humans to obtain a binary mask and applies region-aware weighting to align the human regions between predicted and ground-truth latents, ensuring the person's pose, count, and overall appearance are coherently adapted to the target background.We further propose Face-to-Face ID-Preserving (FFIP), which detects and matches faces between the generated image and the source image in terms of face recognition features to enforce identity consistency for each face.In addition, we propose Bidirectional Data Pairing (BDP) strategy to construct BDP-InsHuman, a high-quality dataset with realistic human-background interactions. Experiments demonstrate that InsHuman achieves significant improvements in generating plausible images while keeping human identity unchanged.

中文摘要翻译: 人体插入旨在将特定个体自然地放置到目标背景中。尽管现有的图像编辑模型可能具备这种能力，但它们常常产生失败案例，包括人体姿态与新的背景不协调、人数不一致以及面部身份被改变。此外，公开可用的人体数据集通常缺乏全身肖像以及人物与背景之间的真实物理交互。为应对这些挑战，我们提出了InsHuman，用于自然且保持身份一致的人体插入。具体而言，我们提出人体-背景自适应融合（HBAF），该方法检测前景人体以获得二值掩码，并采用区域感知加权来对齐预测潜空间与真实潜空间中的人体区域，确保人物的姿态、数量和整体外观能够与目标背景协调一致。我们进一步提出面部到面部身份保持（FFIP），该方法在人脸识别特征层面检测并匹配生成图像与源图像之间的人脸，以强制保持每个面部的身份一致性。此外，我们提出双向数据配对（BDP）策略，构建了BDP-InsHuman高质量数据集，包含真实的人-背景交互。实验表明，InsHuman在生成合理图像的同时保持人体身份不变方面取得了显著改进。

</details>


### [56] [ChartREG++: Towards Benchmarking and Improving Chart Referring Expression Grounding under Diverse referring clues and Multi-Target Referring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07415)
*Tianhao Niu,Ziyu Han,Qingfu Zhu,Wanxiang Che*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对现有图表指代表达理解基准的局限性（定位形式单一、目标数量有限、语言线索依赖过强、图表类型覆盖窄），提出了一个支持多定位形式、多目标、多样化线索和多种图表类型的基准，并引入代码驱动的合成管线生成像素级精确掩码，训练实例分割模型并集成到多模态定位框架中，显著优于现有基线。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有图表指代表达理解基准存在四个主要局限：（1）大多采用边界框，限制了精细图表元素的定位精度；（2）大多只假设单个或两个指代目标，无法处理多实例目标指代；（3）语言表达过度依赖文本线索或数据排名线索；（4）覆盖的图表类型范围狭窄。因此需要更全面、更具挑战性的基准来推动该领域发展。

Method: （1）构建了一个系统支持多种定位形式、多个指代目标、多样化指代线索和多种图表类型的基准；（2）引入代码驱动的合成管线，利用绘图程序与渲染图表基元之间的内在对齐，生成跨图表元素类型和粒度的像素级精确实例掩码；（3）使用合成掩码训练实例分割模型，并将其集成到通用多模态定位框架中。

Result: 在代表性多模态大模型上的评估显示存在显著的性能差距；所提出的系统在自建基准上持续优于基线，并且能很好地泛化到从ChartQA派生的真实图表定位基准上。

Conclusion: 本文提出的图表指代表达理解基准和代码驱动的合成管线有效解决了现有基准的局限性，所训练的实例分割模型集成到多模态定位框架后能够显著提升性能，并在真实图表数据上展现出良好的泛化能力。

Original Abstract: Referring expression grounding is a core problem in visual grounding and is widely used as a diagnostic of spatial grounding and reasoning in vision and language models, yet most prior work focuses on natural images. In contrast, existing chart referring expression grounding-related benchmarks remain limited: (1) they largely adopt bounding boxes, constraining localization precision for fine chart elements (2) they mostly assume a single and two referred target instances, failing to handle multi-instance target references; (3) the language expressions over-rely on textual cues or data-rank clues (4) they cover only a narrow range of chart types. To address these issues, we introduce a chart referring expression grounding benchmark that systematically supports multiple localization forms, multiple referred targets, diverse grounding cues and diverse chart types. Results across representative multimodal large models reveal a significant performance gap. We further introduce a code-driven synthesis pipeline that exploits the inherent alignment between plotting programs and rendered chart primitives to derive pixel accurate instance masks across chart element types and granularities. We train an instance segmentation model with the synthesized masks and integrate it into a general-purpose multimodal grounding framework. The resulting system consistently outperforms baselines on our benchmark and generalizes well to a ChartQA-derived real-chart grounding benchmark.

中文摘要翻译: 指代表达理解是视觉定位中的核心问题，被广泛用作视觉与语言模型中空间定位与推理的诊断工具，然而以往的大多数工作聚焦于自然图像。相比之下，现有的图表指代表达理解相关基准仍然存在局限：（1）它们大多采用边界框，限制了精细图表元素的定位精度；（2）它们大多假设单个或两个指代目标，无法处理多实例目标指代；（3）语言表达过度依赖文本线索或数据排名线索；（4）仅覆盖了狭窄范围的图表类型。为了解决这些问题，我们引入了一个图表指代表达理解基准，系统性地支持多种定位形式、多个指代目标、多样化的指代线索和多种图表类型。在代表性多模态大模型上的评估结果揭示了显著的性能差距。我们进一步引入了一个代码驱动的合成管线，利用绘图程序与渲染图表基元之间的内在对齐，生成跨图表元素类型和粒度的像素级精确实例掩码。我们使用合成掩码训练了一个实例分割模型，并将其集成到一个通用多模态定位框架中。最终的系统在我们的基准上持续优于基线，并且能够很好地泛化到从ChartQA派生的真实图表定位基准上。

</details>


### [57] [Learning Image-Adaptive Scale Fields for Metric Depth Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07418)
*Yuanyan Li,Matthias Althoff*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出将单目深度估计中的度量深度恢复问题建模为图像自适应尺度场建模，通过从语义和几何线索中推导低维线性组合基图，并利用稀疏度量锚点通过最小二乘求解权重，从而提升度量深度精度和鲁棒性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 单目深度估计（MDE）通常输出未知尺度或偏移的深度图，当仅有稀疏度量锚点时，恢复准确的度量深度具有挑战性，但实际应用中又十分必要。现有方法直接修正深度效果不佳，因此需要一种更有效的度量深度恢复方法。

Method: 将度量深度恢复重新表述为图像自适应尺度场建模。具体地，将深度校正建模为图像自适应基图的低维线性组合，这些基图从MDE估计和中间表示中蕴含的语义与几何线索推导得出，基图的权重通过稀疏度量锚点求解最小二乘问题高效确定。

Result: 在多个数据集和代表性MDE模型上的大量实验表明，该方法在度量深度精度上显著提升，在极端锚点稀疏条件下具有强鲁棒性，并且能够提供空间尺度变化可解释的分解。

Conclusion: 提出的图像自适应尺度场建模方法能有效从稀疏度量锚点恢复准确的度量深度，具有良好的泛化能力和可解释性。

Original Abstract: Monocular depth estimation (MDE) typically produces depth estimations that are defined up to an unknown scale or shift. When only sparse metric anchors are available, recovering accurate metric depth becomes challenging yet necessary for practical applications. We address this problem by formulating metric depth recovery as image-adaptive scale field modeling. Instead of directly correcting the depth, we reformulate the correction as a low-dimensional linear combination of image-adaptive basis maps. These maps are derived from semantic and geometric cues encoded in the MDE estimations and intermediate representations. The weights of basis maps are efficiently determined from sparse metric anchors via a least-squares problem. This formulation yields improved metric depth accuracy, strong robustness under extreme anchor sparsity, and an interpretable decomposition of spatial scale variations. Extensive experiments across multiple datasets and representative MDE models demonstrate the effectiveness and general applicability of our approach.

中文摘要翻译: 单目深度估计（MDE）通常输出定义在未知尺度或偏移下的深度估计。当仅有稀疏度量锚点时，恢复准确的度量深度变得具有挑战性，但在实际应用中却十分必要。本文通过将度量深度恢复问题公式化为图像自适应尺度场建模来解决该问题。我们不直接校正深度，而是将校正重新表述为图像自适应基图的低维线性组合。这些基图源自MDE估计及中间表示中编码的语义和几何线索。基图的权重通过最小二乘问题由稀疏度量锚点高效确定。该公式在度量深度精度上显著提升，在极端锚点稀疏条件下具有强鲁棒性，并提供空间尺度变化可解释的分解。在多个数据集和代表性MDE模型上的大量实验证明了我们方法的有效性和广泛适用性。

</details>


### [58] [Towards Photorealistic and Efficient Bokeh Rendering via Diffusion Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07429)
*Linxiao Shi,Siming Zheng,Zerong Wang,Hao Zhang,Jinwei Chen,Bo Li,Shifeng Chen,Peng-Tao Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MagicBokeh 是一个基于扩散模型的统一框架，通过联合优化散景渲染和超分辨率，并引入退化感知深度模块，实现了在低质量、高变焦图像上的高质量、高效散景渲染。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有移动设备因紧凑光学设计（如小光圈）难以产生自然真实的散景效果；现有学习方法在高数字变焦下拍摄的低分辨率图像上表现不佳；简单的两阶段方案（先增强再渲染）效率低且引入误差累积。

Method: 提出MagicBokeh统一扩散框架：(1) 替代训练策略联合优化散景渲染与超分辨率；(2) 焦点感知掩码注意力机制提升可控性与视觉保真度；(3) 退化感知深度模块实现从低质量输入中更准确的深度估计。

Result: 实验表明MagicBokeh能高效生成逼真的散景效果，尤其在真实世界的低分辨率图像上表现出色。

Conclusion: MagicBokeh通过统一框架有效解决了低质量输入下的散景渲染难题，为未来散景渲染技术的发展奠定了基础。

Original Abstract: Existing mobile devices are constrained by compact optical designs, such as small apertures, which make it difficult to produce natural, optically realistic bokeh effects. Although recent learning-based methods have shown promising results, they still struggle with photos captured under high digital zoom levels, which often suffer from reduced resolution and loss of fine details. A naive solution is to enhance image quality before applying bokeh rendering, yet this two-stage pipeline reduces efficiency and introduces unnecessary error accumulation. To overcome these limitations, we propose MagicBokeh, a unified diffusion-based framework designed for high-quality and efficient bokeh rendering. Through an alternative training strategy and a focus-aware masked attention mechanism, our method jointly optimizes bokeh rendering and super-resolution, substantially improving both controllability and visual fidelity. Furthermore, we introduce degradation-aware depth module to enable more accurate depth estimation from low-quality inputs. Experimental results demonstrate that MagicBokeh efficiently produces photorealistic bokeh effects, particularly on real-world low-resolution images, paving the way for future advancements in bokeh rendering. Our code and models are available at https://github.com/vivoCameraResearch/MagicBokeh.

中文摘要翻译: 现有移动设备受限于紧凑的光学设计（如小光圈），难以产生自然、光学上逼真的散景效果。虽然近年来基于学习的方法取得了令人鼓舞的成果，但在高数字变焦倍数下拍摄的照片上仍表现不佳，这些照片通常存在分辨率降低和细节丢失的问题。一种简单的解决方案是在应用散景渲染前先增强图像质量，但这种两阶段流水线会降低效率并引入不必要的误差累积。为克服这些局限，我们提出MagicBokeh，一个面向高质量高效散景渲染的统一扩散框架。通过替代训练策略和焦点感知掩码注意力机制，我们的方法联合优化散景渲染与超分辨率，显著提升了可控性和视觉保真度。此外，我们引入了退化感知深度模块，能够从低质量输入中实现更准确的深度估计。实验结果表明，MagicBokeh能高效生成逼真的散景效果，尤其在真实世界的低分辨率图像上表现出色，为未来散景渲染技术的发展铺平了道路。我们的代码和模型已在https://github.com/vivoCameraResearch/MagicBokeh开源。

</details>


### [59] [Sparse Autoencoders as Plug-and-Play Firewalls for Adversarial Attack Detection in VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07447)
*Hao Wang,Yiqun Sun,Pengfei Wei,Lawrence B. Hsieh,Daisuke Kawahara*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SAEgis，一种基于稀疏自编码器（SAE）的轻量级对抗攻击检测框架，无需对抗训练即可检测视觉-语言模型（VLM）输入图像是否被对抗扰动，在域内、跨域和跨攻击设置下均表现优异。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 视觉-语言模型（VLM）在现实应用中部署日益广泛，但其安全性研究相对不足，现有VLM（包括最新闭源和开源模型）仍高度易受对抗攻击，下游应用面临重大风险。

Method: 在预训练VLM中插入稀疏自编码器（SAE）模块，使用标准重建目标进行训练；SAE学到的稀疏潜藏特征天然捕捉到攻击相关信号，利用这些特征对输入图像是否被对抗扰动进行分类。

Result: 在域内、跨域和跨攻击设置下均展现出强检测性能，尤其在跨域泛化方面相比现有基线有大幅提升；融合多层信号可进一步提高鲁棒性和稳定性。

Conclusion: SAEgis是首个将SAE作为即插即用机制用于VLM对抗攻击检测的工作，无需额外对抗训练、引入极小开销，为提升真实VLM系统的安全性提供了一种实用的方法。

Original Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have advanced rapidly and are increasingly deployed in real-world applications, especially with the rise of agent-based systems. However, their safety has received relatively limited attention. Even the latest proprietary and open-weight VLMs remain highly vulnerable to adversarial attacks, leaving downstream applications exposed to significant risks. In this work, we propose a novel and lightweight adversarial attack detection framework based on sparse autoencoders (SAEs), termed SAEgis. By inserting an SAE module into a pretrained VLM and training it with standard reconstruction objectives, we find that the learned sparse latent features naturally capture attack-relevant signals. These features enable reliable classification of whether an input image has been adversarially perturbed, even for previously unseen samples. Extensive experiments show that SAEgis achieves strong performance across in-domain, cross-domain, and cross-attack settings, with particularly large improvements in cross-domain generalization compared to existing baselines. In addition, combining signals from multiple layers further improves robustness and stability. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to explore SAE as a plug-and-play mechanism for adversarial attack detection in VLMs. Our method requires no additional adversarial training, introduces minimal overhead, and provides a practical approach for improving the safety of real-world VLM systems.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉-语言模型（VLM）发展迅速，并在现实应用中的部署日益广泛，尤其是随着基于智能体的系统兴起。然而，其安全性受到的关注相对有限。即使是最新的闭源和开源VLM仍然高度易受对抗攻击，这使得下游应用面临重大风险。在这项工作中，我们提出了一种基于稀疏自编码器（SAE）的新型轻量级对抗攻击检测框架，称之为SAEgis。通过将SAE模块插入预训练VLM并使用标准重建目标进行训练，我们发现学习到的稀疏潜藏特征能够自然捕捉攻击相关信号。这些特征使得即使对于未见过的样本，也能可靠地分类输入图像是否遭受了对抗扰动。大量实验表明，SAEgis在域内、跨域和跨攻击设置下均展现出强劲性能，尤其在跨域泛化方面相比现有基线有大幅提升。此外，融合来自多个层的信号进一步提升了鲁棒性和稳定性。据我们所知，这是首个探索将SAE作为即插即用机制用于VLM对抗攻击检测的工作。我们的方法不需要额外的对抗训练，引入极小的开销，并为提升真实VLM系统的安全性提供了一种实用方案。

</details>


### [60] [EditRefiner: A Human-Aligned Agentic Framework for Image Editing Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07457)
*Zitong Xu,Huiyu Duan,Yifei Nie,Mingda Du,Sijing Wu,Xiongkuo Min,Tianyi Zheng,Jian Zhang,Shusong Xu,Jinwei Chen,Bo Li,Guangtao Zhai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EditRefiner 是一个层次化、可解释且与人类对齐的智能体框架，通过感知-推理-行动-评估循环来修正文本引导图像编辑后的细粒度问题，显著优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有文本引导图像编辑模型虽取得进展，但编辑后图像仍存在物体不自然、光照不匹配、意外改变等细粒度问题；已有的精炼方法要么依赖昂贵的迭代式重新生成，要么使用弱空间定位能力的视觉语言模型，导致语义漂移和不可靠的局部修正。

Method: 首先构建了 EditFHF-15K 细粒度人类反馈数据集，包含来自12个TIE模型的15K张图像、60K个标注伪影区域、80K个编辑失败区域及45K个MOS评分；在此基础上提出 EditRefiner 框架，由四个智能体组成：(1) 感知智能体：检测伪影和编辑失败的上下文显著性图；(2) 推理智能体：基于感知线索进行类人诊断推理；(3) 行动智能体：根据推理结果规划并执行局部重编辑；(4) 评估智能体：评估重编辑图像质量并判断是否需要进一步修正。

Result: 在失真定位、诊断准确性和人类感知对齐三个方面，EditRefiner 一致性地优于现有最先进方法，建立了一种自我修正且感知可靠的图像编辑新范式。

Conclusion: 通过引入人类反馈数据集和层次化智能体框架，将图像编辑后修正重新构建为类人的感知-推理-行动-评估循环，有效解决了细粒度编辑缺陷问题，为可自我修正的图像编辑建立了新范式。

Original Abstract: Recent text-guided image editing (TIE) models have made remarkable progress, yet edited images still frequently suffer from fine-grained issues such as unnatural objects, lighting mismatch, and unexpected changes.
