Omni TM Autoencoder (Omni TM-AE) applies this paradigm to static embedding by exploiting automaton state distributions within a single clause layer, but its training process remains slow. In this work, we propose FastOmniTMAE, a reformulation of Omni TM-AE that replaces sequential training dependencies with a two-stage parallel process: evaluation and update. Using a Single-Run Multi-Environment Benchmark covering classification, similarity, and clustering, FastOmniTMAE achieves up to 5$\times$ faster training in classification while maintaining comparable embedding quality under both Spearman and Kendall similarity measures. To address the limited efficiency of TM training on conventional GPUs, we further implement FastOmniTMAE as a reusable accelerator on SoC-FPGA platforms. The Multi-Hardware Benchmark shows that FastOmniTMAE achieves similarity scores of 0.669 on a resource-constrained FPGA and 0.696 on an UltraScale+ SoC, demonstrating efficient logic-based embedding training with a small hardware footprint.

中文摘要翻译: 自然语言处理中的嵌入模型日益依赖深层架构（如BERT），而Word2Vec等简单模型虽提供高效表示但可解释性有限。Tsetlin Machine（TM）提供了一种基于逻辑的替代学习范式。Omni TM自编码器（Omni TM-AE）通过利用单子句层内的自动机状态分布将该范式应用于静态嵌入，但其训练过程仍然缓慢。在本工作中，我们提出FastOmniTMAE，这是对Omni TM-AE的重构，将顺序训练依赖替换为两阶段并行过程：评估与更新。使用涵盖分类、相似度和聚类的单次运行多环境基准测试，FastOmniTMAE在分类训练中实现高达5倍的加速，同时在Spearman和Kendall相似度度量下保持相当的嵌入质量。针对传统GPU上TM训练效率有限的问题，我们进一步将FastOmniTMAE实现为SoC-FPGA平台上的可复用加速器。多硬件基准测试显示，FastOmniTMAE在资源受限的FPGA上达到0.669的相似度分数，在UltraScale+ SoC上达到0.696，展示了低硬件占用下高效的逻辑基嵌入训练能力。

</details>


### [152] [Response Time Enhances Alignment with Heterogeneous Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06987)
*Federico Echenique,Alireza Fallah,Baihe Huang,Michael I. Jordan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用用户响应时间作为辅助信号，结合漂移扩散模型（DDM）来估计匿名异构标注者的群体平均偏好，从而解决仅依赖二元选择数据导致的偏好不可识别问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的LLM偏好对齐方法假设所有标注者共享相同偏好，忽略了真实场景中标注者的高度异质性和匿名性，导致仅依赖二元选择数据会使学习到的策略产生扭曲，群体平均偏好不可识别。

Method: 在偏好数据中引入响应时间作为辅助信号，利用漂移扩散模型（DDM）对每个决策过程建模，提出一种新的、一致的异构偏好估计器，能够在匿名标注者仅贡献单个选择的情况下渐近收敛到真实的平均偏好。

Result: 理论证明了估计量在极端情况下的渐近一致性；在合成数据和真实数据集上，该方法始终优于标准基线方法，后者会陷入偏差平台期。

Conclusion: 响应时间几乎零成本获取且无需用户追踪或身份识别，该方法为未来数据采集流程提升社会效益开辟了新机会，无需用户级标识符或重复询问。

Original Abstract: Aligning large language models (LLMs) to human preferences typically relies on aggregating pooled feedback into a single reward model. However, this standard approach assumes that all labelers share the same underlying preferences, ignoring the fact that real-world labelers are highly heterogeneous and usually anonymous. Consequently, relying solely on binary choice data fundamentally distorts the learned policy, making the true population-average preference unidentifiable. To overcome this critical limitation, we demonstrate that augmenting preference datasets with a simple, secondary signal -- the user's response time -- can restore the identifiability of the population's average preference. By modeling each decision as a Drift-Diffusion Model (DDM), we introduce a novel, consistent estimator of heterogeneous preferences that successfully corrects the distortions of standard choice-only labels. We prove that our estimator asymptotically converges to the true average preference even in extreme cases where each anonymous labeler contributes only a single choice. Empirically, across both synthetic and real-world datasets, our method consistently outperforms standard baselines that otherwise fail and plateau at a bias floor. Because response times are essentially free to record and require zero user tracking or identification, our results bring promises and open up new opportunities for future data-collection pipelines to improve the social benefit without requiring user-level identifiers or repeated elicitations.

中文摘要翻译: 将大型语言模型（LLM）与人类偏好对齐通常依赖于将汇总的反馈聚合到单一奖励模型中。然而，这一标准方法假设所有标注者共享相同的内在偏好，忽略了现实世界中的标注者具有高度异质性且通常是匿名的这一事实。因此，仅依赖二元选择数据从根本上扭曲了学习到的策略，使得真实的群体平均偏好变得不可识别。为了克服这一关键局限，我们证明：在偏好数据集中加入一个简单的辅助信号——用户的响应时间——可以恢复群体平均偏好的可识别性。通过将每个决策建模为漂移扩散模型（DDM），我们提出了一种新的、一致的异构偏好估计器，成功纠正了标准仅含选择标签带来的偏差。我们证明，即使在每个匿名标注者仅贡献单个选择的极端情况下，我们的估计量也能渐近收敛到真实的平均偏好。在合成数据集和真实数据集上的实验表明，我们的方法始终优于那些失败并停滞在偏差平台期的标准基线方法。由于响应时间几乎零成本获取，且无需用户追踪或身份识别，我们的结果为未来数据采集流程带来了希望，并开辟了新的机遇——无需用户级标识符或重复询问即可提升社会效益。

</details>


### [153] [Dual-Agent Co-Training for Health Coaching via Implicit Adversarial Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07011)
*Da Long,Lingyi Fu,Diya Michelle Rao,Jasmine Ruales Carrera,Yang Bai,Shandian Zhe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种双智能体协同训练框架（健康教练与客户模拟器交互式共训），通过DPO优化教练、对抗性训练客户模拟器，提升AI健康教练的辅导质量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 人类健康教练稀缺且成本高昂，现有方法仅单侧优化（固定一方训练另一方），限制了交互空间探索和智能体能力边界拓展。

Method: 双智能体框架：教练智能体使用DPO（基于多维度LLM评判器识别的帕累托主导响应对）进行优化；客户模拟器通过反转上述偏好进行对抗性训练，形成隐式对抗训练动态，整个过程可解释为随机博弈。

Result: 大量实验表明该方法在多个重要维度上有效提升了辅导质量。

Conclusion: 双智能体协同对抗训练比单侧优化更能有效探索交互空间，提升目标智能体的能力边界，在AI健康教练场景中显著改善辅导质量。

Original Abstract: Motivational-interviewing-based health coaching is an effective approach for improving mental health and promoting healthy behavior change. However, the scarcity of trained human coaches and the high cost of coaching services make such support inaccessible to many people who could benefit from it. This motivates the development of AI health coaches that can provide scalable and affordable support. Existing methods typically optimize only one side of the interaction: they either train a dialogue agent against a fixed client environment or train a client simulator against a fixed assistant. This one-sided setup can limit exploration of the interaction space and may be inefficient at developing the capabilities required by the target agent and pushing its performance boundaries. In this paper, we propose a dual-agent framework that interactively co-trains both the health coach agent and the client simulator. The coach is optimized with DPO using Pareto-dominant response pairs identified by a multi-dimensional LLM judge. In turn, the client is trained adversarially by reversing these preferences, inducing an implicit adversarial training dynamic. We further show that this co-training process admits a natural stochastic-game interpretation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method effectively improves coaching quality across several important dimensions.

中文摘要翻译: 基于动机访谈的健康教练是一种改善心理健康和促进健康行为改变的有效方法。然而，受过训练的人类教练稀缺且教练服务成本高昂，使得许多本可受益的人群无法获得这种支持。这促使了AI健康教练的开发，以提供可扩展且负担得起的支持。现有方法通常只优化交互中的一侧：要么针对固定的客户环境训练对话智能体，要么针对固定的助手训练客户模拟器。这种单侧设置可能限制交互空间的探索，并且在开发目标智能体所需能力及突破其性能边界方面效率较低。本文提出一种双智能体框架，交互式地共同训练健康教练智能体和客户模拟器。教练智能体通过使用多维度LLM评判器识别的帕累托主导响应对进行DPO优化。随后，客户模拟器通过反转这些偏好进行对抗性训练，从而引入隐式对抗训练动态。我们进一步证明，该共同训练过程具有自然的随机博弈解释。大量实验表明，我们的方法在多个重要维度上有效提升了辅导质量。

</details>


### [154] [FlashMol: High-Quality Molecule Generation in as Few as Four Steps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07020)
*Xinyuan Wei,Zian Li,Shaoheng Yan,Cai Zhou,Muhan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FlashMol提出了一种超快速分子构象生成模型，利用分布匹配蒸馏（DMD）将扩散模型的采样步数从1000步压缩至4步，同时保持甚至超越原始模型的质量，实现了高达250倍的加速。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的扩散模型（如GeoLDM）虽然能生成高质量的3D分子构象，但需要数百步采样，在大规模虚拟筛选中效率低下；而现有的少步生成方法虽将步数降至12-50步，但严重牺牲了样本稳定性。因此需要一种既能保持高质量又能实现超快速生成的分子构象模型。

Method: 1) 将分布匹配蒸馏（DMD，一种反向KL散度最小化目标）适配到分子生成领域进行有效蒸馏；2) 针对DMD的局部最小化行为，重新调整分子生成的时间步长分布，为生成器提供更好的初始化；3) 引入Jensen-Shannon散度正则化项来缓解DMD的模式缺失行为（倾向于覆盖单一模式），通过结合前向KL散度的均值寻找行为来提升生成多样性。

Result: 在QM9和GEOM-DRUG数据集上的大量实验表明，FlashMol在仅需4步采样的条件下，匹配甚至超越了原始1000步教师模型的质量，实现了高达250倍的采样速度加速，同时保持了高分子的质量。

Conclusion: FlashMol通过蒸馏技术和时间步长重排，成功将分子构象生成的采样步数压缩至极低（4步），在保持高质量的同时实现了数量级的加速，为大规模计算药物筛选提供了实用的超快速分子生成方案。

Original Abstract: Generating chemically valid 3D molecular conformations is critical for computational drug discovery. Classical diffusion-based models like GeoLDM perform well but require hundreds of steps, making large-scale in silico screening impractical. Recent efforts on few-step molecular generation have accelerated this process to 12-50 steps, but they often largely sacrifice sample stability. In this work, we present FlashMol, an ultra-fast molecule generative model producing high-quality molecular conformations in as few as 4 steps. To achieve this, we adapt distribution matching distillation (DMD) - a reverse KL-divergence minimization objective - to the molecular domain for effective distillation. Considering the local minimization behavior of DMD, we respace the molecule generation timesteps, providing the generator with much better initialization and enables effective distillation. Additionally, to mitigate the mode-seeking behavior of DMD and improve diversity, we further regularize it with a Jensen-Shannon divergence term, which incorporates the mean-seeking behavior of the forward KL divergence. Extensive experiments on QM9 and GEOM-DRUG datasets demonstrate that FlashMol matches and even surpasses the original 1000-step teacher, achieving up to 250$\times$ acceleration in sampling speed while maintaining high molecular quality.

中文摘要翻译: 生成化学有效的3D分子构象对于计算药物发现至关重要。经典的基于扩散的模型如GeoLDM表现良好，但需要数百步采样，使得大规模计算机筛选不切实际。近期关于少步分子生成的努力将这一过程加速至12-50步，但通常严重牺牲了样本稳定性。在这项工作中，我们提出了FlashMol，一个超快速分子生成模型，能够在仅4步内生成高质量的分子构象。为实现这一目标，我们将分布匹配蒸馏（DMD）——一种反向KL散度最小化目标——适配到分子领域以实现有效蒸馏。考虑到DMD的局部最小化行为，我们重新调整了分子生成的时间步长分布，为生成器提供了更好的初始化并实现了有效蒸馏。此外，为了缓解DMD的模式寻找行为并提高多样性，我们进一步用Jensen-Shannon散度项对其进行正则化，该散度项融合了前向KL散度的均值寻找行为。在QM9和GEOM-DRUG数据集上的大量实验表明，FlashMol匹配甚至超越了原始1000步教师模型，在保持高分子质量的同时实现了高达250倍的采样速度加速。

</details>


### [155] [Self Driving Datasets: From 20 Million Papers to Nuanced Biomedical Knowledge at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07022)
*Haydn Jones,Yimeng Zeng,Alden Rose,Li S. Yifei,Yining Huang,Kaiwen Wu,Jiaming Liang,Maggie Ziyu Huan,Yoseph Barash,Cesar de la Fuente-Nunez,Osbert Bastani,Zachary Ives,Mark Yatskar,Jacob R. Gardner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Starling系统，利用LLM将PubMed文献自动转化为结构化数据集，在规模、准确性和信息丰富度上超越传统人工 curated 生物医学数据库，并生成了多个领域最大的公开数据集。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 人工维护的生物医学数据库成本高昂、更新滞后于文献、且丢弃了实验上下文信息，导致数据正确性和覆盖范围的评估困难。因此需要一种自动化方法将文献直接转化为更大、更细致、更准确的结构化数据集。

Method: (1) 基于9个生物医学本体的LLM实体标注流水线，在2250万篇论文、2.5万亿token的PubMed语料库中标注45亿实体；(2) 支持实体过滤语义查询的混合稀疏-稠密检索系统；(3) Starling多智能体深度研究系统，根据自然语言任务描述自动设计检索过滤器、生成提取模式、输出带有丰富上下文的结构化记录。

Result: 在血脑屏障通透性、口服生物利用度、急性毒性(LD50)、基因-疾病关联、蛋白质亚细胞定位和化学反应六项任务中生成约630万条记录，多项为对应领域最大公开数据集。前沿模型对提取结果的拒绝率仅0.6-7.7%，远低于常用人工 curated 数据库的错误率(如BBB_Martins 16.5%, Bioavailability_Ma 7.3%)。

Conclusion: 该语料库、检索系统和智能体的组合为AI驱动的治疗设计奠定了基础，证明了LLM可以自主且经济地将PubMed转化为比人工 curated 数据库更大、更细致、更准确的结构化数据集。

Original Abstract: Manually curated biomedical repositories -- spanning bioactivity, genomics, and chemistry -- are expensive to maintain, lag behind primary literature, and discard experimental context, obscuring nuances needed to assess data correctness and coverage. We show that PubMed itself can be autonomously and cost-effectively turned into structured datasets that are larger, more nuanced, and more accurate than the curated databases they replace. We present three coupled contributions: (1) an LLM-based entity-tagging pipeline, grounded in nine biomedical ontologies, that tags 4.5B entities across 19 categories in a 22.5M-paper, 2.5T-token PubMed corpus; (2) hybrid sparse-dense retrieval supporting entity-filtered semantic queries over the tagged corpus; and (3) Starling, a multi-agent deep research system that, given only a natural-language task description, designs precision- and recall-targeted retrieval filters, induces an extraction schema, and emits structured records with nuance-rich fields and supporting passages. Across six tasks -- blood-brain barrier permeability, oral bioavailability, acute toxicity (LD50), gene-disease associations, protein subcellular localization, and chemical reactions -- Starling produces ~6.3M records (91K-3M per task); several are, to our knowledge, the largest public datasets for their property. Frontier-model rejection of our extractions is 0.6-7.7% across tasks, far below error rates we measure on widely used curated counterparts (e.g., 16.5% on BBB_Martins, 7.3% on Bioavailability_Ma). Beyond scale and accuracy, the supporting passages carry nuance tabular databases discard -- e.g., oral bioavailability may depend on fed vs. fasted state. Together, the corpus, retrieval, and agent establish a foundation for AI-driven therapeutic design. Code and datasets: https://github.com/starling-labs/starling.

中文摘要翻译: 人工 curated 的生物医学数据库——涵盖生物活性、基因组学和化学等领域——维护成本高昂、滞后于原始文献，并丢弃了实验上下文，掩盖了评估数据正确性和覆盖范围所需的细微信息。我们证明，PubMed本身可以自主且经济高效地转化为结构化数据集，这些数据集比它们所取代的人工 curated 数据库更大、更细致、更准确。我们提出三项耦合贡献：(1) 一个基于LLM的实体标注流水线，基于九个生物医学本体，在包含2250万篇论文、2.5万亿token的PubMed语料库中标注了19个类别的45亿实体；(2) 支持实体过滤语义查询的混合稀疏-稠密检索系统；(3) Starling，一个多智能体深度研究系统，仅需给定自然语言任务描述，即可设计面向精确率和召回率的检索过滤器，推导出提取模式，并输出包含丰富上下文字段和支持性段落的结构化记录。在六项任务——血脑屏障通透性、口服生物利用度、急性毒性(LD50)、基因-疾病关联、蛋白质亚细胞定位和化学反应——中，Starling生成了约630万条记录（每项任务91K-300万条）；据我们所知，其中多项是各自性质的最大公开数据集。前沿模型对我们提取结果的拒绝率为0.6-7.7%，远低于我们在广泛使用的人工 curated 对照数据库上测得的错误率（例如BBB_Martins 16.5%，Bioavailability_Ma 7.3%）。除了规模和准确性，支持性段落还承载了表格数据库所丢弃的细微信息——例如，口服生物利用度可能取决于进食与空腹状态。该语料库、检索系统和智能体共同为AI驱动的治疗设计奠定了基础。代码和数据集：https://github.com/starling-labs/starling。

</details>


### [156] [Delulu: A Verified Multi-Lingual Benchmark for Code Hallucination Detection in Fill-in-the-Middle Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07024)
*Mahdi Erfanian,Nelson Daniel Troncoso,Aashna Garg,Amabel Gale,Xiaoyu Liu,Pareesa Ameneh Golnari,Shengyu Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Delulu，一个多语言代码补全幻觉检测基准，包含 1,951 个 FIM 样本，覆盖 7 种语言和 4 类幻觉。通过对抗性流水线生成、多模型评估、聚类挖掘、Docker 验证和人工审核构建。评估 11 个开源模型发现，最强模型仅达 84.5% pass@1，证实该任务存在本质困难。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 大语言模型在代码生成的 Fill-in-the-Middle (FIM) 任务中频繁产生幻觉（如虚构 API、无效参数、未定义变量、不存在导入），这些错误表面上合理但导致运行时错误，现有基准难以有效检测此类问题。

Method: 通过对抗性流水线构建基准：1) 前沿 LLM 生成看似合理的幻觉样本；2) 四个不同 Judge 模型评估；3) 基于嵌入的聚类挖掘难度递增的样本；4) Docker 容器验证黄金补全能编译而幻觉变体产生运行时错误；5) 人工专家审查去除有偏或可简单判定的样本。最终得到 1,951 个 FIM 样本，涵盖 7 种语言和 4 类幻觉。

Result: 评估 11 个开源 FIM 模型（0.5B-32B，5 个模型家族）：最强模型仅 84.5% pass@1，无家族超过 0.77 Edit Similarity，每个家族都在不可忽略的样本上产生与幻觉对齐的补全，证明幻觉困难是任务固有的而非模型家族特异的。

Conclusion: Delulu 基准暴露的代码补全幻觉困难是任务内在的，所有现有模型均无法完美解决，该基准可用于未来模型的鲁棒性评估。数据集、容器和评估框架已开源。

Original Abstract: Large Language Models for code generation frequently produce hallucinations in Fill-in-the-Middle (FIM) tasks -- plausible but incorrect completions such as invented API methods, invalid parameters, undefined variables, or non-existent imports. These failures pass superficial review yet introduce runtime errors. We introduce Delulu, a verified multi-lingual benchmark of 1,951 FIM samples across 7 languages and 4 hallucination types. Samples are curated through an adversarial pipeline: a frontier LLM generates plausible hallucinations, four diverse judge models evaluate them, embedding-based clustering mines progressively harder examples, self-contained Docker containers verify that golden completions compile while hallucinated variants produce the expected runtime error, and a final human-expert review removes any remaining biased or trivially decidable samples. We evaluate 11 open-weight FIM models from five families spanning 0.5B-32B parameters: a six-point Qwen2.5-Coder scaling slate, plus a cross-family slate (CodeLlama, DeepSeek-Coder-V2, StarCoder2). The strongest model reaches only 84.5% pass@1, no family exceeds 0.77 Edit Similarity, and every family produces hallucination-aligned completions on a non-trivial share of samples, confirming that the difficulty exposed by Delulu is task-intrinsic rather than family-specific. We release the benchmark, containers, and evaluation framework at https://github.com/microsoft/delulu.

中文摘要翻译: 用于代码生成的大语言模型在"中间填充"（Fill-in-the-Middle, FIM）任务中频繁产生幻觉——即看似合理但实际错误的补全，例如虚构的 API 方法、无效参数、未定义变量或不存在的导入。这些错误能通过表面审查，却会在运行时引发错误。我们提出了 Delulu，一个经过验证的多语言基准，包含 1,951 个 FIM 样本，覆盖 7 种编程语言和 4 类幻觉。样本通过对抗性流水线精心筛选：由前沿 LLM 生成看似合理的幻觉，四种不同的 Judge 模型进行评估，基于嵌入的聚类挖掘难度递增的样本，自包含的 Docker 容器验证黄金补全能正常编译而幻觉变体产生预期的运行时错误，最后经领域专家审查去除任何残留的有偏样本或可简单判定的样本。我们评估了来自 5 个模型家族的 11 个开源 FIM 模型（参数量从 0.5B 到 32B）：包括 Qwen2.5-Coder 的 6 点规模渐进系列，以及跨家族系列（CodeLlama、DeepSeek-Coder-V2、StarCoder2）。最强模型仅达到 84.5% 的 pass@1，没有任何模型家族的 Edit Similarity 超过 0.77，且每个家族都在不可忽略的样本上产生了与幻觉对齐的补全，这证实了 Delulu 所暴露的困难是任务固有的，而非特定于某个模型家族。我们已在 https://github.com/microsoft/delulu 开源了该基准、容器和评估框架。

</details>


### [157] [A Systematic Investigation of The RL-Jailbreaker in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07032)
*Montaser Mohammedalamen,Kevin Roice,Reginald McLean,Alyssa Lefaivre Škopac*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统分解了强化学习（RL）越狱攻击框架，将RL越狱拆解为问题形式化（奖励函数、动作空间、回合长度）和算法措施（RL算法、训练数据、奖励塑形）两个维度，揭示了密集奖励和延长回合长度是越狱成功的主要结构性驱动因素。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 尽管强化学习已被用于将越狱建模为多步攻击的序列优化问题，但该框架为何能成功实现越狱的机制性理解仍然不完整，因此需要系统性地分析RL越狱的结构性决定因素。

Method: 首次对RL越狱框架进行系统分解，将其拆解为问题形式化（奖励函数、动作空间、回合长度）和算法措施（RL算法、训练数据、奖励塑形）两个维度，通过控制变量实验分析各组件对越狱成功率的贡献。

Result: RL越狱攻击成功攻破了所有目标模型和安全防护措施；环境形式化（特别是密集奖励和延长回合长度）是越狱成功的主要驱动因素。

Conclusion: 该分析为提高RL越狱攻击效率提供了工具，并最终有助于强化生成模型对基于RL的攻击的抵抗力。

Original Abstract: The evolution of generative models from next-token predictors to autonomous engines of complex systems necessitates rigorous safety hardening. Adversarial jailbreaking, the strategic manipulation of models to elicit harmful output, remains a primary threat to safe deployment. While Reinforcement Learning (RL) frames jailbreaking as a multi-step attack through sequential optimization, a mechanistic understanding of why the framework succeeds remains incomplete. To fill this gap, we present the first systematic decomposition of RL jailbreaking. We deconstruct the framework into problem formalization (reward function, action space, episode length), and algorithmic measures (RL algorithm, training data, reward-shaping) to identify the structural determinants of adversarial success. Our results reveal that the RL-jailbreaker successfully compromised all targeted models and safeguards. Through this first-of-its-kind analysis, we demonstrate that environment formalization, specifically dense rewards and extended episode lengths, is the primary driver of jailbreaking success. This work provides a tool for improving RL-jailbreaker efficiency and, ultimately, harden generative models resistant to RL-based attacks.

中文摘要翻译: 生成模型从下个词预测器向复杂系统自主引擎的演变，迫切需要严格的安全性加固。对抗性越狱——通过策略性操控模型以诱导其输出有害内容——仍然是安全部署的主要威胁。尽管强化学习将越狱框架化为通过序列优化的多步攻击，但该框架为何能成功实现越狱的机制性理解仍然不完整。为填补这一空白，我们首次对RL越狱进行了系统性分解。我们将该框架拆解为问题形式化（奖励函数、动作空间、回合长度）和算法措施（RL算法、训练数据、奖励塑形）两个维度，以识别对抗性成功的结构性决定因素。实验结果表明，RL越狱攻击成功攻破了所有目标模型和安全防护措施。通过这一首次分析，我们证明了环境形式化，特别是密集奖励和延长回合长度，是越狱成功的主要驱动因素。这项工作为提高RL越狱攻击效率提供了工具，并最终有助于强化生成模型对基于RL的攻击的抵抗力。

</details>


### [158] [Learning Material-Aware Hamiltonian Risk Fields for Safe Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07038)
*Aditya Sai Ellendula,Yi Wang,Chandrajit Bajaj*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于上下文能量的port-Hamiltonian导航策略，通过学习力通道实现选择性风险感知导航：仅在存在可行低风险方向时激活规避力，否则抑制横向机动，从而避免不安全的幻觉动作。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有风险感知导航策略缺乏选择性——在无安全替代方案时仍可能激活规避行为，导致不安全的幻觉动作。理想策略应仅当局部场景存在更低风险的可行机动时才暴露规避自由度，否则应抑制该行为。

Method: 在port-Hamiltonian导航策略中增加一个上下文能量项，产生一个具有可证伪选择性的学习力通道。当局部风险场中存在可行低风险方向时，上下文力朝该方向激活；当逃逸路径被阻塞或不可用时，路径感知门控抑制横向力。使用CVaR尾部风险目标函数使梯度更新聚焦于罕见但后果严重的风险转换。

Result: 在延迟必需逃逸基准中，路线感知CVaR将过早力激活从0.950降至0.180（vs DWA），成功率从0.480提升至0.810且零重规划。RELLIS-3D真实越野地形上，正确激活率0.837、错误激活率0.114（vs 标量风险梯度0.378/0.752）。DFC2018静态语义地图上，灾难性失败从0.60降至0.10，振荡减少90.7%。高速公路场景中，车道逃逸可行时碰撞率从100%降至0%；无逃逸路径时策略抑制横向机动。

Conclusion: 选择性属性源于上下文能量的梯度结构而非训练时调参，该方法在多种场景下显著优于现有方法，实现了真正有选择性的风险感知导航。

Original Abstract: Risk-aware navigation should be selective: a policy should expose evasive degrees of freedom only when the local scene admits a lower-risk feasible maneuver, and suppress them when no safer alternative exists. We show that adding one context-energy term to a port-Hamiltonian navigation policy produces a learned force channel with exactly this falsifiable signature. When the local risk field contains a feasible lower-risk direction, the induced context force activates toward it; when the apparent escape is blocked or not yet available, a route-aware gate suppresses lateral force rather than hallucinating an unsafe maneuver. A CVaR tail-risk objective focuses gradient updates on rare but consequential risk transitions. We validate the selectivity signature across four settings. In the primary delayed-required-escape benchmark, route-aware CVaR reduces premature force activation from 0.950 to 0.180 versus DWA while raising success from 0.480 to 0.810 with zero replans. On real off-road terrain (RELLIS-3D), route-aware enrichment achieves correct activation rate 0.837 and false activation rate 0.114, compared to 0.378/0.752 for scalar risk gradients. On static semantic maps (DFC2018), enrichment reduces catastrophic failure from 0.60 to 0.10 and oscillation by 90.7% while preserving path efficiency. In highway traffic, collisions drop from 100% to 0% when a lane escape is feasible; when no escape exists, the policy suppresses the lateral maneuver. The selectivity property follows from the gradient structure of the context energy rather than from training-time tuning.

中文摘要翻译: 风险感知导航应当具有选择性：一个策略应仅在局部场景允许存在更低风险的可行机动时才暴露规避自由度，并在没有更安全替代方案时抑制这些自由度。我们证明，向port-Hamiltonian导航策略添加一个上下文能量项，能产生一个具有这种可证伪选择性的学习力通道。当局部风险场中包含一个可行的低风险方向时，诱导的上下文力朝该方向激活；当明显的逃逸路径被阻塞或尚不可用时，一种路径感知门控机制抑制横向力，而非幻觉出一个不安全的机动动作。CVaR尾部风险目标函数使梯度更新聚焦于罕见但后果严重的风险转换。我们在四个场景中验证了选择性特征。在主要的延迟必需逃逸基准测试中，路线感知CVaR将过早力激活率从0.950降至0.180（对比DWA），同时将成功率从0.480提升至0.810，且零重规划。在真实越野地形（RELLIS-3D）上，路线感知增强实现了0.837的正确激活率和0.114的错误激活率，而标量风险梯度的对应值为0.378/0.752。在静态语义地图（DFC2018）上，增强将灾难性失败从0.60降至0.10，振荡减少90.7%，同时保持路径效率。在高速公路交通场景中，当车道逃逸可行时碰撞率从100%降至0%；当无逃逸路径存在时，该策略抑制横向机动。选择性性质源于上下文能量的梯度结构，而非训练时的调参。

</details>


### [159] [PACEvolve++: Improving Test-time Learning for Evolutionary Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07039)
*Minghao Yan,Bo Peng,Benjamin Coleman,Ziqi Chen,Zhouhang Xie,Shuo Chen,Zhankui He,Noveen Sachdeva,Weili Wang,Ed H. Chi,Shivaram Venkataraman,Wang-Cheng Kang,Derek Zhiyuan Cheng,Beidou Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PACEvolve++框架，使用可训练的advisor模型通过强化学习在测试时动态调整进化搜索策略，在不同进化阶段采用不同的优化方法，在负载均衡、序列推荐和蛋白质适应度外推任务上优于现有最先进方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有进化搜索系统依赖固定提示策略采样候选解，在评估昂贵、需要学习任务特定搜索动态的实际场景中适应性不足。

Method: 提出advisor-model强化学习框架PACEvolve++，将搜索策略决策与实现解耦：可训练advisor生成、评估和选择假设，更强的前沿模型将假设转化为可执行候选。提出阶段自适应方法，进化早期使用组相对反馈学习广泛偏好，后期使用best-of-k前沿贡献支持稳定细化。

Result: 在专家并行负载均衡、序列推荐和蛋白质适应度外推任务上，PACEvolve++优于使用前沿模型的最先进进化搜索框架，收敛更快，且在进化搜索中稳定了测试时训练。

Conclusion: PACEvolve++通过解耦决策与实现、结合阶段自适应优化，有效提升了进化搜索在昂贵评估场景下的适应性和收敛效率。

Original Abstract: Large language models have become drivers of evolutionary search, but most systems rely on a fixed, prompt-elicited policy to sample next candidates. This limits adaptation in practical engineering and research tasks, where evaluations are expensive, and progress depends on learning task-specific search dynamics. We introduce PACEvolve++, an advisor-model reinforcement learning framework for test-time policy adaptation in evolutionary search agents. PACEvolve++ decouples strategic search decisions from implementation: a trainable advisor generates, assesses, and selects hypotheses, while a stronger frontier model translates selected hypotheses into executable candidates. To train the advisor under non-stationary feedback, we propose a phase-adaptive approach that adapts its optimization strategy to different phases of the evolutionary process. Early in evolution, it uses group-relative feedback to learn broad search preferences; later, as reward gaps compress, it emphasizes best-of-$k$ frontier contribution to support stable refinement. Across expert-parallel load balancing, sequential recommendation, and protein fitness extrapolation, PACEvolve++ outperforms the state-of-the-art evolutionary search framework with frontier models, achieving faster convergence and stabilizing test-time training during evolutionary search.

中文摘要翻译: 大语言模型已成为进化搜索的驱动引擎，但大多数系统依赖固定的、通过提示引发的策略来采样下一候选解。这在评估代价高昂且进展依赖于学习任务特定搜索动态的实际工程和研究任务中限制了适应性。我们提出PACEvolve++，一个用于进化搜索智能体中测试时策略自适应的顾问-模型强化学习框架。PACEvolve++将策略搜索决策与实现解耦：一个可训练的顾问生成、评估和选择假设，而一个更强的前沿模型将选中的假设转化为可执行的候选解。为了在非平稳反馈下训练顾问，我们提出一种阶段自适应方法，使其优化策略适应进化过程的不同阶段。在进化早期，它使用组相对反馈来学习广泛的搜索偏好；后期当奖励差距压缩时，它强调基于best-of-k前沿贡献来支持稳定精细优化。在专家并行负载均衡、序列推荐和蛋白质适应度外推任务上，PACEvolve++优于使用前沿模型的最先进进化搜索框架，实现了更快的收敛速度，并在进化搜索过程中稳定了测试时训练。

</details>


### [160] [Unlocking High-Fidelity Molecular Generation from Mass Spectra via Dual-Stream Line Graph Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07048)
*Xujun Che,Xiuxia Du,Depeng Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出双流线图扩散模型DualLGD，将分子图去噪重构为原子级和键级推理两个耦合子问题的交替求解，通过线图构建键空间、结构约束交叉注意力同步双流，在质谱分子生成任务上取得约3倍于先前最优的性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有图扩散方法采用单流范式处理原子和键，原子-键信息只能在层间隐式同步，这种单流架构（而非特定聚合核的选择）是核心架构瓶颈。

Method: 提出双流线图扩散模型DualLGD：(1) 将分子图去噪重构为原子级推理和键级推理两个耦合子问题的交替求解，每个子问题在专用表示空间中运行；(2) 利用线图作为键空间的自然数学构造，使键角、二面角、共轭链和环对应键之间的局部拓扑模式；(3) 采用关联约束双向交叉注意力在每一层同步两个流，确保每个原子只关注其入射键，反之亦然。

Result: 在NPLIB1和MassSpecGym基准上，top-1准确率分别达34.37%和23.89%，约为先前最优方法的3倍。消融实验证实架构是主要改进来源：无预训练的DualLGD已超越先前最佳的全预训练模型。

Conclusion: 双流范式打破了单流架构的瓶颈，为质谱分子生成提供了更优的解决方案。

Original Abstract: De novo molecular generation from tandem mass spectra is a challenging inverse problem whose core difficulty lies in the circular dependency between atom-level and bond-level reasoning: determining a bond's type requires knowing its endpoint atoms' chemical environment, yet an atom's environment is in turn defined by its incident bonds. Existing graph diffusion methods process atoms and bonds within a single computation stream, where atom-bond information synchronization can only occur implicitly across layers. We argue that this single-stream paradigm, rather than the choice of any particular aggregation kernel, is a key architectural bottleneck. We propose DualLGD (Dual-stream Line Graph Diffusion), which reformulates molecular graph denoising as the alternating solution of two coupled subproblems: atom-level reasoning and bond-level reasoning, each operating in its own dedicated representation space. The line graph provides a natural mathematical construction for the bond space, in which bond angles, dihedrals, conjugation chains, and rings correspond to local topological motifs between bonds. Incidence-constrained bidirectional cross-attention synchronizes the two streams at every layer, ensuring that each atom attends only to its incident bonds and vice versa, respecting the fundamental chemical principle that an atom's environment is determined by its bonding context. On the NPLIB1 and MassSpecGym benchmarks, DualLGD achieves top-1 accuracy of 34.37\% and 23.89\%, approximately $3\times$ the previous state of the art. Ablation studies confirm the architecture as the primary source of improvement: DualLGD without any pre-training already surpasses the previous best fully pretrained model.

中文摘要翻译: 从串联质谱数据出发进行从头分子生成是一个具有挑战性的逆问题，其核心困难在于原子级推理与键级推理之间的循环依赖关系：确定键的类型需要知道其端点原子的化学环境，而原子的环境又由其入射键所定义。现有的图扩散方法在单一计算流中处理原子和键，原子-键信息只能隐式地在各层之间同步。我们认为，这种单流范式（而非任何特定聚合核的选择）才是关键架构瓶颈。我们提出DualLGD（双流线图扩散），将分子图去噪重构为两个耦合子问题——原子级推理和键级推理——的交替求解，每个子问题在其专用表示空间中运行。线图为键空间提供了自然的数学构造，其中键角、二面角、共轭链和环对应于键之间的局部拓扑模式。关联约束的双向交叉注意力在每一层同步两个流，确保每个原子只关注其入射键，反之亦然，这遵循了原子环境由其键合上下文决定的基本化学原理。在NPLIB1和MassSpecGym基准上，DualLGD的top-1准确率分别达到34.37%和23.89%，约为先前最优方法的3倍。消融研究证实架构是改进的主要来源：没有任何预训练的DualLGD已经超越了先前最佳的全预训练模型。

</details>


### [161] [Towards Differentially Private Reinforcement Learning with General Function Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07049)
*Yi He,Xingyu Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次为具有通用函数逼近的差分隐私在线强化学习提供了理论保证，将隐私机制的适用范围从表格型和线性设置扩展到了更一般的函数逼近场景。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 以往差分隐私在线强化学习的理论保证仅局限于表格型和线性设置，缺乏对更一般的函数逼近场景的理论支持。本文旨在填补这一空白，为通用函数逼近下的隐私在线强化学习提供首个理论保证。

Method: 结合批量策略更新方案与指数机制（exponential mechanism），并配以新颖的遗憾分析（regret analysis）。

Result: 在通用函数逼近下，模型无关设置的遗憾值达到了与线性情况相当的水平，量级为 $\widetilde{O}(K^{3/5})$（K为回合数）。此外，首次建立了依赖于可覆盖性（coverability）这一标准复杂度度量的批量更新在线强化学习的遗憾界，并揭示了线性函数逼近下隐私强化学习现有结果中的根本性缺陷。

Conclusion: 本文证明了即使在通用函数逼近下，差分隐私在线强化学习也能达到与线性情况相媲美的性能，同时澄清了该领域现有结果中的不足，为后续研究指明了方向。

Original Abstract: We present the first theoretical guarantees for differentially private online reinforcement learning (RL) with general function approximation, extending beyond prior work restricted to tabular and linear settings. Our approach combines a batched policy update scheme with the exponential mechanism, together with a novel regret analysis. We show that, even under general function approximation, the regret in the model-free setting under differential privacy matches the state of the art for the linear case, scaling as $\widetilde{O}(K^{3/5})$, where $K$ denotes the number of episodes. As an important by-product, we also establish the first regret bound for online RL with batch update that depends on the standard complexity measure of coverability, complementing existing results based on a newly introduced Eluder-Condition class. In addition, we uncover fundamental gaps in recent results for private RL with linear function approximation, thereby clarifying its landscape.

中文摘要翻译: 我们首次为具有通用函数逼近的差分隐私在线强化学习提供了理论保证，将先前仅限于表格型和线性设置的工作进行了扩展。我们的方法将批量策略更新方案与指数机制相结合，并配以新颖的遗憾分析。我们表明，即使在通用函数逼近下，模型无关设置中的差分隐私遗憾值也达到了与线性情况相当的水平，量级为 $\widetilde{O}(K^{3/5})$，其中 $K$ 表示回合数。作为一个重要的副产品，我们还首次建立了依赖于可覆盖性这一标准复杂度度量的批量更新在线强化学习的遗憾界，对基于新引入的Eluder-Condition类的结果进行了补充。此外，我们揭示了线性函数逼近下隐私强化学习近期结果中的根本性缺陷，从而澄清了该领域的研究格局。

</details>


### [162] [Dr. Post-Training: A Data Regularization Perspective on LLM Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07063)
*Pingbang Hu,Xueshen Liu,Z. Morley Mao,Jiaqi W. Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Dr. Post-Training框架，将通用训练数据重新定义为数据诱导的正则化器，通过约束模型更新方向来防止对稀缺目标数据的过拟合，并引入一系列方法实现更灵活的偏差-方差权衡，在SFT、RLHF和RLVR任务上全面超越现有数据选择方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有数据选择方法虽能利用通用训练数据辅助稀缺的高质量目标数据，但其核心是将通用数据视为待选择的池子，未能充分挖掘其作为正则化约束的潜力，难以实现最优的偏差-方差权衡。

Method: 提出Dr. Post-Training框架：在每个训练步中，利用通用训练数据构建模型更新方向的可行集，然后将稀缺目标数据指定的更新方向投影到该可行集上。标准训练和数据选择方法可视为该框架的特例。进一步提出一系列方法以扩展设计空间，并通过系统优化实现LLM规模下的高效部署。

Result: 在SFT、RLHF和RLVR任务上的大量实验表明，所提方法一致地优于最先进的数据选择基线方法；系统基准测试确认了其计算效率。

Conclusion: 将通用训练数据重新概念化为数据诱导的正则化器（而非数据选择池）是一种更优的范式，能够提供更灵活的偏差-方差权衡，并在多种后训练场景中取得一致领先的性能。

Original Abstract: Data selection methods address a critical challenge in LLM post-training: effectively leveraging scarce, high-fidelity target data alongside abundant but imperfectly aligned general training data. In this work, we move beyond the data-selection framing and introduce Dr. Post-Training (Data-Regularized Post-Training), a novel framework that reconceptualizes general training data as a data-induced regularizer that prevents overfitting to the scarce target objective, rather than serving as a pool for selection. Specifically, our framework proposes that at each training step, construct a feasible set of model update directions using the general training data, and project the model update direction specified by the scarce target data onto that feasible set. Standard training and existing data selection methods arise as special cases with different choices of the data-induced regularizer, and these methods correspond to different points on a bias--variance spectrum with different regularization strength. Building on this view, we propose a family of methods offering a richer design space and more flexible bias--variance tradeoffs. For practical LLM-scale use, we introduce careful system optimizations that realize these methods with minimal overhead. Extensive experiments across SFT, RLHF, and RLVR show that our methods consistently outperform state-of-the-art data selection baselines, and system benchmarks confirm their efficiency.

中文摘要翻译: 数据选择方法解决了大语言模型后训练中的一个关键挑战：如何有效利用稀缺的高保真目标数据以及丰富但对齐不完美的通用训练数据。在本工作中，我们超越了数据选择的框架，提出了Dr. Post-Training（数据正则化后训练），一种新颖的框架，将通用训练数据重新概念化为一种数据诱导的正则化器，用于防止对稀缺目标目标的过拟合，而非作为待选择的池子。具体而言，我们的框架提出，在每个训练步骤中，利用通用训练数据构建模型更新方向的可行集，并将由稀缺目标数据指定的模型更新方向投影到该可行集上。标准训练和现有数据选择方法均是该框架在数据诱导正则化器不同选择下的特例，这些方法对应于偏差-方差谱系上具有不同正则化强度的不同点。基于这一视角，我们提出了一系列方法，提供了更丰富的设计空间和更灵活的偏差-方差权衡。为了在大规模LLM上实用，我们引入了精心的系统优化，以最小化开销实现这些方法。在SFT、RLHF和RLVR上的大量实验表明，我们的方法一致优于最先进的数据选择基线，系统基准测试也证实了其效率。

</details>


### [163] [Less Random, More Private: What is the Optimal Subsampling Scheme for DP-SGD?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07072)
*Andy Dong,Ayfer Özgür*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明在差分隐私机器学习中，泊松子采样因参与方差导致次优的隐私放大效果，而提出的平衡迭代子采样（BIS）通过结构化参与（每个样本固定迭代次数）实现了更强的隐私放大，在噪声谱两端最优，并在DP-SDG中可将噪声乘子降低高达9.6%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 泊松子采样虽因其非结构化随机性便于隐私放大分析而被广泛采用，但其引入了显著的参与方差（每个样本在不同训练迭代中出现的次数差异大）。本文旨在证明这种方差不仅是实践中的瑕疵，更是导致隐私放大次优的根本原因，并探索结构化采样能否获得更强的隐私保障。

Method: 提出平衡迭代子采样（BIS）方法，使每个样本恰好参与固定次数的迭代，消除参与方差的同时保留各迭代边际参与率的均匀性。为将渐近理论转化为有限噪声保证，引入了一种实用的近精确蒙特卡洛隐私会计方法（Monte Carlo accountant），消除了现有RDP和基于组合的PLD分析中的分析松弛量。

Result: 理论分析证明BIS在噪声谱两端（σ→0和σ→∞）均达到最优；在超过60种实际DP-SDG配置下的评估中，BIS在低噪声区域（高实用性私密训练的关键区域）一致优于泊松子采样，噪声乘子最多降低9.6%。

Conclusion: 推翻了"更多采样随机性必然带来更强隐私放大"的直觉：在DP-SDG中，结构化参与既可以更实用也可以更私密。

Original Abstract: Poisson subsampling is the default sampling scheme in differentially private machine learning, largely because its unstructured randomness yields tractable privacy amplification analyses. Yet this same randomness introduces substantial participation variance: each sample appears in very different numbers of training iterations. In this work, we show that this variance is not merely a practical artifact to be tolerated, but a fundamental source of suboptimal privacy amplification. We prove that Balanced Iteration Subsampling (BIS), a structured scheme in which each sample participates in exactly a fixed number of iterations, achieves stronger privacy amplification than Poisson subsampling and is optimal at both extremes of the noise spectrum ($σ\to 0$ and $σ\to \infty$). Our analysis reveals that the privacy-noise tradeoff is governed not by maximizing randomness, but by eliminating participation variance while preserving uniform marginal participation across iterations. To translate this asymptotic theory into finite-noise guarantees, we introduce a practical near-exact Monte Carlo accountant for BIS, which removes the analytical slack of existing RDP and composition-based PLD analyses. Evaluations across more than 60 practical DP-SGD configurations show that BIS consistently outperforms Poisson subsampling in the low-noise regimes most relevant for high-utility private training, reducing the required noise multiplier by up to $9.6\%$. These results overturn the common intuition that more sampling randomness necessarily yields stronger privacy amplification: in DP-SGD, structured participation can be both more practical and more private. Our implementation is available at https://github.com/dong-xin-ao-andy/bis-mc-accountant.

中文摘要翻译: 泊松子采样是差分隐私机器学习中的默认采样方案，主要是因为其非结构化随机性带来了可处理的隐私放大分析。然而，这种随机性引入了显著的参与方差：每个样本出现在训练迭代中的次数差异很大。在本文中，我们证明这种方差不仅是实践中的瑕疵需要容忍，更是导致隐私放大次优的根本原因。我们证明平衡迭代子采样（BIS）——一种每个样本恰好参与固定次数迭代的结构化方案——实现了比泊松子采样更强的隐私放大，并且在噪声谱的两端（σ→0和σ→∞）均为最优。我们的分析揭示了隐私-噪声权衡的关键不在于最大化随机性，而在于消除参与方差同时保持各迭代间均匀的边际参与率。为将这种渐近理论转化为有限噪声保证，我们为BIS引入了一种实用的近精确蒙特卡洛会计方法，消除了现有RDP和基于组合的PLD分析中的分析松弛量。在超过60种实际DP-SDG配置下的评估显示，BIS在高实用性私密训练最相关的低噪声区域中一致优于泊松子采样，将所需噪声乘子降低了高达9.6%。这些结果推翻了常见的直觉——更多的采样随机性必然带来更强的隐私放大：在DP-SDG中，结构化参与既可以更实用也可以更私密。我们的实现可在https://github.com/dong-xin-ao-andy/bis-mc-accountant获取。

</details>


### [164] [ModelLens: Finding the Best for Your Task from Myriads of Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07075)
*Rui Cai,Weijie Jacky Mo,Xiaofei Wen,Qiyao Ma,Wenhui Zhu,Xiwen Chen,Muhao Chen,Zhe Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ModelLens是一个统一的模型推荐框架，通过从公开排行榜的噪声交互中学习模型-数据集-性能三元组的隐含能力图谱，实现了无需在目标数据集上运行候选模型即可为未见过的数据集推荐未见过模型的功能。在包含4.7万模型、9.6万数据集的162万条评估记录基准上，该方法超越了依赖元数据或需要在目标数据集上运行候选模型的各种基线方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 开源模型生态已包含数十万个预训练模型，但为新数据集挑选最佳模型日益困难：新模型和未基准测试的数据集不断涌现，对任意一方都没有先验记录。现有方法（AutoML、迁移性估计、模型路由）仅能处理这种"野外"场景的片段，要么需要预定义小规模候选池，要么需要在目标数据集上运行昂贵的逐模型前向传播。

Method: 核心思路是公开排行榜的交互记录（尽管分散且有噪声）共同勾勒出模型在不同评估设置下的隐含能力图谱。通过学习一个面向模型-数据集-度量三元组的性能感知潜在空间，ModelLens能够在无需在目标数据集上运行候选模型的情况下，对未见过数据集上的未见过模型进行排序。

Result: 在包含1.62M条评估记录（涵盖4.7万个模型和9.6万个数据集）的新基准上，ModelLens超越了依赖元数据或需要在目标数据集上运行候选模型的基线方法。其推荐的Top-K池在多个有代表性的路由方法上实现了最高81%的性能提升，覆盖多样化的QA基准。对最近发布的基准数据集的案例研究进一步验证了其在文本和视觉-语言任务上的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 论文提出的ModelLens框架成功利用公开排行榜的噪声交互信息构建了模型能力的隐含图谱，实现了高效的"野外"模型推荐，且实验证明该方法具有良好的泛化能力。

Original Abstract: The open-source model ecosystem now contains hundreds of thousands of pretrained models, yet picking the best model for a new dataset is increasingly infeasible: new models and unbenchmarked datasets emerge continuously, leaving practitioners with no prior records on either side. Existing approaches handle only fragments of this in-the-wild setting: AutoML and transferability estimation select models from small predefined pools or require expensive per-model forward passes on the target dataset, while model routing presupposes a given candidate pool. We introduce ModelLens, a unified framework for model recommendation in the wild. Our key insight is that public leaderboard interactions, though scattered and noisy, collectively trace out an implicit atlas of model capabilities across heterogeneous evaluation settings, a signal rich enough to learn from directly. By learning a performance-aware latent space over model--dataset--metric tuples, ModelLens ranks unseen models on unseen datasets without running candidates on the target dataset. On a new benchmark of 1.62M evaluation records spanning 47K models and 9.6K datasets, ModelLens surpasses baselines that either rely on metadata alone or require running each candidate on the target dataset. Its recommended Top-K pools further improve multiple representative routing methods by up to 81% across diverse QA benchmarks. Case studies on recently released benchmarks further confirm generalization to both text and vision-language tasks.

中文摘要翻译: 开源模型生态如今已包含数十万个预训练模型，然而为新数据集挑选最佳模型日益变得不可行：新模型和未基准测试的数据集不断涌现，使得从业者在任意一方都没有先验记录可循。现有方法仅能处理这种"野外"场景的片段：自动机器学习（AutoML）和迁移性估计方法要么从预定义的小候选池中选择模型，要么需要在目标数据集上执行昂贵的逐模型前向传播；而模型路由方法则预设了一个给定的候选池。我们提出了ModelLens，一个针对野外场景下模型推荐问题的统一框架。我们的关键洞察是：公开排行榜的交互记录尽管分散且带有噪声，但共同勾勒出了一幅跨异构评估设置的模型能力隐含图谱——这一信号足够丰富，可以直接从中学习。通过学习一个面向模型-数据集-度量三元组的性能感知潜在空间，ModelLens能够在不需在目标数据集上运行候选模型的情况下，对未见过数据集上的未见过模型进行排序。在一个包含162万条评估记录、涵盖4.7万个模型和9.6万个数据集的新基准上，ModelLens超越了那些仅依赖元数据或需要每个候选模型在目标数据集上运行的基线方法。其推荐的Top-K候选池在多个有代表性的路由方法上实现了最高达81%的性能提升，覆盖多样化的问答（QA）基准。对最近发布的基准数据集的案例研究进一步验证了该方法在文本和视觉-语言任务上的泛化能力。

</details>


### [165] [Test-Time Compositional Generalization in Diffusion Models via Concept Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07078)
*Zekun Wang,Anant Gupta,Tianyi Zhu,Christopher J. MacLellan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种测试时组合生成方法，利用预训练扩散模型在时间索引下的分数几何结构，从噪声边际中自动发现查询特定的密度模式概念，并通过产品-of-专家模型进行组合，无需预定义概念库。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的扩散模型组合生成方法通常假设相关的概念或条件信号已经预先存在，缺乏从模型自身学习到的表示中发现并组合新概念的能力。作者希望探究预训练扩散模型能否自主发现查询特定的概念并进行测试时组合。

Method: 1) 针对单个分布外查询，在多个噪声时间步上对分数函数 $s_θ(x_t,t)$ 进行梯度上升，恢复局部密度模式；2) 将这些模式映射到干净空间的高斯分布；3) 使用次模似然目标贪心地选择相关原型；4) 将选中的原型组合成产品-of-专家(PoE)教师模型，得到解析分数；5) 通过无分类器引导直接采样，或生成样本池用于训练新的类嵌入和低秩适配器。

Result: 在ColorMNIST和CelebA上构建的留出法组合基准测试中，解析PoE采样器和低秩适配模型均优于仅查询基线和最近训练类基线。

Conclusion: 扩散模型的时间索引分数几何中包含可复用的密度模式概念，支持测试时的组合生成，无需预定义的概念库。

Original Abstract: Compositional generalization requires models to produce novel configurations from familiar parts. In diffusion models, prior compositional generation methods typically assume that the relevant concepts or conditioning signals are already available. We instead ask whether a pretrained diffusion model can discover query-specific concepts from the time-indexed scores it learns for the noisy marginals $p_t(x_t)$ and compose them at test time. Given a single out-of-distribution query, our method performs gradient ascent on $s_θ(x_t,t) \approx \nabla_{x_t}\log p_t(x_t)$ at multiple noising timesteps to recover local density modes, maps these modes into clean-space Gaussians, greedily selects relevant prototypes with a submodular likelihood objective, and combines them into a product-of-experts (PoE) teacher model with an analytic score. This teacher model can be sampled directly through classifier-free guidance or used to generate a sample pool for training a new class embedding and low-rank adapter. On held-out composition benchmarks built from ColorMNIST and CelebA, both the analytic PoE sampler and the low-rank adapted model outperform query-only and nearest trained-class baselines. These results suggest that the time-indexed score geometry of the diffusion model contains reusable density-mode concepts that support test-time compositional generation without a predefined concept library.

中文摘要翻译: 组合泛化要求模型从熟悉的部分中产生新的配置。在扩散模型中，先前的组合生成方法通常假设相关概念或条件信号已经可用。相反，我们探究预训练扩散模型是否能够从它为噪声边际 $p_t(x_t)$ 学习的时间索引分数中自主发现查询特定的概念，并在测试时将它们组合起来。给定单个分布外查询，我们的方法在多个加噪时间步上对 $s_θ(x_t,t) \approx \nabla_{x_t}\log p_t(x_t)$ 执行梯度上升以恢复局部密度模式，将这些模式映射到干净空间的高斯分布，通过次模似然目标贪心地选择相关原型，并将其组合成具有解析分数的产品-of-专家(PoE)教师模型。该教师模型可以直接通过无分类器引导进行采样，或用于生成样本池以训练新的类嵌入和低秩适配器。在基于ColorMNIST和CelebA构建的留出法组合基准测试中，解析PoE采样器和低秩适配模型均优于仅查询基线和最近训练类基线。这些结果表明，扩散模型的时间索引分数几何中包含可复用的密度模式概念，支持无需预定义概念库的测试时组合生成。

</details>


### [166] [Actor-Critic with Active Importance Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07094)
*Majid Molaei,Gabor Paczolay,Matteo Papini,Alberto Maria Metelli,Marcello Restelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AISAC算法，通过优化行为策略最小化策略梯度方差，在保持无偏估计的同时提高样本效率和训练稳定性，在连续控制任务中取得显著改进。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 标准Actor-Critic方法在策略梯度估计中存在高方差问题，导致学习不稳定和样本效率低下。现有方法缺乏对行为策略的系统性优化来降低梯度方差。

Method: 基于重要性采样原理，AISAC通过优化行为策略以最小化梯度方差，同时保持梯度估计的无偏性。对于连续动作空间，采用高斯行为策略并通过交叉熵最小化进行优化。

Result: 理论分析证明了方差缩减和无偏性。在Inverted Pendulum和Half Cheetah任务上的实验表明，相比标准Actor-Critic方法，AISAC在学习速度、样本效率和训练稳定性方面均有提升。优化行为策略还改善了目标策略更新和critic估计精度。

Conclusion: AISAC通过行为策略优化加速收敛并稳定强化学习训练，具有实际应用潜力。未来工作将结合SAC和TD3等先进算法以应对更复杂环境。

Original Abstract: This paper introduces the Active-Importance-Sampling Actor-Critic (AISAC) algorithm, an extension of the Actor-Critic framework for reducing variance in policy gradient estimation. AISAC optimizes the behavior policy to minimize gradient variance while preserving unbiased gradient estimates. Using importance sampling principles, the algorithm adapts the behavior policy toward efficient data collection distributions aligned with target policy gradients.
  For continuous action spaces, AISAC employs Gaussian behavior policies optimized through cross-entropy minimization. We provide theoretical analysis demonstrating variance reduction and unbiasedness. Experiments on Inverted Pendulum and Half Cheetah tasks show improved learning speed, sample efficiency, and training stability compared to standard Actor-Critic methods.
  Results indicate that optimizing the behavior policy improves both target policy updates and critic estimation accuracy across different hyperparameter settings. AISAC accelerates convergence and stabilizes reinforcement learning training, making it promising for real-world applications. Future work includes integration with advanced algorithms such as Soft Actor-Critic and TD3 for more complex environments.

中文摘要翻译: 本文提出了主动重要性采样演员-评论家（AISAC）算法，这是对演员-评论家框架的扩展，旨在降低策略梯度估计中的方差。AISAC优化行为策略以最小化梯度方差，同时保持梯度估计的无偏性。基于重要性采样原理，该算法使行为策略自适应地朝向与目标策略梯度相一致的高效数据收集分布进行调整。对于连续动作空间，AISAC采用通过交叉熵最小化优化的高斯行为策略。我们提供了证明方差缩减和无偏性的理论分析。在Inverted Pendulum和Half Cheetah任务上的实验表明，与标准演员-评论家方法相比，AISAC在学习速度、样本效率和训练稳定性方面均有提升。结果表明，在不同超参数设置下，优化行为策略既改进了目标策略更新，也提高了评论家估计精度。AISAC加速了收敛并稳定了强化学习训练，使其在实际应用中具有前景。未来工作包括与Soft Actor-Critic和TD3等先进算法的集成，以应对更复杂的环境。

</details>


### [167] [Query-efficient model evaluation using cached responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07096)
*Hayden Helm,Ben Johnson,Carey Priebe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于数据核视角空间（DKPS）的方法，利用缓存的模型响应来预测新模型在基准测试上的性能，从而大幅减少评估所需的查询次数。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现代评估框架中，对新模型进行完整评估需要查询所有样本，成本过高。而已有模型的响应已被缓存，可以利用这些额外信息来减少评估新模型所需的查询数量。

Method: 基于数据核视角空间（DKPS）量化黑盒模型之间的关系，利用缓存的其他模型响应来预测新模型的基准性能；并提出了离线查询选择方法，通过最大化参考模型的拟合优度来提升预测精度。

Result: 理论证明了DKPS方法在特定条件下具有查询高效性；实验表明DKPS方法在显著降低查询预算的情况下，达到了与基线方法相同的平均绝对误差。

Conclusion: DKPS方法能够在保证预测精度的前提下大幅减少评估新模型所需的查询数量，且通过离线查询选择可进一步提升预测准确性。

Original Abstract: Evaluating a new model on an existing benchmark is often necessary to understand its behavior before deployment. For modern evaluation frameworks, generating and evaluating a response for all queries can be prohibitively expensive. In practice, responses from previously-evaluated models are often cached -- creating a potential opportunity to use this additional information to decrease the number of queries required to accurately evaluate a new model. In this paper, we introduce an approach for predicting benchmark performance that leverages cached model responses based on the Data Kernel Perspective Space (DKPS), a method for quantifying the relationship between models in the black-box setting. Theoretically, we show that DKPS-based methods are query-efficient under certain conditions. Empirically, we demonstrate that DKPS-based methods achieve the same mean absolute error as baselines with a substantially decreased query budget. We conclude by proposing an offline method for selecting a set of queries that maximizes the goodness-of-fit on reference models, improving prediction accuracy over random query selection.

中文摘要翻译: 在将新模型部署之前，通常需要在现有基准测试上对其进行评估以了解其行为。然而，对于现代评估框架而言，对所有查询生成并评估响应可能成本过高。实践中，先前已评估模型的响应通常会被缓存——这提供了一个潜在机会，即利用这些额外信息来减少准确评估新模型所需的查询数量。在本文中，我们提出了一种基于数据核视角空间（DKPS）的方法来预测基准性能，该方法能够在黑盒设置下量化模型之间的关系。理论上，我们证明了DKPS方法在特定条件下具有查询高效性。实验上，我们展示了DKPS方法在显著降低查询预算的情况下，达到了与基线方法相同的平均绝对误差。最后，我们提出了一种离线方法，通过最大化参考模型的拟合优度来选择一组查询，从而比随机查询选择获得更高的预测精度。

</details>


### [168] [CarCrashNet: A Large-Scale Dataset and Hierarchical Neural Solver for Data-Driven Structural Crash Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07098)
*Mohamed Elrefaie,Dule Shu,Matthew Klenk,Faez Ahmed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了CarCrashNet，一个面向数据驱动结构碰撞仿真的公开高保真基准数据集，包含超过14,000个保险杠横梁碰撞仿真和825个整车碰撞仿真，并设计了CrashSolver模型用于整车碰撞预测，验证了其作为可重复研究基础的有效性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 碰撞仿真是现代车辆开发的核心，但由于非线性接触、大变形、材料塑性和失效等复杂力学行为，结构碰撞力学建模极具挑战。现有数据集大多规模有限、不公开或缺乏全车级别仿真，亟需一个公开、高保真、多模态的基准来推动数据驱动的碰撞仿真研究。

Method: 1) 构建CarCrashNet基准数据集：包含14,000+保险杠横梁碰撞仿真（变几何/材料/边界条件）和825个整车碰撞仿真（基于Dodge Neon、Toyota Yaris、Chevrolet Silverado三种车型）。2) 基于OpenRadioss开源有限元工作流，并利用实验碰撞数据和商业求解器Ansys LS-DYNA进行验证。3) 提出CrashSolver机器学习模型用于高分辨率有限元数据的整车碰撞预测。4) 与现有几何深度学习和Transformer神经求解器进行基准对比。

Result: OpenRadioss开源工作流在实验数据和LS-DYNA对比中得到了验证；CrashSolver在全车碰撞预测任务上优于现有的几何深度学习和Transformer基线方法；CarCrashNet定位为结构仿真、耐撞性建模和AI驱动虚拟碰撞测试的可重复研究基础。

Conclusion: CarCrashNet作为公开高保真基准数据集，结合CrashSolver模型，为数据驱动的结构碰撞仿真提供了可靠的研究平台，推动了碰撞仿真领域的可重复研究和AI驱动的虚拟测试。数据集已开源。

Original Abstract: Crash simulation is a cornerstone of modern vehicle development because it reduces the need for costly physical prototypes, accelerates safety-driven design iteration, and increasingly supports virtual testing workflows. At the same time, modeling structural crash mechanics remains exceptionally challenging: the response is governed by nonlinear contact, large deformation, material plasticity, failure, and complex multi-body interactions evolving over space and time on high-resolution finite-element meshes. In this work, we introduce \textsc{CarCrashNet}, a public high-fidelity open-source benchmark for data-driven structural crash simulation. \textsc{CarCrashNet} combines component-scale and full-vehicle simulations in a multi-modal format, including more than 14{,}000 bumper-beam pole-impact simulations with varying geometry, materials, and boundary conditions, together with 825 full-vehicle crash simulations built from three industry-standard vehicle models of increasing structural complexity: Dodge Neon, Toyota Yaris, and Chevrolet Silverado. To establish the reliability of the benchmark, we validate our open-source finite-element workflow based on OpenRadioss against both experimental crash data and the commercial solver Ansys LS-DYNA. We also introduce \textsc{CrashSolver}, a machine-learning model designed for full-vehicle crash prediction from high-resolution finite-element crash data. We further perform extensive benchmarking across the released datasets and evaluate \textsc{CrashSolver} against state-of-the-art geometric deep learning and transformer-based neural solvers. Our results position \textsc{CarCrashNet} as a foundation for reproducible research in structural simulation, crashworthiness modeling, and AI-driven virtual crash testing. The dataset is available at https://github.com/Mohamedelrefaie/CarCrashNet.

中文摘要翻译: 碰撞仿真是现代车辆开发的基石，因为它减少了昂贵的物理样机需求，加速了以安全为导向的设计迭代，并日益支持虚拟测试工作流。与此同时，结构碰撞力学建模仍然极具挑战性：其响应受非线性接触、大变形、材料塑性、失效以及在高分辨率有限元网格上随空间和时间演化的复杂多体相互作用的控制。本文提出了CarCrashNet，一个面向数据驱动结构碰撞仿真的公开高保真开源基准。CarCrashNet以多模态格式整合了部件级和整车级仿真，包含超过14,000个变几何、变材料和变边界条件的保险杠横梁碰撞仿真，以及基于三种工业级标准车型（Dodge Neon、Toyota Yaris和Chevrolet Silverado，结构复杂度递增）构建的825个整车碰撞仿真。为了验证基准的可靠性，我们将基于OpenRadioss的开源有限元工作流与实验碰撞数据及商业求解器Ansys LS-DYNA进行对比验证。我们还提出了CrashSolver，一个专为高分辨率有限元碰撞数据的整车碰撞预测设计的机器学习模型。进一步地，我们对发布的数据集进行了广泛的基准测试，并将CrashSolver与最先进的几何深度学习和基于Transformer的神经求解器进行了评估。实验结果表明，CarCrashNet可作为结构仿真、耐撞性建模和AI驱动虚拟碰撞测试领域可重复研究的基石。数据集已开源，地址为https://github.com/Mohamedelrefaie/CarCrashNet。

</details>


### [169] [Almost Sure Convergence Rates of Stochastic Approximation and Reinforcement Learning via a Poisson-Moreau Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07104)
*Xinyu Liu,Zixuan Xie,Shangtong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对马尔可夫噪声下随机逼近和强化学习的几乎必然收敛率问题，提出了一种基于泊松方程校正和Moreau-envelope平滑的新型Lyapunov漂移构造方法，针对收缩更新类算法获得了接近最优的收敛速率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在马尔可夫噪声下建立随机逼近和强化学习的几乎必然收敛率是一个基础性理论难题，现有方法难以获得紧的收敛速率。

Method: 提出一种新颖的Lyapunov漂移构造方法，将基于泊松方程的马尔可夫噪声校正技术与针对收缩映射的Moreau-envelope平滑技术相结合。

Result: 对于幂律学习率$O(n^{-η})$（$η\in (1/2, 1)$），获得任意接近$o(n^{1-2η})$的几乎必然收敛率；对于调和学习率$O(n^{-1})$，获得任意接近$o(n^{-1})$的几乎必然收敛率，该结果接近由迭代对数律给出的最优速率$O(n^{-1}\log\log n)$（针对i.i.d.噪声特例）。

Conclusion: 本文提出的方法能够为马尔可夫噪声下具有收缩更新的随机逼近算法建立近乎最优的几乎必然收敛率，是解决该基础理论难题的重要进展。

Original Abstract: Establishing almost sure convergence rates for stochastic approximation and reinforcement learning under Markovian noise is a fundamental theoretical challenge. We make progress towards this challenge for a class of stochastic approximation algorithms whose expected updates are contractive, a setting that arises in many reinforcement learning algorithms such as $Q$-learning and linear temporal difference learning. Specifically, for a power-law learning rate $O(n^{-η})$ with $η\in (1/2, 1)$, we obtain an almost sure convergence rate arbitrarily close to $o(n^{1 - 2η})$. For a harmonic learning rate $O(n^{-1})$, we obtain an almost sure convergence rate arbitrarily close to $o(n^{-1})$, which we argue is a strong result because it is close to the optimal rate $O(n^{-1}\log\log n)$ given by the law of the iterated logarithm (for a special case of i.i.d. noise). Key to our analysis is a novel Lyapunov drift construction that applies a Poisson-equation based correction for Markovian noise to the well-established Moreau-envelope smoothing for the contractive mapping.

中文摘要翻译: 在马尔可夫噪声下建立随机逼近和强化学习的几乎必然收敛率是一个基础性理论难题。本文针对一类期望更新为收缩性的随机逼近算法（该类设置出现在许多强化学习算法中，例如$Q$-学习和线性时序差分学习）在该问题上取得了进展。具体而言，对于幂律学习率$O(n^{-η})$（$η\in (1/2, 1)$），我们获得了任意接近$o(n^{1-2η})$的几乎必然收敛率。对于调和学习率$O(n^{-1})$，我们获得了任意接近$o(n^{-1})$的几乎必然收敛率，我们认为这是一个强结果，因为它接近由迭代对数律给出的最优速率$O(n^{-1}\log\log n)$（针对独立同分布噪声的特例）。我们分析的关键是一种新颖的Lyapunov漂移构造方法，该方法将基于泊松方程的马尔可夫噪声校正技术应用于稳健的Moreau-envelope平滑，以处理收缩映射。

</details>


### [170] [Where to Spend Rollouts: Hit-Utility Optimal Rollout Allocation for Group-Based RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07114)
*Tao Wang,Shuo Li,Yan Sun,Dongsheng Ding,Edgar Dobriban*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HORA，一种无学习的自适应 rollout 分配策略，用于替代GRPO中固定次数的采样方式，通过最大化后验命中效用（hit utility）来提升计算效率，在数学推理任务中保持Pass@1的同时提升Pass@K。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于组的策略优化方法（如GRPO）为每个提示分配固定数量的rollout，导致资源浪费：对于采样组已饱和的提示过度分配计算资源，而对需要更多探索的提示分配不足。因此需要一种自适应的rollout分配策略来优化计算效率。

Method: 提出命中效用（hit utility），即对某个提示额外分配一组rollout后至少有一个正确rollout的后验概率。基于此，提出HORA（Hit-Utility Optimal Rollout Allocation），一种无学习的rollout分配策略，在每个分配批次内最大化总后验命中效用，自适应地重新分配rollout预算，但不改变下游奖励评估和基于组的优势估计器。

Result: 在四个数学推理基准和三种模型规模上，HORA在12个模型-基准配置中有10个保持相当的Pass@1并提升了Pass@K，1个持平，1个饱和例外。HORA还与其他基于组的估计器（如RLOO）兼容。消融研究表明HORA使用的均匀先验与五种基于提示的条件学习先验具有竞争力。

Conclusion: HORA作为一种轻量级、无学习的自适应rollout分配方法，能有效提升基于组策略优化的计算效率，在保持主要性能指标的同时改善多样性指标，且易于集成到现有框架中。

Original Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has emerged as a central paradigm for improving the reasoning capabilities of large language models. Group-based policy optimization methods, such as GRPO, typically allocate a fixed number of rollouts to every prompt. This uniform allocation can be inefficient: it over-allocates compute to prompts whose sampled groups are already saturated while under-exploring prompts for which additional samples may reveal useful correct trajectories. To address this limitation, we introduce hit utility, the posterior probability that at least one rollout in a proposed additional allocation for a prompt will be correct. Building on this notion, we propose Hit-Utility Optimal Rollout Allocation (HORA), a learning-free rollout allocation policy that maximizes total posterior hit utility within each allocation batch. HORA adaptively reallocates rollout budgets while leaving the downstream reward evaluation and group-based advantage estimator unchanged. Across four mathematical reasoning benchmarks and three model scales, HORA preserves comparable Pass@1 and improves Pass@K over compute-matched GRPO in ten of twelve model--benchmark configurations, with one tie and one saturated exception. It is also drop-in compatible with other group-based estimators such as RLOO. Ablation studies indicate that the uniform prior used by HORA is competitive with five prompt-conditioned learned-prior alternatives.

中文摘要翻译: 基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）已成为提升大语言模型推理能力的核心范式。基于组的策略优化方法（如GRPO）通常为每个提示分配固定数量的rollout。这种均匀分配可能效率低下：它对采样组已饱和的提示过度分配计算资源，而对那些额外采样可能揭示有用正确轨迹的提示探索不足。为解决这一局限，我们引入了命中效用（hit utility），即对某个提示进行额外分配后至少有一个rollout正确的后验概率。基于这一概念，我们提出了命中效用最优rollout分配（HORA），一种无需学习的rollout分配策略，在每个分配批次内最大化总后验命中效用。HORA自适应地重新分配rollout预算，同时保持下游奖励评估和基于组的优势估计器不变。在四个数学推理基准和三种模型规模上，HORA在12个模型-基准配置中有10个保持了相当的Pass@1并提升了Pass@K，其中1个配置持平，1个为饱和例外。它还可与RLOO等其他基于组的估计器即插即用。消融研究表明，HORA使用的均匀先验与五种基于提示的条件学习先验相比具有竞争力。

</details>


### [171] [Conformal-Style Quantile Analyses for Stochastic Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07115)
*Chengyu Du,Mengfan Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ACP-UCB1算法，利用自适应保形推断估计上尾端点并融合UCB乐观奖励，解决在尾部性能而非均值指标下的随机赌博机问题，实现了对数级上分位遗憾。 

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 经典赌博机算法以均值奖励为准则，但许多实际场景更关注上尾表现（高回报端）。均值与上尾端点对臂的排名可能不一致，导致传统算法与目标错配。 

Method: 提出ACP-UCB1策略，结合自适应保形估计（adaptive conformal estimate）估计上尾端点，并引入UCB型乐观奖励。技术关键包括：通过奖励分位数集中性、重计算得分分位数的扰动论证、以及自适应水平的确定性局部化来控制端点估计。 

Result: ACP-UCB1实现了对数级上分位遗憾，每臂贡献为O(log n / Δ_j^{ACP})；提供了与UCB1对比的指标特定遗憾分解；数值实验验证了性能与改进。 

Conclusion: ACP-UCB1能够有效处理以上尾端点为目标的分位数赌博机问题，理论保证与实验均表明其优于传统均值准则方法。 

Original Abstract: Stochastic bandit algorithms are usually analyzed under a mean-reward criterion, yet many problems favor arms with strong upper-tail performance, which we study herein. For a fixed miscoverage level \(α\), the natural upper-tail target of arm \(j\) is the upper endpoint \(F_j^{-1}(1-α/2)\) of a central prediction interval. This target can rank arms differently from their means, creating a central mismatch with the classical bandit objective. To this end, we propose ACP-UCB1, a conformal-style policy that combines an adaptive conformal estimate of the upper endpoint with a UCB-type optimism bonus. The technical challenge is that the conformity scores used by ACP-UCB1 are recomputed from evolving empirical quantile estimates and evaluated at an adaptive level. We control this endpoint through reward-quantile concentration, a perturbation argument for recomputed score quantiles, and deterministic localization of the adaptive level. ACP-UCB1 achieves logarithmic upper-quantile regret with per-arm contribution \(O(\nicefrac{\log n}{Δ_j^{\mathrm{ACP}}})\). We also provide metric-specific regret decompositions comparing ACP-UCB1 with UCB1 and use numerical experiments to validate performance and improvement.

中文摘要翻译: 随机赌博机算法通常以均值奖励为准则进行分析，然而许多问题更偏好具有强上尾表现的臂，本文对此进行研究。对于固定的误覆盖水平α，臂j的自然上尾目标是中心预测区间的上端点F_j^{-1}(1-α/2)。该目标对臂的排名可能与均值不同，从而与经典赌博机目标产生中心错配。为此，我们提出ACP-UCB1，一种保形风格策略，它将上端点的自适应保形估计与UCB型乐观奖励相结合。技术挑战在于ACP-UCB1使用的保形得分是从演化的经验分位数估计中重新计算的，并在自适应水平上评估。我们通过奖励分位数集中性、重计算得分分位数的扰动论证以及自适应水平的确定性局部化来控制该端点。ACP-UCB1实现了对数级上分位遗憾，每臂贡献为O(log n / Δ_j^{ACP})。我们还提供了与UCB1对比的指标特定遗憾分解，并通过数值实验验证了性能与改进。 

</details>


### [172] [When Symbol Names Should Not Matter: A Logistic Theory of Fresh-Symbol Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07120)
*Wenjie Guan,Jelena Bradic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在Transformer核逻辑回归框架下，研究了固定标签模板分类任务中模型能否学习符号重命名不变的决策规则，揭示了训练数据中的偶然token重叠（碰撞）会扰乱理想模板级分类器，并提出了基于碰撞图的边距传递保证。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 探究Transformer是否基于抽象符号而非具体token名称进行推理，特别是在训练和测试使用不相交词表时，模型能否学到符号重命名不变的决策规则。

Method: 在Transformer核逻辑回归框架下，通过正则化核逻辑分类分析学习到的预测器，将其分解为理想模板级分类器与有限样本扰动项，并用带色碰撞图编码token重叠结构，推导出新鲜符号分类的高概率边距传递保证。

Result: 词表大小控制碰撞的平均发生率，但碰撞的几何结构决定理想分类边距是否得以保持；该扰动框架可推广到抽象增强输入，得到通用的边距-碰撞准则用于判断何种提示策略能改善新鲜符号泛化。

Conclusion: 模板任务中Transformer的符号泛化能力受训练数据中token碰撞的几何结构而非仅碰撞率影响，正则化、样本量和Transformer核结构共同决定了边距传递的可靠性。

Original Abstract: Template tasks have emerged as a clean testbed for asking whether transformers reason with abstract symbols rather than concrete token names. We study the fixed-label classification version of this problem, where train and test examples share latent templates but may use disjoint vocabularies. Unlike next-token prediction, the model need not emit unseen symbols; it must learn a decision rule invariant to symbol renaming. We analyze regularized kernel logistic classification in the transformer-kernel regime. Our main result decomposes the learned predictor into an ideal template-level classifier and a finite-sample perturbation caused by accidental token overlaps in the training data. We encode these overlaps by a colored collision graph and prove high-probability margin-transfer guarantees for fresh-symbol classification. This perspective extends template-based analyses to logistic classification and refines scalar diversity conditions: vocabulary size controls the average rate of collisions, but collision geometry controls whether the ideal classification margin is preserved. More broadly, the same perturbation framework applies to abstraction-augmented inputs, yielding a general margin-versus-collision criterion for identifying when prompting strategies improve fresh-symbol generalization. Synthetic template experiments illustrate the predicted roles of regularization, sample size, and transformer-kernel structure.

中文摘要翻译: 模板任务已成为一个简洁的测试平台，用于探究Transformer是否基于抽象符号而非具体token名称进行推理。我们研究了该问题的固定标签分类版本，其中训练样本和测试样本共享潜在模板，但可能使用不相交的词表。与下一token预测不同，模型无需生成未见过的符号；它必须学习一个对符号重命名保持不变的决策规则。我们在Transformer核机制下分析了正则化核逻辑分类。我们的主要结果将学习到的预测器分解为理想的模板级分类器与由训练数据中偶然token重叠引起的有限样本扰动。我们通过带色碰撞图编码这些重叠，并证明了新鲜符号分类的高概率边距传递保证。这一视角将基于模板的分析扩展到了逻辑分类，并细化了标量多样性条件：词表大小控制碰撞的平均发生率，但碰撞的几何结构决定理想分类边距是否得以保持。更广泛地说，相同的扰动框架适用于抽象增强输入，从而得出一个通用的边距-碰撞准则，用于判断何种提示策略能改善新鲜符号泛化。合成模板实验展示了正则化、样本量和Transformer核结构所起到的预期作用。

</details>


### [173] [Convergence and Emergence of In-Context Reinforcement Learning with Chain of Thought](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07123)
*Zixuan Xie,Xinyu Liu,Rohan Chandra,Shangtong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次从理论上分析了链式思维（CoT）生成如何与上下文强化学习（ICRL）交互，证明在策略评估场景中CoT过程等价于反复执行时序差分学习更新，且策略评估误差随CoT长度几何级下降并最终饱和。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 尽管经验研究表明CoT能增强ICRL能力，但缺乏理论层面的理解，尤其是CoT与ICRL之间的交互机制尚不明确。

Method: 在策略评估设定下，使用线性Transformer进行分析，证明特定Transformer参数下CoT生成过程等价于重复执行时序差分（TD）学习更新；并提供有限样本收敛分析，证明预训练损失下该参数为全局极小值点。

Result: 1) CoT生成过程等价于反复执行TD学习更新；2) 策略评估误差随CoT长度呈几何级下降，最终在由上下文长度决定的统计下限处饱和；3) 所需的Transformer参数是预训练损失的全局极小值点。

Conclusion: 从理论上揭示了CoT增强ICRL的机制，为相关经验现象提供了理论基础。

Original Abstract: In-context reinforcement learning (ICRL) refers to the ability of RL agents to adapt to new tasks at inference time without parameter updates by conditioning on additional context. Recent empirical studies further demonstrate that Chain-of-Thought (CoT) generation can amplify this ICRL capability. This paper is the first to provide a theoretical understanding on how CoT interacts with ICRL. We conduct our analysis in a policy evaluation setup with linear Transformer. We prove that with specific Transformer parameters, the CoT generation process is equivalent to repeatedly executing temporal difference learning updates. Additionally, we provide finite sample convergence analysis showing that the policy evaluation error decreases geometrically with CoT length and eventually saturates at a statistical floor determined by the context length. We also prove that the desired Transformer parameters are a global minimizer of the pretraining loss, providing a theoretical understanding on the empirical emergence of those parameters.

中文摘要翻译: 上下文强化学习（ICRL）指强化学习智能体在推理时无需更新参数，仅通过附加上下文即可适应新任务的能力。近期经验研究进一步表明，链式思维（CoT）生成能够增强这种ICRL能力。本文首次从理论上理解CoT如何与ICRL交互。我们在线性Transformer的策略评估设定下进行分析。我们证明，在特定Transformer参数下，CoT生成过程等价于反复执行时序差分学习更新。此外，我们提供了有限样本收敛分析，表明策略评估误差随CoT长度几何级下降，并最终在由上下文长度决定的统计下限处饱和。我们还证明了所需的Transformer参数是预训练损失的全局极小值点，为这些参数在经验中的涌现提供了理论理解。

</details>


### [174] [The Position Curse: LLMs Struggle to Locate the Last Few Items in a List](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07127)
*Zhanqi Zhang,Hua-Dong Xiong,Robert C. Wilson,Mikio Aoi,Marcelo G. Mattar,Li Ji-An*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了大型语言模型在基于位置的信息检索中存在的“位置诅咒”现象：模型在前向检索（从位置到内容）上表现尚可，但反向检索（从内容到位置）严重滞后；通过LoRA微调可部分改善，但绝对性能仍远未饱和。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 尽管LLM能在海量无关文本中精准定位单个事实（"大海捞针"），却无法正确检索短列表中的最后几个项目。这种看似矛盾的失败表明模型存在根本性的位置理解缺陷，尤其是在代码编辑等需要精确定位的场景中，该问题尤为突出。

Method: 设计了两种互补的查询任务：(1) 给定序列中的位置（前向/后向偏移量），检索对应的项目；(2) 给定项目，返回其位置。位置通过相对于锚点（列表端点或列表中的其他项目）的前向或后向偏移来指定。同时构建了位置聚焦训练数据集PosBench，并使用LoRA微调进行能力恢复实验。

Result: (1) 在开源和闭源前沿模型中，反向检索性能显著落后于前向检索；(2) LoRA微调能同时改善前向和反向检索能力，并能泛化到保留的代码理解基准测试PyIndex上；(3) 但绝对性能仍远未达到饱和水平。

Conclusion: 基于位置的检索是LLM的一项关键但尚未被充分解决的能力，尤其对于需要精确定位和编辑的大型代码库场景。该能力应成为未来预训练目标和模型设计的重要关注方向。

Original Abstract: Modern large language models (LLMs) can find a needle in a haystack (locating a single relevant fact buried among hundreds of thousands of irrelevant tokens) with near-saturated accuracy, yet fail to retrieve the last few items in a short list. We call this failure the Position Curse. For instance, even in a two-line code snippet, Claude Opus 4.6 misidentifies the second-to-last line most of the time. To characterize this failure, we evaluated two complementary queries: given a position in a sequence (of letters or words), retrieve the corresponding item; and given an item, return its position. Each position is specified as a forward or backward offset from an anchor, either an endpoint of the list (its start or end) or another item in the list. Across both open-source and frontier closed-source models, backward retrieval substantially lags forward retrieval. To test whether this capability can be rescued by post-training, we constructed PosBench, a position-focused training dataset. LoRA fine-tuning improves both forward and backward retrieval and generalizes to a held-out code-understanding benchmark (PyIndex), yet absolute performance remains far from saturated. As LLM coding agents increasingly operate over large codebases where precise indexing becomes essential for code understanding and editing, position-based retrieval emerges as a key capability for future pretraining objectives and model design.

中文摘要翻译: 现代大型语言模型（LLM）可以在"大海捞针"（在数十万个无关词元中定位单个相关事实）时达到近乎饱和的准确率，却无法正确检索短列表中的最后几个项目。我们将这种失败称为"位置诅咒"。例如，即使在仅有两行代码的片段中，Claude Opus 4.6 在大部分情况下也会错误识别倒数第二行。为了刻画这种失败，我们评估了两种互补的查询任务：给定序列（字母或单词序列）中的位置，检索对应的项目；以及给定项目，返回其位置。每个位置均以相对于锚点的前向或后向偏移量来指定，锚点可以是列表的端点（起点或终点），也可以是列表中的另一个项目。在开源和前沿闭源模型中，反向检索的性能均显著落后于前向检索。为了测试这种能力能否通过后训练得到恢复，我们构建了PosBench，一个专注于位置任务的训练数据集。LoRA微调能够同时改善前向和反向检索能力，并能泛化到保留的代码理解基准测试（PyIndex）上，但其绝对性能仍远未达到饱和。随着LLM编程智能体越来越多地运行于大型代码库之上——在这些场景中，精确定位对于代码理解和编辑至关重要——基于位置的检索正成为未来预训练目标和模型设计的一项关键能力。

</details>


### [175] [Simple KNN-Based Outlier Detection Achieves Robust Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07130)
*Tianle Jiang,Yufa Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明，在最优簇大小的实际假设下，简单地移除具有较大K近邻距离的点即可实现鲁棒k均值聚类的常数因子近似保证，且实验表明该方法在聚类代价和运行时间上优于或匹敌更复杂的算法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 尽管鲁棒k均值与离群点检测密切相关，但经典离群点检测启发式方法在鲁棒k均值中的有效性缺乏理论和实证理解。

Method: 在最优簇大小的实际假设下，通过移除具有较大K近邻距离的点，将鲁棒k均值问题规约为标准k均值问题，实现常数因子近似保证。

Result: 理论结果表明该方法实现了从鲁棒k均值到标准k均值的常数因子约简，且无需引入额外中心或丢弃多余离群点；实验表明在真实数据集上，该方法在聚类代价和运行时间上优于或匹敌更复杂的算法。

Conclusion: 基于KNN的简单启发式方法在鲁棒聚类中具有惊人有效性，为桥接离群点检测与聚类技术提供了新机遇。

Original Abstract: Being robust to the presence of outliers is crucial for applying clustering algorithms in practice. In the $\textit{robust $k$-Means}$ problem (i.e., $k$-Means with outliers), the goal is to remove $z$ outliers and minimize the $k$-Means cost on the remaining points. Despite the close connection between robust $k$-Means and outlier detection, both theoretical and empirical understanding of the effectiveness of $\textit{classic outlier detection heuristics}$ for robust $k$-Means remains limited. In this paper, we prove that under a practical assumption on the optimal cluster sizes, simply removing points with large $K$-Nearest-Neighbor distances achieves performance comparable to prior work in terms of approximation guarantees: it yields a constant-factor reduction from robust $k$-Means to standard $k$-Means, without introducing additional centers or discarding extra outliers, as is commonly required by existing approaches. Empirically, experiments on real-world datasets show that our method outperforms or matches several more sophisticated algorithms in terms of clustering cost and runtime. These results demonstrate that simple KNN-based heuristics can be surprisingly effective for robust clustering, highlighting new opportunities to bridge techniques from outlier detection and clustering.

中文摘要翻译: 在实际应用中，聚类算法对离群点的鲁棒性至关重要。在鲁棒k均值问题（即带离群点的k均值）中，目标是移除z个离群点，并在剩余点上最小化k均值代价。尽管鲁棒k均值与离群点检测密切相关，但经典离群点检测启发式方法在鲁棒k均值中的有效性在理论和实证层面的理解仍然有限。在本文中，我们证明在关于最优簇大小的实际假设下，简单地移除具有较大K近邻距离的点即可在近似保证上达到与先前工作相当的性能：它实现了从鲁棒k均值到标准k均值的常数因子约简，而无需像现有方法那样引入额外中心或丢弃多余离群点。在实验方面，在真实数据集上的实验表明，我们的方法在聚类代价和运行时间上优于或匹敌几种更复杂的算法。这些结果表明，基于KNN的简单启发式方法在鲁棒聚类中具有惊人的有效性，突显了桥接离群点检测与聚类技术的新机遇。

</details>


### [176] [GAD in the Wild: Benchmarking Graph Anomaly Detection under Realistic Deployment Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07133)
*Jingjing Zhou,Shiyu Huang,Qing Qing,Zuquan Yuan,Huafei Huang,Ziqi Xu,Mingliang Hou,Xikun Zhang,Renqiang Luo,Ivan Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个面向图异常检测（GAD）的多维度基准，系统评估了模型在百万级图、极端异常稀疏和缺失节点属性等现实部署挑战下的表现，揭示了现有GNN方法在大规模图上存在内存瓶颈、低异常率下性能崩溃以及重构模型对属性填充策略敏感等问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的GAD基准大多局限于小规模、精心标注且异常比例相对均衡的数据集，导致学术评估与真实部署之间存在巨大差距。为了弥合这一差距，需要在更贴近实际部署场景的条件下系统评估GAD模型。

Method: 从5个不同图数据（包含两个原生工业级数据集，节点数超过370万）中派生出系列受控基准变体，涵盖百万级规模、极端异常稀缺和缺失节点属性三个现实挑战维度，并对9种代表性GAD模型进行系统评估。

Result: (1) 大多数基于GNN的方法由于内存需求过高无法扩展到百万节点图；(2) 在真实异常率（如0.1%）下检测性能急剧下降，常导致零召回；(3) 基于重构的模型对属性填充策略高度敏感。实验表明，实验室环境下的强性能并不能保证生产环境中的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 强实验室性能不代表生产环境鲁棒性，现有GAD模型在大规模、不完美图上存在显著局限性，本文发布的基准可作为诊断测试床，推动鲁棒且可扩展的GAD系统发展。

Original Abstract: Graph Anomaly Detection (GAD) is a critical task in graph machine learning with vital applications in financial fraud detection and social platform governance. However, existing GAD benchmarks are often restricted to small-scale, curated graphs with relatively balanced anomaly ratios, leaving a substantial gap between academic evaluation and real-world deployment. To bridge this gap, we present a multi-dimensional benchmark that systematically evaluates GAD models under three deployment-relevant challenges: million-scale graphs, extreme anomaly scarcity, and missing node attributes. We derive a family of controlled benchmark variants from five diverse graphs, including two native industrial-scale datasets with over 3.7 million nodes. Our extensive evaluation of nine representative GAD models reveals three major limitations: (1) most GNN-based methods fail to scale to million-node graphs due to prohibitive memory requirements; (2) detection performance drops sharply under realistic anomaly ratios (e.g., 0.1\%), often resulting in zero recall; and (3) reconstruction-based models are highly sensitive to attribute imputation strategies. Our findings suggest that strong performance in laboratory settings does not guarantee robustness in production environments. We release this benchmark and empirical evaluation as a diagnostic testbed to promote the development of robust and scalable GAD systems for large-scale, imperfect graphs encountered in practice. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/Benchmark_GAD-E7A3.

中文摘要翻译: 图异常检测（GAD）是图机器学习中的一项关键任务，在金融欺诈检测和社交平台治理中有着重要应用。然而，现有的GAD基准通常局限于小规模、精心标注且异常比例相对均衡的图，导致学术评估与实际部署之间存在显著差距。为弥合这一差距，我们提出了一个多维度基准，在三个部署相关的现实挑战下系统评估GAD模型：百万级图、极端异常稀缺以及缺失节点属性。我们从五个不同的图中派生出系列受控基准变体，其中包括两个原生工业级数据集，节点数超过370万。我们对9种代表性GAD模型的广泛评估揭示了三大主要局限性：(1) 大多数基于GNN的方法由于内存需求过高无法扩展到百万节点图；(2) 在真实异常率（例如0.1%）下检测性能急剧下降，通常导致零召回；(3) 基于重构的模型对属性填充策略高度敏感。我们的研究结果表明，实验室环境下的强性能并不能保证生产环境中的鲁棒性。我们将此基准和实证评估作为诊断测试床发布，以推动面向大规模、不完美实际图的鲁棒且可扩展的GAD系统的发展。代码可在https://anonymous.4open.science/r/Benchmark_GAD-E7A3获取。

</details>


### [177] [Adaptive Negative Reinforcement for LLM Reasoning:Dynamically Balancing Correction and Diversity in RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07137)
*Yash Ingle,Jaival Chauhan,Ankit Yadav,Sudhakar Mishra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对RLVR框架中负样本强化（NSR）方法使用固定惩罚权重的局限性，提出了两种扩展方法：自适应负样本强化（A-NSR）和置信度加权负样本强化（CW-NSR），在数学推理数据集上取得了良好效果。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有NSR技术在训练过程中使用固定的惩罚权重，对所有错误回答一视同仁，这限制了模型的学习效率和泛化能力。

Method: 提出两种扩展方法：1）A-NSR：使用时间依赖调度函数，训练初期侧重纠错稳定模型，后期转向更精细的控制更新；2）CW-NSR：基于模型归一化序列似然度分配惩罚权重，对高置信度错误路径施以更大惩罚，对不确定的探索性错误惩罚较轻。

Result: 在MATH、AIME 2025和AMC23等困难推理数据集上，使用Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B架构进行评估，验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 理论分析表明，这些机制能够控制token级更新，使模型利用先验引导的概率重分布，同时提供对过拟合的自然防御。

Original Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has become a highly effective method for improving the reasoning abilities of Large Language Models (LLMs). Recent research shows that Negative Sample Reinforcement (NSR) -- which focuses on penalizing incorrect steps rather than simply rewarding correct ones -- can match or even exceed the performance of more complex frameworks like PPO and GRPO across the entire Pass@k spectrum. However, current NSR techniques usually apply a fixed penalty throughout the training process and treat every incorrect response with the same weight.
  To address these limitations, we propose two extensions to the NSR framework: Adaptive Negative Sample Reinforcement. Rather than using a fixed update rule, A-NSR uses time-dependent scheduling functions. In the initial training phases, the system focuses heavily on correcting errors to stabilize the model. As training continues, it shifts toward more subtle and controlled updates. We also introduce Confidence-Weighted Negative Reinforcement, which operates on the principle that different mistakes carry different levels of importance. CW-NSR assigns specific penalty weights based on the model's normalized sequence likelihood. If the model is highly confident in a wrong path, it receives a larger penalty and for uncertain errors -- where the model is effectively exploring -- are penalized less strictly. Our formal analysis shows how these mechanisms govern token-level updates, allowing the model to leverage prior-guided probability redistribution while providing a natural defense against overfitting. We evaluated these methods on difficult reasoning datasets, including MATH, AIME 2025, and AMC23, using the Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B architecture.

中文摘要翻译: 基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）已成为提升大型语言模型（LLMs）推理能力的高效方法。最新研究表明，负样本强化（Negative Sample Reinforcement, NSR）——即侧重于惩罚错误步骤而非简单地奖励正确步骤——在整个Pass@k谱系中可以匹配甚至超越PPO和GRPO等更复杂框架的性能。然而，当前的NSR技术通常在训练过程中应用固定的惩罚，并对每个错误回答赋予相同的权重。为解决这些局限性，我们提出了负样本强化框架的两种扩展方法：自适应负样本强化（Adaptive Negative Sample Reinforcement, A-NSR）。不同于使用固定更新规则，A-NSR采用时间依赖的调度函数。在初始训练阶段，系统着重于纠正错误以稳定模型；随着训练的进行，它逐渐转向更精细和受控的更新。我们还引入了置信度加权负样本强化（Confidence-Weighted Negative Reinforcement, CW-NSR），其核心原则是不同类型的错误具有不同的重要性。CW-NSR基于模型归一化序列似然度分配特定的惩罚权重：若模型对错误路径高度自信，则受到更大的惩罚；而对于不确定的错误——即模型实际上在进行探索的情况——则惩罚较轻。我们的形式化分析展示了这些机制如何控制token级更新，使模型能够利用先验引导的概率重分布，同时提供对过拟合的自然防御。我们使用Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B架构，在包括MATH、AIME 2025和AMC23在内的困难推理数据集上对这些方法进行了评估。

</details>


### [178] [Regret-Oracle Complexity Tradeoffs in Agnostic Online Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07155)
*Idan Attias,Steve Hanneke,Arvind Ramaswami*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对不可知在线学习中的oracle复杂度问题，提出了一种自适应的不可知到可实现约简方法，利用弱一致性oracle和VC维边界动态剪枝，将oracle查询复杂度从双指数级降至多项式级$O(T^{d_\mathrm{VC}+1})$，同时保持近最优的期望遗憾，并首次形式化刻画了遗憾与oracle复杂度之间的权衡。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 经典的不可知在线学习通过Littlestone标准最优算法(SOA)约简到可实现场景，但SOA计算上不可行。现有的oracle高效方法虽然实现了近最优期望遗憾，但面临双指数级的oracle复杂度$O(T^{2^{O(d_\mathrm{LD})}})$。本文旨在显著降低这一oracle复杂度，同时依赖更弱的原始操作。

Method: 提出一种自适应且动态的不可知到可实现的约简方法，利用弱一致性oracle（仅判断标记数据集是否可实现）动态剪枝不可实现的标签序列。通过VC维($d_\mathrm{VC}$)边界约束动态维护的活跃路径数量，将查询复杂度降至$O(T^{d_\mathrm{VC}+1})$。同时形式化刻画了遗憾与oracle复杂度之间的权衡，给出了上界和下界。

Result: 1) oracle查询复杂度从$O(T^{2^{O(d_\mathrm{LD})}})$降至$O(T^{d_\mathrm{VC}+1})$；2) 完美保持近最优的期望遗憾；3) 动态剪枝同时减少了内存开销；4) 给出了遗憾与oracle复杂度之间的权衡上界；5) 证明了受限查询预算$Q = o(\sqrt{T})$的学习器必然遭受$\Omega(T/Q)$的期望遗憾。

Conclusion: 本文通过自适应的动态剪枝策略，在更弱的弱一致性oracle假设下，将不可知在线学习的oracle复杂度从双指数级大幅降至多项式级，同时保持了近最优的遗憾性能，并系统刻画了遗憾与计算开销之间的基本权衡。

Original Abstract: Agnostic online learning is classically solved via a reduction to the realizable setting, utilizing Littlestone's Standard Optimal Algorithm (SOA) as a base learner. However, the SOA is computationally intractable to execute even for a single round. To overcome this barrier, recent work in oracle-efficient online learning replaces the SOA with a realizable base learner that accesses the concept class exclusively through an offline empirical risk minimization (ERM) oracle. While such agnostic learners achieve near-optimal expected regret, they suffer from a doubly-exponential oracle complexity of $O\big(T^{2^{O(d_\mathrm{LD})}}\big)$, where $d_\mathrm{LD}$ is the Littlestone dimension and $T$ is the number of rounds. In this work, we significantly improve this oracle complexity while relying on an even weaker primitive: a weak-consistency oracle, which merely decides whether a given labeled dataset is realizable. At the core of our approach is an adaptive and dynamic agnostic-to-realizable reduction that actively prunes non-realizable label sequences on the fly. By using the VC dimension ($d_\mathrm{VC}$) to bound the number of dynamically maintained active paths, our algorithm reduces the total query complexity down to $O(T^{d_\mathrm{VC}+1})$ while perfectly preserving near-optimal expected regret. Crucially, this dynamic pruning also yields a memory reduction over the standard reduction. Furthermore, we formally quantify the regret--oracle complexity tradeoff, providing upper bounds that smoothly interpolate between restricted query budgets and attainable expected regret. We complement these with lower bounds proving that any learner restricted to $Q = o(\sqrt{T})$ queries must suffer an expected regret of $Ω(T/Q)$.

中文摘要翻译: 经典的不可知在线学习通过约简到可实现场景来解决，利用Littlestone标准最优算法(SOA)作为基学习器。然而，即使仅执行单轮，SOA在计算上也是不可行的。为了克服这一障碍，近期oracle高效的在线学习工作用可实现的基学习器替代SOA，该基学习器仅通过离线经验风险最小化(ERM)oracle访问概念类。尽管此类不可知学习器实现了近最优的期望遗憾，但它们遭受着双指数级的oracle复杂度$O(T^{2^{O(d_\mathrm{LD})}})$，其中$d_\mathrm{LD}$是Littlestone维度，$T$是轮数。本文在依赖更弱原始操作（即弱一致性oracle，仅判断给定标记数据集是否可实现）的条件下，显著改进了这一oracle复杂度。我们方法的核心是一种自适应且动态的不可知到可实现的约简方法，能够主动地动态剪枝不可实现的标签序列。通过使用VC维($d_\mathrm{VC}$)约束动态维护的活跃路径数量，我们的算法将总查询复杂度降至$O(T^{d_\mathrm{VC}+1})$，同时完美保持近最优的期望遗憾。关键的是，这种动态剪枝还带来了相对于标准约简方法的内存开销减少。此外，我们正式量化了遗憾与oracle复杂度之间的权衡，提供了在受限查询预算与可达到的期望遗憾之间平滑插值的上界。我们进一步用下界进行补充，证明了任何受限为$Q = o(\sqrt{T})$次查询的学习器必须承受$\Omega(T/Q)$的期望遗憾。

</details>


### [179] [Cost-Ordered Feasibility for Multi-Armed Bandits with Cost Subsidy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07171)
*Ishank Juneja,Carlee Joe-Wong,Osman Yağan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究带成本补贴的多臂老虎机问题（MAB-CS），即最小化成本同时满足相对于未知最优奖励的质量约束。提出了COF算法，通过智能组合所有臂的样本评估廉价臂的可行性，并给出了实例相关的理论下界与上界，在MovieLens和Goodreads数据集及合成实例上验证了优越性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统MAB追求最大化累积奖励，但实际应用中常需在满足最低奖励约束的前提下最小化成本。现有方法未充分考虑奖励约束相对于未知最优奖励的情形，且缺乏实例相关的理论下界分析。

Method: 提出Cost-Ordered Feasibility (COF)算法，通过智能组合所有臂的采样信息来评估廉价臂的可行性，并利用成本排序指导决策。

Result: 推导了实例相关的期望次优样本下界，严格推广了先前结果；建立了COF在期望累积成本和质量遗憾上的实例相关上界；在MovieLens、Goodreads数据集及合成实例上，COF相比基线方法展现出更优的经验性能。

Conclusion: 本文在理论上开发了定性更优的遗憾上界，COF算法在实证上也表现出明显改进，证实了所提方法的有效性。

Original Abstract: The classic multi-armed bandit (MAB) problem tackles the challenge of accruing maximum reward while making decisions under uncertainty. However, in applications, often the goal is to minimize cost subject to a constraint on the minimum permissible reward, an objective captured by multi-armed bandits with cost-subsidy (MAB-CS). Of interest to this paper is the setting where the quality (reward) constraint is specified relative to the unknown best reward and the cost of each arm is known. We characterize the expected sub-optimal samples required by any policy by proving instance-dependent lower bounds that offer new insight into the problem and are a strict generalization of prior bounds. Then, we propose an algorithm called Cost-Ordered Feasibility (COF) that leverages our insight and intelligently combine samples from all arms to gauge the feasibility of a cheap arm. Thereafter, we analyze COF to establish instance-dependent upper bounds on its expected cumulative cost and quality regret, i.e., relative to the cheapest feasible arm. Finally, we empirically validate the merits of COF, comparing it to baselines from the literature through extensive simulation experiments on the MovieLens and Goodreads datasets as well as representative synthetic instances. Not only does our paper develop qualitatively better theoretical regret upper bounds, but COF also convincingly demonstrates improved empirical performance.

中文摘要翻译: 经典的多臂老虎机（MAB）问题处理在不确定性下做决策的同时获取最大累积奖励的挑战。然而在实际应用中，目标往往是在满足最低允许奖励约束的前提下最小化成本，这一目标由带成本补贴的多臂老虎机（MAB-CS）所刻画。本文关注的是奖励（质量）约束相对于未知最优奖励进行指定且每个臂的成本已知的设置。我们通过证明与实例相关的下界来刻画任何策略所需的期望次优样本数，这些下界为该问题提供了新的见解，并且是对先前下界的严格推广。然后，我们提出一种称为成本排序可行性（COF）的算法，该算法利用我们的见解，智能地组合来自所有臂的样本以评估廉价臂的可行性。随后，我们分析COF，建立其在期望累积成本和质量遗憾（相对于最便宜的可行臂）上的实例相关上界。最后，通过在MovieLens和Goodreads数据集以及代表性合成实例上进行广泛的仿真实验，将COF与文献中的基线方法进行比较，实证验证了其优点。本文不仅理论上开发了定性更优的遗憾上界，而且COF在经验性能上也表现出令人信服的改进。

</details>


### [180] [HyperEyes: Dual-Grained Efficiency-Aware Reinforcement Learning for Parallel Multimodal Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07177)
*Guankai Li,Jiabin Chen,Yi Xu,Xichen Zhang,Yuan Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HyperEyes是一个并行多模态搜索智能体，通过视觉定位与检索的原子化融合、两阶段训练（数据合成+双粒度效率感知强化学习），在提升精度的同时大幅减少工具调用轮次，并提出了兼顾效率的新基准IMEB。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有顺序搜索智能体每处理一个目标实体就调用一次工具，导致交互轮次冗余、推理效率低下，需要让智能体在同一轮次中并行发起多个查询以"搜得更广而非更长"。

Method: 两阶段训练：(1) 冷启动阶段，通过并行适配数据合成流水线生成多实体和多约束查询数据，并用渐进式拒绝采样筛选效率优先的轨迹；(2) 双粒度效率感知强化学习框架——宏观层面提出TRACE（工具使用参考自适应成本效率）奖励，在训练中单调收紧参考值以抑制多余工具调用；微观层面采用在策略蒸馏，将外部教师模型的密集token级纠正信号注入失败轨迹中，解决稀疏结果奖励的信用分配问题。

Result: 在6个基准上，HyperEyes-30B相比最强的开源同类智能体，准确率提升9.9%，平均工具调用轮次减少5.3倍。

Conclusion: 并行搜索策略显著优于顺序搜索，双粒度效率感知强化学习能有效平衡精度与推理成本，且效率应与准确性一同作为搜索智能体的核心评估维度。

Original Abstract: Existing multimodal search agents process target entities sequentially, issuing one tool call per entity and accumulating redundant interaction rounds whenever a query decomposes into independent sub-retrievals. We argue that effective multimodal agents should search wider rather than longer: dispatching multiple grounded queries concurrently within a round. To this end, we present HyperEyes, a parallel multimodal search agent that fuses visual grounding and retrieval into a single atomic action, enabling concurrent search across multiple entities while treating inference efficiency as a first-class training objective. HyperEyes is trained in two stages.
