Motivation: 现有OTA-FL系统在现实信道条件下，聚合可靠性和用户参与度受限于不利的信道衰落。尽管FA技术能通过位置域可重构性利用空间分集，但缺乏对其在OTA-FL中性能的严格理论分析。现有研究多依赖优化方法或数值评估，缺少可解析的性能刻画框架。

Method: 构建了一个上行OTA-FL框架，包含一个固定天线接入点和多个FA用户，工作于空间相关衰落信道。采用基于copula的方法（Clayton copula）对FA端口间的空间信道相关性进行建模，以捕捉与最差衰落条件相关的下尾依赖性。推导了聚合错误中断概率和每轮期望参与用户数的闭式表达式。

Result: 数值结果验证了所推导的闭式表达式的准确性，并表明FA辅助的OTA-FL在聚合可靠性和用户参与效率方面显著优于传统固定天线方案，同时在实用系统条件下提供了有价值的见解。

Conclusion: FA技术能够有效提升OTA-FL系统的性能，通过动态天线位置选择实现更有利的信道条件，从而提高用户成功参与聚合的概率。所提出的解析框架为FA在分布式学习系统中的应用提供了理论基础。

Original Abstract: Fluid antenna (FA) technology has recently emerged as an effective means of exploiting spatial diversity through position-domain reconfigurability. This paper investigates the integration of FA into over-the-air federated learning (OTA-FL) systems with the aim of improving aggregation reliability and user participation under realistic channel conditions. By dynamically selecting antenna positions, FA-equipped users can exploit additional spatial degrees of freedom to realize more favorable channel conditions, thereby increasing the probability of successful contribution to the OTA aggregation process in each communication round. We consider an uplink OTA-FL framework consisting of a single fixed-antenna access point and multiple FA-enabled users operating over spatially correlated fading channels. Unlike existing studies that primarily rely on optimization-based designs or numerical evaluations, we develop a tractable analytical framework that enables a rigorous performance characterization of FA-assisted OTA-FL. In particular, closed-form expressions are derived for the aggregation error outage probability and the expected number of participating users per round. Spatial channel correlation across FA ports is modeled using a copula-based approach, where the Clayton copula is adopted to capture lower-tail dependence relevant to worst-case fading conditions. Numerical results validate the analytical findings and demonstrate that FA-assisted OTA-FL significantly outperforms conventional fixed-antenna schemes in terms of aggregation reliability and participation efficiency, while providing insights under practical system considerations.

中文摘要翻译: 流体天线（FA）技术近期作为一种通过位置域可重构性来利用空间分集的有效手段而崭露头角。本文研究了将FA集成到空中联邦学习（OTA-FL）系统中，旨在现实信道条件下提升聚合可靠性和用户参与度。通过动态选择天线位置，配备FA的用户能够利用额外的空间自由度来实现更有利的信道条件，从而在每个通信轮次中提高成功贡献于OTA聚合过程的概率。我们考虑一个上行OTA-FL框架，该框架由一个固定天线接入点和多个工作在空间相关衰落信道上的FA用户组成。与现有主要依赖基于优化的设计或数值评估的研究不同，我们开发了一个易于处理的解析框架，能够对FA辅助的OTA-FL进行严格的性能刻画。具体而言，推导了聚合错误中断概率和每轮期望参与用户数的闭式表达式。采用基于copula的方法对FA端口间的空间信道相关性进行建模，其中Clayton copula被用于捕捉与最差衰落条件相关的下尾依赖性。数值结果验证了分析结论，并表明FA辅助的OTA-FL在聚合可靠性和参与效率方面显著优于传统固定天线方案，同时在实用系统考量下提供了深刻见解。

</details>


### [212] [Homomorphic Directional Beamforming with Analog True Time Delay Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06930)
*Ibrahim Pehlivan,Danijela Cabric*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于同态性的分裂波束图生成算法（HDB），用于真时延（TTD）阵列的低成本频率依赖波束赋形，解决了现有方法计算复杂、存储需求大或存在波束斜视退化的问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有TTD阵列参数设置方法存在实际局限性：要么计算复杂的优化过程，要么需要极大的存储查找表，要么因波束斜视效应导致性能退化。因此需要一种低内存、低计算成本的实用解决方案。

Method: 首先严格分析了波束图合成过程，揭示了TTD阵列配置矩阵与对应波束图之间的同态性和数学结构；然后提出同态定向波束赋形（HDB）算法，利用生成器波束图字典来近似期望的分裂波束图，该字典规模比现有方法低数个数量级且不忽略波束斜视。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提算法能够以低内存和低计算成本实现实用部署，且HDB设计在不同子带的用户间提供接近均匀的波束赋形增益，实现功率分配的公平性。

Conclusion: HDB算法通过利用TTD阵列的同态结构，实现了低复杂度、低存储需求且不牺牲波束斜视建模精度的分裂波束图生成，为实际部署提供了可行方案。

Original Abstract: Recently, true-time-delay (TTD) arrays, also referred to as joint phase-time arrays (JPTA), have been investigated for low-cost frequency-dependent beamforming capabilities to enable various applications, including beam-squint correction, fast beam training, and serving multiple user equipment (UE)s by frequency band to direction mapping, termed as split beampatterns. Several heuristics and optimization-based solutions have been proposed to determine TTD array parameters settings. However, they have practical limitations due to either computationally demanding optimization procedures, requirements for extremely large memory look-up tables, or degradations due to the beam-squint effect. In this article, we propose a novel split-beampattern generation algorithm based on the observed homomorphism between TTD array configuration matrices and corresponding beampatterns. First, we rigorously analyze the beampattern synthesis process and demonstrate the observed homomorphism and mathematical structure. Then, we propose the Homomorphic Directional Beamforming (HDB) algorithm to approximate the desired split beampatterns by utilizing a generator beampattern dictionary that requires a dictionary size orders of magnitude lower than existing approaches without ignoring the beam squint. With extensive simulations, we show that the proposed algorithm can provide a practical implementation with low memory and low computational cost requirements. In addition, HDB design provides close to uniform beamforming gains among UEs in different subbands, enabling fairness in power allocation.

中文摘要翻译: 近年来，真时延（TTD）阵列，也称为联合相位-时延阵列（JPTA），因其低成本的频率依赖波束赋形能力而被广泛研究，可实现波束斜视校正、快速波束训练以及通过频带-方向映射服务多个用户设备（UE）（称为分裂波束图）等多种应用。已有多种启发式和基于优化的方法用于确定TTD阵列参数设置。然而，这些方法存在实际局限性，原因包括计算复杂的优化过程、需要极大的存储查找表，或因波束斜视效应导致的性能退化。在本文中，我们基于观察到的TTD阵列配置矩阵与对应波束图之间的同态性，提出了一种新颖的分裂波束图生成算法。首先，我们严格分析了波束图合成过程，并论证了所观察到的同态性及数学结构。然后，我们提出同态定向波束赋形（HDB）算法，通过利用生成器波束图字典来近似期望的分裂波束图，该字典规模比现有方法低数个数量级，且不忽略波束斜视。通过大量仿真，我们证明所提算法能以低内存和低计算成本实现实用部署。此外，HDB设计在不同子带的用户间提供接近均匀的波束赋形增益，从而实现功率分配的公平性。

</details>


### [213] [Omnidirectional Transponder for Narrow-band Radar Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07205)
*Oren Cohen,Moshe Vana*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新型频率转换应答器架构，采用紧凑单天线全向设计，克服了传统SAR定标参考目标体积大、视角受限的问题。原型机经无人机载SAR实验验证，在合成孔径内提供高精度脉冲间相位测量，适用于环视SAR和双基SAR等全向定标需求。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统SAR定标参考目标（角反射器、标准应答器）体积大且视角有限，无法满足Circular SAR和双基SAR等应用对全向参考目标的需求。

Method: 提出一种频率转换应答器架构，采用单天线紧凑全向设计，通过频率转换实现信号转发，虽牺牲了工作带宽（主要用于方位向定标），但能在合成孔径内提供高精度脉冲间相位测量。

Result: 原型机经无人机载SAR实验验证，结果表明该设计在全向参考目标场景中具有显著潜力。

Conclusion: 该频率转换应答器架构有效解决了传统定标目标体积大、视角受限的难题，在Circular SAR和双基SAR等需要全向参考目标的应用中展现出重要价值。

Original Abstract: Conventional reference targets for Synthetic Aperture Radar (SAR) calibration, such as corner reflectors and standard transponders, are often inherently large and suffer from limited viewing angles. This paper presents a novel frequency-translating transponder architecture that circumvents these limitations, enabling a truly compact, single-antenna design capable of omnidirectional operation. While the operational bandwidth is consequently narrowed, restricting its use primarily to azimuth-direction calibrations, the design excels at providing highly accurate pulse-to-pulse phase measurements across the synthetic aperture. The transponder was prototyped and experimentally validated with a drone-mounted SAR. The results demonstrate the transponder's significant potential for applications requiring omnidirectional reference targets, such as Circular SAR (CSAR) and bistatic SAR.

中文摘要翻译: 用于合成孔径雷达（SAR）定标的传统参考目标，如角反射器和标准应答器，通常体积较大且视角有限。本文提出一种新型频率转换应答器架构，克服了这些局限性，实现了真正紧凑的单天线全向设计。虽然该设计因此牺牲了工作带宽，主要适用于方位向定标，但它能在合成孔径内提供高精度的脉冲间相位测量。该应答器已通过原型机制造和无人机载SAR实验验证。结果表明，该应答器在需要全向参考目标的应用中，如环视SAR（CSAR）和双基SAR，具有显著潜力。

</details>


### [214] [Resource-Element Energy Difference for Noncoherent Over-the-Air Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07263)
*Hao Chen,Zavareh Bozorgasl*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出REED非相干聚合方法，通过正交资源元素的能量差实现无瞬时CSI的空中联邦学习，在理论保证下达到标准收敛速率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统空中联邦学习依赖瞬时信道状态信息和相干相位对齐，在实际无线系统中难以维持，需要一种不依赖瞬时CSI的非相干聚合方案。

Method: 将实值更新的正负部分分别映射到两个正交资源元素的发射能量上，引入独立相位抖动，服务器通过能量差估计带符号聚合值；仅需慢时间尺度的平均信道功率校准。

Result: 无偏估计且存在精确闭式方差；在平滑非凸目标下证明扰动与局部步长成二次关系，达到标准(1/√T)收敛率；IID设置下接近清洁FedAvg，非IID下性能适度下降。

Conclusion: REED在避免瞬时CSI的同时实现了理论有保证的聚合，为实际OTA-FL部署提供了可行的非相干替代方案。

Original Abstract: Over-the-air federated learning (OTA-FL) reduces uplink latency by exploiting waveform superposition, but conventional analog aggregation schemes typically require instantaneous channel state information (CSI), channel inversion, and coherent phase alignment, which can be difficult to maintain in practical wireless systems. This paper proposes resource-element energy difference (REED), a noncoherent aggregation primitive for continuous signed updates that avoids instantaneous CSI. REED maps the positive and negative parts of each real-valued update to transmit energies on two orthogonal resource elements with independent phase dithers, and the server estimates the signed aggregate from their energy difference. With only slow-timescale calibration of average channel powers, REED is unbiased for the desired signed sum and admits an exact closed-form variance under Rayleigh fading. We incorporate REED into full-participation FedAvg and prove a smooth nonconvex stationarity bound. Under an average per-client energy budget, the aggregation gain can be scheduled so that the REED-induced perturbation scales quadratically with the local stepsize, yielding the canonical (1/sqrt(T)) stationarity rate. Experiments on MNIST and Fashion-MNIST demonstrate that REED closely matches clean FedAvg and coherent CSIT aggregation in IID settings, while maintaining stable convergence with a moderate performance degradation under strong data heterogeneity.

中文摘要翻译: 空中联邦学习（OTA-FL）通过利用波形叠加降低了上行链路延迟，但传统的模拟聚合方案通常需要瞬时信道状态信息（CSI）、信道反转和相干相位对齐，这些在实际无线系统中难以维持。本文提出资源元素能量差（REED），一种用于连续有符号更新的非相干聚合原语，避免了瞬时CSI。REED将每个实值更新的正负部分映射到两个正交资源元素的发射能量上，并引入独立相位抖动，服务器通过能量差估计有符号聚合值。仅需慢时间尺度的平均信道功率校准，REED对所需的有符号求和是无偏的，并且在瑞利衰落下具有精确的闭式方差。我们将REED融入全参与FedAvg，并证明了平滑非凸的驻点界。在平均每客户端能量预算下，聚合增益可被调度，使得REED引起的扰动与局部步长呈二次关系，从而获得标准的(1/√T)驻点速率。在MNIST和Fashion-MNIST上的实验表明，在IID设置下REED与清洁FedAvg和相干CSIT聚合紧密匹配，同时在强数据异质性下保持稳定收敛，性能适度下降。

</details>


### [215] [A Microfabricated PCM-Switched Reconfigurable Intelligent Surface for Wideband Millimeter-Wave Beam Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07311)
*Afsaneh Hojjati-Firoozabadi,Raafat Mansour*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出并实验验证了一种基于电驱动二氧化钒（VO2）开关的可重构智能表面（RIS），用于毫米波波束 steering。该RIS采用多层微加工工艺在氧化铝衬底上制造，在深亚波长单元内集成了数百个亚4微米的VO2开关，实现了33 GHz中心频率下18%分数带宽内10-20 dB的远场增益增强和高达60°的波束偏转。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于半导体的可重构表面在更高频率下受限于封装寄生效应和占用面积，亟需一种可扩展的、能减轻这些瓶颈的RIS架构。

Method: 采用多层微加工工艺在氧化铝衬底上制作RIS，利用电驱动的VO2薄膜相变开关实现表面阻抗调制；通过全波仿真分析开关诱导的阻抗变化，并使用定制WR-28波导测量装置实验表征单元相位和幅度响应；在10×20阵列中集成200个VO2开关，通过列内串联偏置实现可编程空间相位分布控制。

Result: 在33 GHz中心频率、18%分数带宽内实现10-20 dB的远场增益增强，波束 steering角度可达60°，实测辐射方向图与半数值信道模型预测吻合良好。

Conclusion: 通过将薄膜相变材料开关与集成感知单元设计相结合，本文展示了一种可扩展的RIS架构，有效缓解了传统半导体方案中的封装寄生和占用面积问题，为更高频率的可重构表面提供了实用路径。

Original Abstract: This paper presents the design, fabrication, and experimental validation of a reconfigurable intelligent surface (RIS) employing electrically actuated vanadium dioxide (VO2) switches for millimeter wave beam steering. The proposed RIS is realized using a multilayer microfabrication process on an alumina substrate, enabling monolithic integration of hundreds of sub-4 micrometer VO2 switching elements within deeply subwavelength unit cells, approximately one-fifth of the wavelength. The switching-induced modulation of surface impedance is analyzed through full-wave simulations, and the resulting phase and amplitude responses are experimentally characterized using a custom WR-28 waveguide measurement setup. Based on the validated unit-cell design, a 10 x 20 RIS array integrating 200 VO2 switches is fabricated. The switches within each column are serially biased using integrated routing lines, allowing programmable control of the spatial phase distribution across the surface. Synthesized phase profiles enable dynamic beam steering, resulting in measured far-field gain enhancement of 10-20 dB over an 18 percent fractional bandwidth centered at 33 GHz, with steering angles up to 60 degrees. The measured radiation patterns are in good agreement with semi-numerical channel modeling predictions. By combining thin-film PCM switching with an integration-aware unit-cell design, this work demonstrates a scalable RIS architecture that mitigates packaging parasitics and footprint limitations inherent to conventional semiconductor-based implementations, providing a practical pathway toward higher-frequency reconfigurable surfaces.

中文摘要翻译: 本文介绍了一种采用电驱动二氧化钒（VO2）开关的可重构智能表面（RIS）的设计、制造与实验验证，用于毫米波波束转向。该RIS在氧化铝衬底上通过多层微加工工艺实现，能够在深亚波长单元（约为波长的五分之一）内单片集成数百个亚4微米的VO2开关单元。通过全波仿真分析了开关引起的表面阻抗调制，并利用定制WR-28波导测量装置对所得相位和幅度响应进行了实验表征。基于已验证的单元设计，制造了一个集成200个VO2开关的10×20 RIS阵列。每列内的开关通过集成布线进行串联偏置，从而实现对表面空间相位分布的可编程控制。合成的相位分布实现了动态波束转向，在33 GHz中心频率、18%分数带宽内测得远场增益增强10-20 dB，转向角度可达60°。实测辐射方向图与半数值信道模型预测结果吻合良好。通过将薄膜相变材料（PCM）开关与集成感知单元设计相结合，本文展示了一种可扩展的RIS架构，有效缓解了传统半导体实现方案中固有的封装寄生和占用面积限制，为更高频段的可重构表面提供了一条实用路径。

</details>


### [216] [Over-the-Air Beamforming Design for Full-Duplex Cell-Free Massive MIMO Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07368)
*Bikshapathi Gouda,Antti Tölli*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对全双工无小区大规模MIMO系统中的干扰问题，提出了一种基于迭代上下行导频信号的分布式波束赋形设计方法，通过导频域投影和最佳响应更新机制，有效抑制了自干扰、AP间耦合和UE间交叉链路干扰，提升了系统收敛速度和有效和速率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 全双工无小区大规模MIMO系统中，虽然同时上下行传输能提升频谱效率，但引入了残余自干扰、AP间耦合干扰和UE间交叉链路干扰，现有方案未能有效解决这些干扰的联合优化问题。

Method: 提出一种全分布式波束赋形设计方法：基于迭代上下行导频信号，在联合上下行和均方误差准则下进行优化；引入导频域投影抑制UE间导频泄露，实现AP处交叉项的准确重构；在交替优化框架中采用UE端最佳响应更新以改善强UE间干扰下的收敛性。

Result: 数值结果表明，相比分离的上下行分布式OTA波束赋形训练方案及仅基于本地CSI的设计，本文方法收敛更快、有效和速率更高，尤其对强干扰UE的提升更为显著。

Conclusion: 本文提出的全分布式波束赋形方法有效解决了全双工无小区MIMO系统中的多种干扰问题，具有更优的收敛性能和系统吞吐量，为全双工无小区系统的实际部署提供了可行的分布式方案。

Original Abstract: We study a full-duplex (FD) cell-free massive MIMO system where distributed access points (APs) operate in FD mode while user equipments (UEs) remain half-duplex. Although simultaneous uplink (UL) and downlink (DL) transmissions improve spectral efficiency, they introduce residual self-interference, AP-to-AP coupling, and UE-to-UE cross-link interference. Building on prior over-the-air distributed beamforming frameworks, we develop a fully distributed beamforming design based on iterative UL and DL pilot signaling under a joint UL and DL sum mean-square error criterion that explicitly accounts for these interference components. In FD operation, simultaneous UL and DL pilot transmissions cause UE-to-UE pilot leakage, which contaminates the reconstruction of the cross terms required for AP-specific beamforming design. To mitigate this effect, we introduce a pilot-domain projection of the received signals at the UEs, which suppresses the interference component and enables accurate cross-term reconstruction at the APs. In addition, best-response updates at the UEs are employed within the alternating optimization framework to improve convergence under strong UE-to-UE interference. Numerical results demonstrate faster convergence and higher effective sum rate, with particularly significant gains for strongly interfering UEs, compared with both separate UL and DL distributed OTA beamforming training schemes and designs based solely on local channel state information.

中文摘要翻译: 本文研究了一种全双工无小区大规模MIMO系统，其中分布式接入点以全双工模式运行，而用户设备保持半双工。尽管同时上下行传输提升了频谱效率，但引入了残余自干扰、接入点间耦合干扰以及用户设备间交叉链路干扰。基于已有的空中分布式波束赋形框架，我们提出了一种全分布式波束赋形设计方法，该方法基于迭代的上下行导频信号，并在联合上下行和均方误差准则下明确考虑了上述干扰分量。在全双工运行中，同时的上下行导频传输会导致用户设备间的导频泄露，从而污染接入点特定波束赋形设计所需交叉项的重建。为缓解这一问题，我们引入了一种对用户设备接收信号进行导频域投影的方法，该方法抑制了干扰分量，使得接入点能够准确重建交叉项。此外，在交替优化框架中，我们在用户设备端采用了最佳响应更新，以改善强用户设备间干扰下的收敛性。数值结果表明，与分离的上下行分布式空中波束赋形训练方案以及仅基于本地信道状态信息的设计相比，本文方法具有更快的收敛速度和更高的有效和速率，尤其对于强干扰用户设备而言增益尤为显著。

</details>


### [217] [PropSplat: Map-Free RF Field Reconstruction via 3D Gaussian Propagation Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08035)
*William Bjorndahl,Maninder Pal Singh,Farhad Nouri,Joseph Camp*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: PropSplat提出了一种无需地图的无线电传播模型构建方法，利用3D各向异性高斯原语重建射频场，仅依赖稀疏的RF测量数据即可实现高精度传播预测，性能优于现有神经辐射场方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统的站点特定传播模型构建依赖射线追踪（需详细3D地图）或密集测量采集，这两种方式成本高昂且难以快速部署，尤其在地理数据不可用或过时的情况下。因此需要一种无需地图、仅依赖稀疏RF测量数据的高效传播建模方法。

Method: PropSplat使用3D各向异性高斯原语表示射频场，每个高斯编码相对于显式基线路径损耗模型的标量路径损耗偏移量，并带有可学习的路径损耗指数。高斯原语沿观测到的发射器-接收器路径初始化，通过端到端优化学习传播环境，无需外部信息如楼层平面图、地形数据库或杂波数据。

Result: 在室外大规模路测数据集上（六个sub-6 GHz频率，测量间隔300米），PropSplat的RMSE为5.38 dB，优于WRF-GS+ (5.87 dB)、GSRF (7.46 dB)和NeRF² (14.76 dB)。在室内蓝牙低功耗测量中，PropSplat的平均定位误差为0.19m，比NeRF² (1.84m)提升一个数量级，同时RSS预测精度相近。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，仅从稀疏的RF原生测量数据即可实现高精度的站点特定传播重建，减少了对地理数据作为可扩展RF环境建模前提条件的需求。

Original Abstract: Building a site-specific propagation model typically requires either ray-tracing over detailed 3D maps or dense measurement campaigns. Both approaches are expensive and often infeasible for rapid deployments where geographic data is unavailable or outdated. We present PropSplat, a map-free propagation modeling method that reconstructs radio frequency (RF) fields using 3D anisotropic Gaussian primitives. Each Gaussian encodes a scalar path loss offset relative to an explicit baseline path loss model with a learnable path loss exponent. Gaussians are initialized along observed transmitter--receiver paths and optimized end-to-end to learn the propagation environment without external information like floor plans, terrain databases, or clutter data. We evaluate PropSplat against wireless radiance field methods NeRF$^2$, GSRF, and WRF-GS+ on two real-world datasets. On large-scale outdoor drive-tests spanning multiple topographical regions at six sub-6 GHz frequencies, PropSplat achieves 5.38 dB RMSE when training measurements are spaced 300m apart and outperforms WRF-GS+ (5.87 dB), GSRF (7.46 dB), and NeRF$^2$ (14.76 dB). On indoor Bluetooth Low Energy measurements, PropSplat achieves 0.19m mean localization error, an order of magnitude better than NeRF$^2$ (1.84m), while achieving near-identical received signal strength prediction accuracy. These results show that accurate site-specific propagation reconstruction is achievable from sparse RF-native measurements. The need for geographic data as a prerequisite for scalable RF environment modeling is reduced.

中文摘要翻译: 构建站点特定的传播模型通常需要基于详细3D地图的射线追踪或密集的测量采集。这两种方法成本高昂，且在地理数据不可用或过时的情况下往往难以快速部署。我们提出了PropSpat，一种无地图传播建模方法，利用3D各向异性高斯原语重构射频场。每个高斯原语编码相对于显式基线路径损耗模型的标量路径损耗偏移量，并具有可学习的路径损耗指数。高斯原语沿观测到的发射器-接收器路径初始化，并通过端到端优化学习传播环境，无需楼层平面图、地形数据库或杂波数据等外部信息。我们在两个真实世界数据集上评估了PropSpat与无线辐射场方法NeRF²、GSRF和WRF-GS+的性能。在涵盖多个地形区域、六个sub-6 GHz频率的大规模室外路测中，当训练测量间隔为300米时，PropSpat的RMSE为5.38 dB，优于WRF-GS+（5.87 dB）、GSRF（7.46 dB）和NeRF²（14.76 dB）。在室内蓝牙低功耗测量中，PropSpat的平均定位误差达到0.19米，比NeRF²（1.84米）提升一个数量级，同时实现了几乎相同的接收信号强度预测精度。这些结果表明，仅从稀疏的RF原生测量数据即可实现高精度的站点特定传播重建，减少了对地理数据作为可扩展RF环境建模前提条件的需求。

</details>


### [218] [Joint Beamforming and Antenna Placement Optimization in Pinching Antenna Systems with User Mobility: A Deep Reinforcement Learning Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08039)
*Ali Amhaz,Mohamed Elhattab,Chadi Assi,Sanaa Sharafeddine*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究了支持移动用户的夹捏天线系统（PASS），通过联合优化波束赋形和夹捏点位置来最大化用户平均和速率，并采用深度确定性策略梯度（DDPG）方法解决由于用户移动性和随机阻塞带来的复杂非凸优化问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有PASS研究忽略了用户移动性，而最优夹捏配置高度依赖用户位置且需要持续调整，因此需要探索支持移动用户的PASS系统优化方案。

Method: 构建基站服务移动用户的PASS系统模型，将最大化用户平均和速率问题建模为联合优化波束赋形向量和夹捏点位置的优化问题，采用深度确定性策略梯度（DDPG）强化学习框架求解。

Result: 通过仿真验证了所提DDPG方法的有效性，并证明了实时可配置性对于PASS性能增益的关键重要性。

Conclusion: PASS的性能增益严重依赖于实时跟踪或预测用户轨迹的能力，DDPG方法能有效解决用户移动性带来的复杂优化挑战。

Original Abstract: Recently, the pinching antenna systems (PASS) have attracted significant attention due to their ability to exploit dynamically reconfigurable pinching points along waveguides for flexible signal transmission. However, existing work largely overlooks user mobility although the optimal pinching configuration is highly dependent on the user's location and must be continuously adjusted. In this work, we investigate a PASS-enabled system model in which a base station (BS) serves a mobile user. We formulate an optimization problem that aims to maximize the user's average sum rate over a predefined time horizon while satisfying quality-of-service (QoS) constraint. This objective is achieved by jointly optimizing the beamforming vector at the BS and the pinching locations along the waveguides. Nevertheless, the resulting problem is highly non-convex and challenging to solve using conventional optimization techniques due to the intricate coupling among variables. The difficulty is further exacerbated by environmental randomness arising from user mobility and a probabilistic blockage model. This reveals a key engineering challenge: the performance gains of PASS critically rely on the ability to track or predict user trajectories in real time. To address these challenges, we adopt a deep deterministic policy gradient (DDPG) approach within a reinforcement learning framework, which is well-suited for continuous state and action spaces. Finally, extensive simulations are conducted to validate the proposed approach and demonstrate the importance of real-time configurability.

中文摘要翻译: 近年来，夹捏天线系统（PASS）因其能够利用沿波导的动态可重构夹捏点实现灵活信号传输而受到广泛关注。然而，现有工作很大程度上忽略了用户移动性，尽管最优夹捏配置高度依赖于用户位置且必须持续调整。在本工作中，我们研究了一个支持PASS的系统模型，其中基站（BS）服务一个移动用户。我们制定了一个优化问题，旨在在满足服务质量（QoS）约束的同时，最大化用户在预定义时间范围内的平均和速率。该目标通过联合优化基站的波束赋形向量和沿波导的夹捏位置来实现。然而，由于变量之间复杂的耦合关系，所得问题高度非凸，难以使用传统优化技术求解。用户移动性和概率性阻塞模型带来的环境随机性进一步加剧了这一难度。这揭示了一个关键的工程挑战：PASS的性能增益严重依赖于实时跟踪或预测用户轨迹的能力。为了应对这些挑战，我们在强化学习框架内采用深度确定性策略梯度（DDPG）方法，该方法非常适合连续状态和动作空间。最后，通过大量仿真验证了所提方法，并展示了实时可配置性的重要性。

</details>


<div id='q-bio.NC'></div>

### [219] [Dynamical mechanisms of flexible phase-locking in cortical theta oscillators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08014)
*Yangyang Wang,Benjamin R. Pittman-Polletta*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文揭示了皮层振荡器通过多时间尺度抑制性电流之间的相互作用产生延迟Hopf现象，从而在远低于其固有频率的慢速节律下实现灵活锁相的通用动力机制，并以θ振荡器模型验证了该机制在言语分割中的作用。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 听觉皮层振荡器需要灵活地与跨越宽范围时间尺度的输入节律锁相，包括比其固有频率慢得多的节律，但实现这种灵活锁相的机制尚不清楚。

Method: 运用动力系统理论工具，在生物物理可实现的皮层θ振荡器模型中分析多时间尺度抑制性电流（快、中、超慢）的相互作用，通过延迟Hopf分岔（DHB）现象解释锁相频率范围的扩展。

Result: 1) 慢速和超慢速抑制过程的相互作用通过延迟Hopf现象产生长时间的后输入恢复延迟，大幅扩展了夹带发生的频率范围；2) θ时间尺度(4-8 Hz)的抑制电流I_m和δ时间尺度(1-4 Hz)的抑制性钾电流I_KSS对锁相灵活性至关重要；3) 超慢速I_KSS在无外力时作用微弱，但在外部强迫下被招募以支持锁相；4) 中等时间尺度的I_m通过延长超慢流形上的延迟恢复进一步扩展锁相范围。

Conclusion: 多时间尺度内在抑制性电流之间的协调可能是大脑支持对节律性输入灵活锁相的关键机制。

Original Abstract: Oscillatory activity in auditory cortex is thought to play a central role in auditory and speech processing by synchronizing neural rhythms to external acoustic features of the speech stream. To support this function, cortical oscillators must flexibly phase-lock to inputs spanning a wide range of timescales, including rhythms substantially slower than their intrinsic frequency. Here we identify a general dynamical mechanism by which intrinsic inhibitory currents operating on multiple timescales enable such flexible phase-locking. Using tools from dynamical systems theory, we show that interactions between slow and superslow inhibitory processes generate prolonged post-input recovery delays through delayed Hopf phenomena, thereby substantially expanding the frequency range over which entrainment can occur. We demonstrate this mechanisms in a biophysically grounded cortical theta oscillator model for speech segmentation. Specifically, we show that both a theta-timescale (4-8 Hz) inhibitory current $I_m$ and a slower delta-timescale (1-4 Hz) inhibitory potassium current $I_{\rm K_{SS}}$ are crucial for entrainment flexibility. Their interaction creates a three-timescale structure that gives rise to pronounced delay phenomena associated with a delayed Hopf bifurcation (DHB). Interestingly, the superslow $I_{\rm K_{SS}}$ and the associated DHB play little role in the unforced oscillatory dynamics, but are recruited to support phase locking under external forcing. Moreover, the intermediate-timescale current $I_m$, rather than being redundant, further expands the phase-locking range by prolonging delayed recovery along the superslow manifold. Together, these results suggest that coordination among intrinsic inhibitory currents operating on multiple timescales may represent a key mechanism supporting flexible phase locking to rhythmic inputs in the brain.

中文摘要翻译: 听觉皮层的振荡活动被认为通过将神经节律同步到语音流的外部声学特征，在听觉和言语处理中发挥核心作用。为支持这一功能，皮层振荡器必须灵活地与跨越宽范围时间尺度的输入锁相，包括远慢于其固有频率的节律。本文识别出一个通用的动力学机制，即作用于多时间尺度的内在抑制性电流能够实现这种灵活的锁相。运用动力系统理论工具，我们证明了慢速和超慢速抑制过程的相互作用通过延迟Hopf现象产生长时间的后输入恢复延迟，从而大幅扩展了夹带发生的频率范围。我们在一个用于言语分割的生物物理可实现的皮层θ振荡器模型中演示了这一机制。具体而言，我们证明了θ时间尺度(4-8 Hz)的抑制电流I_m和更慢的δ时间尺度(1-4 Hz)的抑制性钾电流I_KSS对锁相灵活性均至关重要。它们的相互作用产生了三时间尺度结构，引发了与延迟Hopf分岔(DHB)相关的显著延迟现象。有趣的是，超慢速的I_KSS和相关的DHB在无外力振荡动力学中作用甚微，但在外部强迫下被招募以支持锁相。此外，中等时间尺度的电流I_m并非冗余，而是通过延长超慢流形上的延迟恢复进一步扩展了锁相范围。综合来看，这些结果表明，作用于多时间尺度的内在抑制性电流之间的协调可能是大脑支持对节律性输入灵活锁相的关键机制。

</details>

### [220] [Multimodal synthesis of MRI and tabular data with diffusion in a joint latent space via cross-attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06699)
*Daniel Mensing,Jan Kapar,Jochen G. Hirsch,Matthias Günther,Horst Hahn,Marvin N. Wright*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出一种多模态潜空间扩散模型，通过交叉注意力机制在共享潜空间内联合生成体积MRI和表格化临床数据，实现多模态医学数据的一致生成，首次证明了在单一潜扩散框架中联合建模MRI与混合类型表格数据的可行性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有生成模型通常只处理单一模态数据，而临床实践中MRI图像与患者表格化数据（如年龄、性别等）天然共存且具有内在关联。缺乏能够同时生成这两种模态数据且保持其一致性的统一框架，限制了合成患者数据在数字孪生等医疗应用中的发展。

Method: 采用变分自编码器（VAE）将MRI和表格数据融合到共享潜空间中，然后通过交叉注意力机制在潜空间中进行扩散生成；使用独立的解码器分别重建MRI体积和表格数据，确保各模态的重建质量。

Result: 在德国国家队列（NAKO）超过10,000名参与者的数据上评估，生成的MRI体积具有解剖学合理性和与合成表格属性一致的身体组成；定量指标（Fr chet距离、精确率-召回率）证实高保真图像生成；表格模态生成优于CTGAN，与TVAE性能相当。

Conclusion: 该工作是首个在单一潜扩散框架中联合建模MRI和混合类型表格数据的研究，为生成一致的合成多模态患者数据提供了概念验证，并推动了医疗领域数字孪生的发展。

Original Abstract: We propose a multimodal latent diffusion model that jointly synthesizes volumetric magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) and tabular clinical data within a shared latent space via cross-attention. This approach enables coherent joint representation learning of MRI and tabular modalities for generative modeling. Our model utilizes a variational autoencoder to fuse the two modalities before diffusion-based synthesis, allowing modality-appropriate reconstruction with separate decoders for MRI and tabular data. We evaluated the framework on data from the German National Cohort (NAKO Gesundheitsstudie), comprising over 10,000 participants with MRI scans and clinical tabular features such as age, sex, body measurements, and ethnicity. The generated MRI volumes exhibited anatomical plausibility and body composition consistent with the synthesized tabular attributes. Quantitative evaluation using Fréchet distance and precision-recall metrics confirmed high-fidelity image generation. In the tabular modality, our model outperformed CTGAN across standard evaluation metrics and achieved results comparable to TVAE, demonstrating competitive performance relative to established unimodal baselines. This work is, to our knowledge, the first to demonstrate the feasibility of jointly modeling MRI and mixed-type tabular data in a single latent diffusion framework, offering a proof-of-concept for generating coherent synthetic multimodal patient data and aligning with the broader goal of developing digital twins in healthcare.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了一种多模态潜扩散模型，通过交叉注意力机制在共享潜空间内联合合成体积磁共振成像（MRI）和表格化临床数据。该方法实现了MRI与表格模态在生成建模中的连贯联合表示学习。我们的模型利用变分自编码器在基于扩散的合成之前融合两种模态，并通过独立的MRI解码器和表格数据解码器实现模态适配的重建。我们在德国国家队列（NAKO Gesundheitsstudie）的数据上评估了该框架，涉及超过10,000名参与者的MRI扫描和临床表格特征（如年龄、性别、身体测量数据和种族）。生成的MRI体积表现出解剖学合理性，且身体组成与合成的表格属性一致。使用Fr​​chet距离和精确率-召回率指标的定量评估证实了高保真图像生成。在表格模态方面，我们的模型在标准评估指标上优于CTGAN，并达到了与TVAE相当的结果，展现出与已建立单模态基线相媲美的竞争性能。据我们所知，这项工作是首次证明在单一潜扩散框架中联合建模MRI与混合类型表格数据的可行性，为生成一致性的合成多模态患者数据提供了概念验证，并与开发医疗数字孪生的更广泛目标相一致。

</details>
